Düþünüyor
Inspecting zip dosyasý ve yapý hazýrlýðý
bash -lc set -e
ls -lah /mnt/data | sed -n '1,120p'
rm -rf /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work
mkdir -p /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
unzip -q '/mnt/data/uxb(02.07)(1).zip' -d /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
find /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base -maxdepth 3 -type d | sed -n '1,200p'

Gözden geçirme ve analiz için dosyalarý belirleme
Yapýlandýrma betiklerini ve ABI'yi incelemem gerek. Muhtemelen mevcut raporlarý kullanabiliriz, ama gerçek kaynaða da bakmalýyým. Önce önemli dosyalarý listelemeliyim.

Anahtar dosyalarý listeleme
bash -lc set -e
cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
printf '\n== root files ==\n'; find . -maxdepth 1 -type f -printf '%f\n' | sort
printf '\n== build scripts ==\n'; find . -type f \( -iname '*.bat' -o -iname '*.ps1' -o -iname '*.cmd' -o -iname 'CMakeLists.txt' -o -iname 'Makefile' \) | sort | sed -n '1,240p'
printf '\n== libsx files ==\n'; find libsx -maxdepth 2 -type f | sort | sed -n '1,260p'
printf '\n== runtime_ext files ==\n'; find runtime_ext -maxdepth 2 -type f | sort | sed -n '1,300p'
printf '\n== include modules ==\n'; find include/modules -maxdepth 1 -type f -printf '%f\n' | sort
printf '\n== runtime native ==\n'; find src/runtime/native -maxdepth 3 -type f | sort | sed -n '1,220p'

Yapýlandýrma düzenlemesi ve dosya taþýmalarý üzerine düþünme
Betiklere göz atmam gerek, özellikle build_all_runtime_ext_dlls.bat ve benzeri dosyalara. Amaç, mevcut yapýyý koruyarak deðer deðiþikliklerini yansýtýrken, dosya düzenlemesi ve yeni yapý eklemeleri yapmak. Eski dosyalarýn üzerine yazmaktan kaçýnmalý, belki uyumlu sembolik linkler kullanýlabilir mi? Windows'ta sembolik linkler yok, o yüzden çýktýlarý düzenlemeyle çözebilirim.

Yama iyileþtirilmesi ve modüller üzerindeki revizyon
Yamanýn geçerli yapýsýný iyileþtirmem gerekir. Özellikle libsx/runtime_ext/include/modules referanslarýný incelemem iyi olacaktýr. Büyük ihtimalle bu bölümler üzerinde arama yaparak daha net bir analiz elde edebilirim. Bu, mevcut yapýyý optimize etmek için önemli bir adým olabilir.

Arama ile baðlantý kontrolü
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
printf 'refs libsx: '; rg -l 'libsx[/\\]' . --glob '!reports/**' --glob '!docs/**' | wc -l
printf 'refs runtime_ext: '; rg -l 'runtime_ext[/\\]' . --glob '!reports/**' --glob '!docs/**' | wc -l
printf 'refs include/modules: '; rg -l 'include[/\\]modules' . --glob '!reports/**' --glob '!docs/**' | wc -l
rg -n 'libsx[/\\]|runtime_ext[/\\]|include[/\\]modules' compiler scripts src tools 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1,240p'

Yamanýn kök yolu ve dosya yapýlandýrmasý incelemesi
Elimizde yeterli veri var. Belki zip içeriðinde üstteki uxb klasörü eksik; betikler, üst düzey UXBc kökünden yollarý varsayýyor. Unzipped sürümü doðrudan uxb köküne yerleþiyor olabilir. Kök relative bir yamaya ihtiyacým olabilir. Betik içeriðini detaylýca incelemem gerek.

Ýnceleme için script içeriklerinin çýkartýlmasý
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
for f in \
 compiler/scripts/build_all_runtime_ext_dlls.bat \
 compiler/scripts/build_imported_library_runtimes.bat \
 compiler/scripts/import_uxb_library_bundle.bat \
 compiler/scripts/audit_uxb_library_bundle.bat \
 compiler/scripts/run_adim29_all_runtime_ext_dll_doctor.ps1 \
 runtime_ext/uxmath/build_uxmath_dll.bat \
 runtime_ext/uxmathcore/build_uxmathcore_dll.bat \
 runtime_ext/uxstats/build_uxstats_dll.bat \
 runtime_ext/uxcollections/build_uxcollections_dll.bat; do
 echo "===== $f ====="; sed -n '1,260p' "$f"; echo; done
===== compiler/scripts/build_all_runtime_ext_dlls.bat =====
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0..\..\.."

echo === ADIM29 build all runtime_ext DLLs ===

echo === FP runtime ===
call uxb\runtime_ext\build_fp_runtime_all.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

echo === Core libraries ===
call uxb\runtime_ext\uxmathcore\build_uxmathcore_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxmath\build_uxmath_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxstats\build_uxstats_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxstats2\build_uxstats2_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxdataset\build_uxdataset_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

echo === Optional/fallback-capable libraries ===
call uxb\runtime_ext\uxaimath\build_uxaimath_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 call uxb\runtime_ext\uxaimath\build_uxaimath_fallback_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxmatrix\build_uxmatrix_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 call uxb\runtime_ext\uxmatrix\build_uxmatrix_fallback_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxgraph\build_uxgraph_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 call uxb\runtime_ext\uxgraph\build_uxgraph_fallback_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxnn\build_uxnn_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 call uxb\runtime_ext\uxnn\build_uxnn_fallback_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxtensor\build_uxtensor_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 call uxb\runtime_ext\uxtensor\build_uxtensor_fallback_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :fail

echo === Optional direct libraries ===
call uxb\runtime_ext\uxcollections\build_uxcollections_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxdataframe\build_uxdataframe_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxllama\build_uxllama_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxnn2\build_uxnn2_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxnn3\build_uxnn3_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxonnx\build_uxonnx_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

call uxb\runtime_ext\uxonnx2\build_uxonnx2_dll.bat
if errorlevel 1 goto :warn_optional

echo OK: ADIM29 build all runtime_ext DLLs completed.
exit /b 0

:warn_optional
echo WARN: Optional runtime DLL build failed. Run doctor for exact matrix:
echo   powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File uxb\compiler\scripts\run_adim29_all_runtime_ext_dll_doctor.ps1 -NoRepair -StrictAll
exit /b 0

:fail
echo ERROR: Required runtime DLL build failed.
exit /b 1

===== compiler/scripts/run_adim29_all_runtime_ext_dll_doctor.ps1 =====
param(
    [switch]$NoRepair,
    [switch]$Build,
    [switch]$StrictAll
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

function Find-RepoRoot {
    $p = Get-Location
    while ($p) {
        if ((Test-Path (Join-Path $p "uxb/src/main.bas")) -and (Test-Path (Join-Path $p "uxb/runtime_ext"))) {
            return $p
        }
        $parent = Split-Path $p -Parent
        if ($parent -eq $p) { break }
        $p = $parent
    }
    throw "Repo root bulunamadi: uxb/src/main.bas + uxb/runtime_ext yok."
}

function CsvEscape([string]$s) {
    if ($null -eq $s) { $s = "" }
    $s = $s.Replace('"','""')
    return '"' + $s + '"'
}

$repo = Find-RepoRoot
Set-Location $repo

$current = "uxb/reports/control/current"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $current | Out-Null

$outDir = "uxb/dist/runtime_ext"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $outDir | Out-Null

$items = @(
    @{dll="uxb_fp80.dll";      family="F80";             source="uxb/runtime_ext/fp80/uxb_fp80_ld.c";              build="uxb/runtime_ext/fp80/build_fp80_dll.bat";                 fallback=""; tier="FP"; deps="gcc,quadmath"},
    @{dll="uxb_fp128.dll";     family="F128";            source="uxb/runtime_ext/fp128/uxb_fp128_quad.c";          build="uxb/runtime_ext/fp128/build_fp128_dll.bat";              fallback=""; tier="FP"; deps="gcc,quadmath"},
    @{dll="uxb_bigfp.dll";     family="BIGF_BIGD_BALL";  source="uxb/runtime_ext/bigfp/uxb_bigfp_mpfr.c";          build="uxb/runtime_ext/bigfp/build_bigfp_dll.bat";              fallback=""; tier="FP"; deps="gcc,mpfr,gmp"},

    @{dll="uxmathcore.dll";    family="MATHCORE";        source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmathcore/uxmathcore.c";         build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmathcore/build_uxmathcore_dll.bat";    fallback=""; tier="CORE"; deps="gcc,libm"},
    @{dll="uxmath.dll";        family="MATH";            source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmath/uxmath.c";                 build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmath/build_uxmath_dll.bat";            fallback=""; tier="CORE"; deps="gcc,libm"},
    @{dll="uxstats.dll";       family="STATS";           source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxstats/uxstats.c";               build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxstats/build_uxstats_dll.bat";          fallback=""; tier="CORE"; deps="gcc,libm"},
    @{dll="uxstats2.dll";      family="STATS2";          source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxstats2/uxstats2.c";             build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxstats2/build_uxstats2_dll.bat";        fallback=""; tier="CORE"; deps="gcc,libm"},
    @{dll="uxdataset.dll";     family="DATASET";         source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxdataset/uxdataset.c";           build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxdataset/build_uxdataset_dll.bat";      fallback=""; tier="CORE"; deps="gcc,libm"},

    @{dll="uxaimath.dll";      family="AIMATH";          source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxaimath/uxaimath.c";             build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxaimath/build_uxaimath_dll.bat";        fallback="uxb/runtime_ext/uxaimath/build_uxaimath_fallback_dll.bat"; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,openblas"},
    @{dll="uxcollections.dll"; family="COLLECTIONS";     source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxcollections/uxcollections_glib.c"; build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxcollections/build_uxcollections_dll.bat"; fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,glib,pkgconf"},
    @{dll="uxdataframe.dll";   family="DATAFRAME";       source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxdataframe/uxdataframe_duckdb.c"; build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxdataframe/build_uxdataframe_dll.bat"; fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,duckdb"},
    @{dll="uxgraph.dll";       family="GRAPH";           source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxgraph/uxgraph_igraph.c";        build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxgraph/build_uxgraph_dll.bat";          fallback="uxb/runtime_ext/uxgraph/build_uxgraph_fallback_dll.bat"; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,igraph,pkgconf"},
    @{dll="uxllama.dll";       family="LLAMA";           source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxllama/uxllama_cli_bridge.c";    build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxllama/build_uxllama_dll.bat";          fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL_HEAVY"; deps="gcc,llama.cpp"},
    @{dll="uxmatrix.dll";      family="MATRIX";          source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmatrix/uxmatrix_openblas.c";    build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmatrix/build_uxmatrix_dll.bat";        fallback="uxb/runtime_ext/uxmatrix/build_uxmatrix_fallback_dll.bat"; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,openblas,pkgconf"},
    @{dll="uxnn.dll";          family="NN";              source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn/uxnn.c";                     build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn/build_uxnn_dll.bat";                fallback="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn/build_uxnn_fallback_dll.bat"; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,openblas"},
    @{dll="uxnn2.dll";         family="NN2";             source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn2/uxnn2.c";                   build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn2/build_uxnn2_dll.bat";              fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,libm"},
    @{dll="uxnn3.dll";         family="NN3";             source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn3/uxnn3.c";                   build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxnn3/build_uxnn3_dll.bat";              fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,libm"},
    @{dll="uxonnx.dll";        family="ONNX";            source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxonnx/uxonnx.c";                 build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxonnx/build_uxonnx_dll.bat";            fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL_HEAVY"; deps="gcc,onnxruntime"},
    @{dll="uxonnx2.dll";       family="ONNX2";           source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxonnx2/uxonnx2.c";               build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxonnx2/build_uxonnx2_dll.bat";          fallback=""; tier="OPTIONAL_HEAVY"; deps="gcc,onnxruntime"},
    @{dll="uxtensor.dll";      family="TENSOR";          source="uxb/runtime_ext/uxtensor/uxtensor.c";             build="uxb/runtime_ext/uxtensor/build_uxtensor_dll.bat";        fallback="uxb/runtime_ext/uxtensor/build_uxtensor_fallback_dll.bat"; tier="OPTIONAL"; deps="gcc,openblas"}
)

$rows = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
$failCount = 0
$requiredFailCount = 0
$buildAttempts = 0
$buildFailures = 0

foreach ($it in $items) {
    $expected = Join-Path $outDir $it.dll
    $status = ""
    $action = ""
    $buildExit = ""
    $usedScript = ""

    if (Test-Path $expected) {
        $status = "DLL_EXISTS"
        $action = "NO_BUILD_NEEDED"
    } elseif (!(Test-Path $it.source)) {
        $status = "SOURCE_MISSING"
        $action = "SOURCE_REQUIRED"
    } elseif (!(Test-Path $it.build)) {
        $status = "BUILD_SCRIPT_MISSING"
        $action = "BUILD_SCRIPT_REQUIRED"
    } elseif ($Build) {
        $buildAttempts++
        $usedScript = $it.build
        cmd /c $it.build | Out-File -FilePath (Join-Path $current ("adim29_build_" + $it.dll + ".log")) -Encoding utf8
        $buildExit = $LASTEXITCODE
        if ($buildExit -ne 0 -and $it.fallback -and (Test-Path $it.fallback)) {
            $usedScript = $it.fallback
            cmd /c $it.fallback | Out-File -FilePath (Join-Path $current ("adim29_build_" + $it.dll + "_fallback.log")) -Encoding utf8
            $buildExit = $LASTEXITCODE
        }
        if ((Test-Path $expected) -and $buildExit -eq 0) {
            $status = "BUILT"
            $action = "OK"
        } else {
            $status = "BUILD_FAILED"
            $action = "CHECK_LOG_AND_DEPS"
            $buildFailures++
        }
    } else {
        $status = "DLL_MISSING"
        $action = "RUN_WITH_BUILD_OR_BUILD_SCRIPT"
    }

    $isFail = 0
    if ($status -in @("SOURCE_MISSING","BUILD_SCRIPT_MISSING","BUILD_FAILED")) { $isFail = 1 }
    if ($StrictAll -and $status -eq "DLL_MISSING") { $isFail = 1 }
    if ($isFail) {
        $failCount++
        if ($it.tier -in @("FP","CORE")) { $requiredFailCount++ }
    }

    $rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{
        dll = $it.dll
        family = $it.family
        tier = $it.tier
        expected_path = $expected
        source = $it.source
        build_script = $it.build
        fallback_script = $it.fallback
        deps = $it.deps
        status = $status
        action = $action
        build_exit_code = "$buildExit"
        used_script = "$usedScript"
    })
}

$csvPath = Join-Path $current "adim29_runtime_ext_dll_matrix.csv"
$headers = @("dll","family","tier","expected_path","source","build_script","fallback_script","deps","status","action","build_exit_code","used_script")
$outLines = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
$outLines.Add(($headers | ForEach-Object { CsvEscape $_ }) -join ",")
foreach ($r in $rows) {
    $outLines.Add(($headers | ForEach-Object { CsvEscape ([string]$r.$_) }) -join ",")
}
$outLines | Set-Content -Encoding utf8 $csvPath

$ok = $true
if ($requiredFailCount -gt 0) { $ok = $false }
if ($StrictAll -and $failCount -gt 0) { $ok = $false }
if ($Build -and $buildFailures -gt 0) { $ok = $false }

$gate = [ordered]@{
    step = "ADIM29_ALL_RUNTIME_EXT_DLL_DOCTOR"
    ok = $ok
    fail_count = $failCount
    required_fail_count = $requiredFailCount
    build_attempts = $buildAttempts
    build_failures = $buildFailures
    matrix = "uxb/reports/control/current/adim29_runtime_ext_dll_matrix.csv"
    runtime_dir = "uxb/dist/runtime_ext"
    note = "Sahte DLL uretmez. -Build verilirse mevcut build scriptleri cagrilir. -StrictAll butun eksikleri fail sayar."
}
$gatePath = Join-Path $current "adim29_runtime_ext_dll_gate.json"
($gate | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5) | Set-Content -Encoding utf8 $gatePath

$mdPath = Join-Path $current "adim29_runtime_ext_dll_gate.md"
if ($ok) {
    "# ADIM29 PASS`n" | Set-Content -Encoding utf8 $mdPath
} else {
    "# ADIM29 FAIL`n`nSee matrix: $csvPath`n" | Set-Content -Encoding utf8 $mdPath
}

Write-Host "ADIM29 matrix: $csvPath"
Write-Host "ADIM29 gate: $gatePath"
if ($ok) { exit 0 } else { exit 1 }

===== runtime_ext/uxmath/build_uxmath_dll.bat =====
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0..\..\.."
set "GCC=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\gcc.exe"
set "OUTDIR=uxb\dist\runtime_ext"
set "OUTDLL=%OUTDIR%\uxmath.dll"
if not exist "%GCC%" (
  echo ERROR: MSYS2 UCRT64 gcc not found: %GCC%
  echo Install MSYS2 UCRT64 and run:
  echo   pacman -S mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-gcc
  exit /b 2
)
if not exist "%OUTDIR%" mkdir "%OUTDIR%"
"%GCC%" -O2 -shared -o "%OUTDLL%" "uxb\runtime_ext\uxmath\uxmath.c" -lm
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo ERROR: uxmath.dll build failed.
  exit /b 1
)
echo OK: %OUTDLL%
endlocal

===== runtime_ext/uxstats/build_uxstats_dll.bat =====

set "OUTDIR=uxb\dist\runtime_ext"
set "OUTDLL=%OUTDIR%\uxstats.dll"
if not exist "%GCC%" (
  echo ERROR: MSYS2 UCRT64 gcc not found: %GCC%

===== runtime_ext/uxcollections/build_uxcollections_dll.bat =====
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0..\..\.."
set "GCC=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\gcc.exe"
set "OUTDIR=uxb\dist\runtime_ext"
set "OUTDLL=%OUTDIR%\uxcollections.dll"
if not exist "%GCC%" (
  echo ERROR: MSYS2 UCRT64 gcc not found: %GCC%
  echo Install MSYS2 UCRT64 and run:
  echo   pacman -S mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-gcc mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-glib2 pkgconf
  exit /b 2
)
if not exist "%OUTDIR%" mkdir "%OUTDIR%"
for /f "usebackq tokens=*" %%I in (`C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\pkg-config.exe --cflags --libs glib-2.0`) do set "GLIBFLAGS=%%I"
"%GCC%" -O2 -shared -o "%OUTDLL%" "uxb\runtime_ext\uxcollections\uxcollections_glib.c" %GLIBFLAGS%
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo ERROR: uxcollections.dll build failed. Check GLib package and pkg-config.
  exit /b 1
)
echo OK: %OUTDLL%
endlocal

FFI ayýklayýcý ve wrapper incelemesi
Devam etmem gerekiyor. FFI ayýklayýcýsýnýn detaylarýný ve wrapperlarý incelemeliyim. Bu adým, mevcut yapýyý daha iyi anlamamý ve iyileþtirmemi saðlayacak.

Ýnceleme ve FFI analiz sonuçlarý
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
printf '\n== CALL DLL/API parser and ABI references ==\n'
rg -n 'CALL\(DLL\)|CALL\(API\)|signature must|invalid calling convention|MAX.*ARG|argCount|callArg|ffi.*arg|parameter' src/runtime src/parser src/interpreter src/semantic src/mir | sed -n '1,320p'
printf '\n== wrapper samples ==\n'
for f in libsx/uxmath/uxmath.bas libsx/uxmathcore/uxmathcore.bas libsx/uxstats/uxstats.bas libsx/uxcollections/uxcollections.bas libsx/uxdataset/uxdataset.bas; do echo "===== $f ====="; sed -n '1,260p' "$f"; done
printf '\n== header samples ==\n'
for f in include/modules/uxmath.uxmh include/modules/uxcollections.uxmh include/modules/uxstats.uxmh; do echo "===== $f ====="; cat "$f"; done

Parser ve FFI detaylarýný inceleme
Çýktý ellipsize edildi, bu yüzden özel ayýklayýcýyý ve FFI çaðrýlarýný gözden geçirmeliyim. Wrappers muhtemelen eski CALL sözdizimi ile yazýlmýþ. Kullanýcý, 8 veya 10 parametreli opsiyonel sistem desteðinde kaynak koduna ayar yapýlacaðýný belirtti. Ancak ayýklayýcý þu an yalnýzca 6 parametreyi desteklediðinden, tam iþlevi incelemem gerekiyor.

Ýnceleme ve ABI imzasý deðerlendirmesi
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
sed -n '240,380p' src/parser/parser/parser_stmt_dispatch.fbs
printf '\n== parser ffi api ==\n'; sed -n '1,240p' src/parser/parser/parser_adim08_call_ffi_api.fbs
printf '\n== x64 invoke helpers ==\n'; sed -n '1,360p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_x64_invoke_helpers.fbs
printf '\n== ffi runtime files ==\n'; find src/runtime/ffi -type f -maxdepth 2 -print -exec sh -c 'echo ==== $1; sed -n "1,260p" "$1"' _ {} \;
End Function

#include once "parser_stmt_semantic_extra.fbs"

Private Function IsValidDllSignatureToken(ByRef signatureText As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = UCase(Trim(signatureText))

    Select Case s
    Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL"
        Return 1
    End Select

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function IsValidDllCallingConventionToken(ByRef conventionText As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = UCase(Trim(conventionText))

    Select Case s
    Case "CDECL", "STDCALL"
        Return 1
    End Select

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function IsAddressableByRefArgNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer) As Integer
    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then Return 0
    Return UCase(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind) = "IDENT"
End Function

Private Function ValidateCallDllSemanticsForNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNode As Integer) As Integer
    If UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(callNode).value)) <> "DLL" Then Return 1

    Dim argCount As Integer
    Dim argIdx(5) As Integer
    argIdx(0) = -1
    argIdx(1) = -1
    argIdx(2) = -1
    argIdx(3) = -1
    argIdx(4) = -1
    argIdx(5) = -1

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(callNode).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        If argCount <= 5 Then argIdx(argCount) = ch
        argCount += 1
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop

    If argCount < 4 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): expects at least 4 arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(0)).kind) <> "STRING" Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): library must be string literal"
        Return 0
    End If

    If Len(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(0)).value)) = 0 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): library cannot be empty"
        Return 0
    End If

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(1)).kind) <> "STRING" Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): symbol must be string literal"
        Return 0
    End If

    If Len(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(1)).value)) = 0 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): symbol cannot be empty"
        Return 0
    End If

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(2)).kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): signature token must be a keyword"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim signatureText As String
    signatureText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(2)).value))
    If IsValidDllSignatureToken(signatureText) = 0 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): invalid signature token: " & signatureText
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argStartPos As Integer
    argStartPos = 3

    If argCount >= 5 Then
        If argIdx(3) <> -1 And UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(3)).kind) = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
            Dim conventionText As String
            conventionText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(3)).value))
            If IsValidDllCallingConventionToken(conventionText) = 0 Then
                ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): invalid calling convention token: " & conventionText
                Return 0
            End If
            argStartPos = 4
        End If
    End If

    If signatureText = "BYREF" Then
        If argStartPos > 5 Or argIdx(argStartPos) = -1 Then
            ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): BYREF requires addressable target argument"
            Return 0
        End If

        If IsAddressableByRefArgNode(ps, argIdx(argStartPos)) = 0 Then
            ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): BYREF requires addressable target argument"
            Return 0
        End If
    End If

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ValidateCallDllSemanticsNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer) As Integer
    If nodeIdx = -1 Then Return 1

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind) = "CALL_STMT" Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind) = "CALL_EXPR" Then
        If ValidateCallDllSemanticsForNode(ps, nodeIdx) = 0 Then Return 0
    End If

    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left <> -1 Then
        If ValidateCallDllSemanticsNode(ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left) = 0 Then Return 0
    End If

    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right <> -1 Then
        If ValidateCallDllSemanticsNode(ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right) = 0 Then Return 0
    End If

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        If ValidateCallDllSemanticsNode(ps, ch) = 0 Then Return 0
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop

== parser ffi api ==
#ifndef UXB_PARSER_ADIM08_CALL_FFI_API_FBS
#define UXB_PARSER_ADIM08_CALL_FFI_API_FBS

Function UXBParserIsInteropCallName(ByRef callName As String) As Integer
    Dim n As String
    n = UCase(Trim(callName))
    If n = "DLL" Or n = "API" Then Return 1
    Return 0
End Function

Function UXBParserNormalizeCallInteropNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNode As Integer, ByRef firstTok As Token) As Integer
    If callNode < 0 Or callNode >= ps.ast.count Then Return 0
    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(callNode).kind) <> "CALL_STMT" Then Return 1

    Dim callName As String
    callName = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(callNode).value))
    If UXBParserIsInteropCallName(callName) = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim argCount As Integer
    argCount = ASTPoolChildCount(ps.ast, callNode)
    If argCount < 2 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(" & callName & ",...): library and symbol arguments are required"
        Return 0
    End If

    If callName = "DLL" Then
        ps.ast.nodes(callNode).kind = "CALL_DLL_STMT"
        ps.ast.nodes(callNode).op = "DLL"
    Else
        ps.ast.nodes(callNode).kind = "CALL_API_STMT"
        ps.ast.nodes(callNode).op = "API"
    End If

    ps.ast.nodes(callNode).symbolName = callName
    ps.ast.nodes(callNode).sourceFile = firstTok.sourceFile
    If ps.ast.nodes(callNode).sourceLength <= 0 Then ps.ast.nodes(callNode).sourceLength = firstTok.lexemeLength

    Dim ch As Integer
    Dim argPos As Integer
    argPos = 0
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(callNode).firstChild
    Do While ch <> AST_NULL
        Select Case argPos
        Case 0
            ps.ast.nodes(ch).role = "LIBRARY"
        Case 1
            ps.ast.nodes(ch).role = "SYMBOL"
        Case 2
            ps.ast.nodes(ch).role = "SIGNATURE"
        Case Else
            ps.ast.nodes(ch).role = "ARG"
        End Select
        argPos += 1
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop

    Return 1
End Function

#endif

== x64 invoke helpers ==
Private Function ExecX64EvalArgs(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef sig As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, argValuesI32() As Integer, argValuesU64() As ULongInt, argValuesF64() As Double, argValuesStr() As String, argValuesStrPtr() As ZString Ptr, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim ai As Integer
    For ai = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos + ai)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: x64 invoke argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If

        Select Case sig
        Case "I32"
            argValuesI32(ai) = ExecEvalNode(ps, es, argNode, errText)
            If errText <> "" Then
                errText = "exec: x64 invoke argument eval failed: " & errText
                Return 0
            End If

        Case "F64"
            Dim argValue As ExecValue
            If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, argValue, errText) = 0 Then
                errText = "exec: x64 invoke argument eval failed: " & errText
                Return 0
            End If
            If argValue.valueType = "STRING" Then
                errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) F64 requires numeric argument"
                Return 0
            End If
            If argValue.valueType = "DOUBLE" Then
                argValuesF64(ai) = argValue.floatValue
            Else
                argValuesF64(ai) = CDbl(argValue.intValue)
            End If

        Case "STRPTR"
            If ExecEvalNodeAsText(ps, es, argNode, argValuesStr(ai), errText) = 0 Then
                errText = "exec: x64 invoke argument eval failed: " & errText
                Return 0
            End If
            argValuesStrPtr(ai) = StrPtr(argValuesStr(ai))

        Case Else
            argValuesU64(ai) = CULngInt(ExecEvalNode(ps, es, argNode, errText))
            If errText <> "" Then
                errText = "exec: x64 invoke argument eval failed: " & errText
                Return 0
            End If
        End Select
    Next ai

    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub ExecX64DispatchInvoke(ByVal procAddr As Any Ptr, ByRef sig As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, argValuesI32() As Integer, argValuesU64() As ULongInt, argValuesF64() As Double, argValuesStrPtr() As ZString Ptr, ByRef valueOut As Integer)
    Select Case sig
    Case "I32"
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fnI320 As ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32
            fnI320 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnI320()
        Case 1
            Dim fnI321 As ExecFnArg1CdeclI32
            fnI321 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnI321(argValuesI32(0))
        Case 2
            Dim fnI322 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32
            fnI322 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnI322(argValuesI32(0), argValuesI32(1))
        Case 3
            Dim fnI323 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32
            fnI323 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnI323(argValuesI32(0), argValuesI32(1), argValuesI32(2))
        Case 4
            Dim fnI324 As ExecFnArg4CdeclI32
            fnI324 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnI324(argValuesI32(0), argValuesI32(1), argValuesI32(2), argValuesI32(3))
        End Select

    Case "U64", "PTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL"
        Dim rvU64 As ULongInt
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fnU640 As ExecFnNoArgCdeclU64
            fnU640 = procAddr
            rvU64 = fnU640()
        Case 1
            Dim fnU641 As ExecFnArg1CdeclU64
            fnU641 = procAddr

Private Function ExecX64DispatchMixedInvoke(ByVal procAddr As Any Ptr, ByRef retType As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByVal argMask As Integer, argValuesU64() As ULongInt, argValuesF64() As Double, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim retClass As String
    retClass = UCase(Trim(retType))
    If retClass = "PTR" Or retClass = "STRPTR" Or retClass = "BYREF" Or retClass = "BYVAL" Or retClass = "VOID" Then retClass = "U64"

    Select Case retClass
    Case "I32"
        Dim rvI32 As Integer
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fn0I32 As ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32
            fn0I32 = procAddr
            rvI32 = fn0I32()
        Case 1
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_I32_1_0 As ExecFnArg1CdeclI32_U
                fn_I32_1_0 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_1_0(argValuesU64(0))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_I32_1_1 As ExecFnArg1CdeclI32_F
                fn_I32_1_1 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_1_1(argValuesF64(0))
            Case Else
                errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 mixed invoke unsupported argument mask"
                Return 0
            End Select
        Case 2
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_I32_2_0 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32_UU
                fn_I32_2_0 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_2_0(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_I32_2_1 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32_FU
                fn_I32_2_1 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_2_1(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1))
            Case 2
                Dim fn_I32_2_2 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32_UF
                fn_I32_2_2 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_2_2(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1))
            Case 3
                Dim fn_I32_2_3 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32_FF
                fn_I32_2_3 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_2_3(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1))
            Case Else
                errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 mixed invoke unsupported argument mask"
                Return 0
            End Select
        Case 3
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_I32_3_0 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_UUU
                fn_I32_3_0 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_0(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_I32_3_1 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_FUU
                fn_I32_3_1 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_1(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 2
                Dim fn_I32_3_2 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_UFU
                fn_I32_3_2 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_2(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 3
                Dim fn_I32_3_3 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_FFU
                fn_I32_3_3 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_3(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 4
                Dim fn_I32_3_4 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_UUF
                fn_I32_3_4 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_4(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case 5
                Dim fn_I32_3_5 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_FUF
                fn_I32_3_5 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_5(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case 6
                Dim fn_I32_3_6 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_UFF
                fn_I32_3_6 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_6(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case 7
                Dim fn_I32_3_7 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32_FFF
                fn_I32_3_7 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_3_7(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case Else
                errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 mixed invoke unsupported argument mask"
                Return 0
            End Select
        Case 4
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_I32_4_0 As ExecFnArg4CdeclI32_UUUU
                fn_I32_4_0 = procAddr
                rvI32 = fn_I32_4_0(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesU64(3))

== ffi runtime files ==
src/runtime/ffi/runtime_ffi.fbs
==== src/runtime/ffi/runtime_ffi.fbs
#ifndef UXB_RUNTIME_FFI_FBS
#define UXB_RUNTIME_FFI_FBS

#include once "../runtime_context.fbs"
#include once "../services/runtime_service_ids.fbs"

Function UXBRuntimeFfiDispatch(ByRef rt As UXBRuntimeContext, ByVal serviceId As String, ByRef args As UXBRuntimeArgList) As UXBInterpValue
    Dim targetText As String = UCase(Trim(rt.targetName))
    If targetText <> "NATIVE" And targetText <> "X64" And targetText <> "X86" Then
        Return UXBRuntimeSetError(rt, UXB_RT_ERR_NATIVE_ONLY, "FFI service requires native target: " & serviceId)
    End If
    Select Case UCase(Trim(serviceId))
    Case RT_DLL_LOAD
        Dim dllName As String = UXBRuntimeArgText(args, 0)
        Dim hid As Integer = UXBRuntimeAllocHandle(rt, "DLL", dllName)
        Return UXBInterpValueHandle("DLL_HANDLE", hid)
    Case RT_DLL_CALL, RT_API_CALL, RT_INLINE_NATIVE
        Return UXBRuntimeSetError(rt, UXB_RT_ERR_FFI, "FFI execution must use native backend bridge: " & serviceId)
    End Select
    Return UXBRuntimeSetError(rt, UXB_RT_ERR_SERVICE_NOT_FOUND, "FFI service not found: " & serviceId)
End Function

#endif
FFI dosyalarýný ve maksimum argümanlarý inceleme
FFI yürütme dosyalarýný tam olarak incelemem gerekiyor çünkü çýktý oldukça büyük, ancak en fazla argüman sayýsýna odaklanmalýyým. "argCount >" þeklinde bir arama yaparak daha fazla bilgi edinmeyi planlýyorum. Bu, sorunlarý çözmek ve gereksinimleri karþýlamak için önemli olabilir.

FFI dosya incelemesi ve argüman arama

src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_x64_invoke_helpers.fbs:719:            Case 15
Fonksiyon imzalarýný ve eski/yeni sarmalayýcý desteðini inceliyorum
Eski ve yeni sarmalayýcýlarý desteklemek için, fonksiyonlarýn imza dizilerini nasýl ele aldýklarýný anlamam gerekiyor. Ýki farklý çaðrý yolu olabilir: eski yolu, tekdüze imza kullanýrken, geniþletilmiþ imza yolu ise farklý argüman tipleri kullanýyor gibi. Exec fonksiyonlarý etrafýnda arama yapýp kontrol edeceðim.

Kaynak incelemesi ve fonksiyon imzasý analizi
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
sed -n '480,820p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs
sed -n '820,1010p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs
printf '\n== ffi type definitions ==\n'; sed -n '1,260p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_types.fbs 2>/dev/null || true
rg -n '^Type ExecFn|ExecFnArg[0-9]+' src/runtime/exec | sed -n '1,260p'
End Sub

Private Function ExecResolveX86InteropTarget(ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef cleanupTypeOut As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    cleanupTypeOut = ""

    ExecLoadFfiX86ResolverIfNeeded
    If gFfiX86ResolverLoadFailed <> 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x86 resolver plan missing/invalid (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_RESOLVER_PLAN_MISSING)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim foundIdx As Integer
    foundIdx = ExecFindX86ResolverEntryIndex(dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, argCount)

    If foundIdx = -1 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x86 resolver no matching entry (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_RESOLVER_NO_MATCH)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    cleanupTypeOut = gFfiX86ResolverEntries(foundIdx).cleanupType

    Dim resolvedAddr As Any Ptr
    If ExecFindX86ResolvedProc(gFfiX86ResolverEntries(foundIdx).stubId, resolvedAddr) <> 0 Then
        Return 1
    End If

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x86 resolver LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x86 resolver GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    ExecAppendX86ResolvedProc gFfiX86ResolverEntries(foundIdx).stubId, procAddr
    ExecUpsertX86SymptrMap gFfiX86ResolverEntries(foundIdx).stubId, procAddr

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX86I32(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0

    If UCase(Trim(signatureText)) <> "I32" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 4 Then Return 1

    Dim resolverIdx As Integer
    resolverIdx = ExecFindX86ResolverEntryIndex(dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, argCount)
    If resolverIdx = -1 Then Return 1

    Dim stubId As Integer
    stubId = gFfiX86ResolverEntries(resolverIdx).stubId

    Dim symMapIdx As Integer
    symMapIdx = ExecFindX86SymptrMapIndex(stubId)
    If symMapIdx = -1 Or gFfiX86SymptrMapEntries(symMapIdx).procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: x86 symptr map entry missing for stub " & LTrim(Str(stubId))
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = gFfiX86SymptrMapEntries(symMapIdx).procAddr

    Dim argValues(0 To 3) As Integer
    Dim ai As Integer
    For ai = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos + ai)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: x86 invoke proof argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If

        argValues(ai) = ExecEvalNode(ps, es, argNode, errText)
        If errText <> "" Then
            errText = "exec: x86 invoke proof argument eval failed: " & errText
            Return 0
        End If
    Next ai

    Dim conv As String
    conv = UCase(Trim(callingConventionText))
    If conv = "" Then conv = "CDECL"

    If conv = "STDCALL" Then
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fnStd0 As ExecFnNoArgStdcallI32
            fnStd0 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd0()
        Case 1
            Dim fnStd1 As ExecFnArg1StdcallI32
            fnStd1 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd1(argValues(0))
        Case 2
            Dim fnStd2 As ExecFnArg2StdcallI32
            fnStd2 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd2(argValues(0), argValues(1))
        Case 3
            Dim fnStd3 As ExecFnArg3StdcallI32
            fnStd3 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd3(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2))
        Case 4
            Dim fnStd4 As ExecFnArg4StdcallI32
            fnStd4 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd4(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2), argValues(3))
        End Select
    Else
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fnC0 As ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32
            fnC0 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC0()
        Case 1
            Dim fnC1 As ExecFnArg1CdeclI32
            fnC1 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC1(argValues(0))
        Case 2
            Dim fnC2 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32
            fnC2 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC2(argValues(0), argValues(1))
        Case 3
            Dim fnC3 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32
            fnC3 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC3(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2))
        Case 4
            Dim fnC4 As ExecFnArg4CdeclI32
            fnC4 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC4(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2), argValues(3))
        End Select
    End If

    gFfiX86InvokeCount += 1
    gFfiX86LastInvokeStubId = stubId
    gFfiX86LastInvokeConv = conv
    If UCase(Trim(gFfiX86ResolverEntries(resolverIdx).cleanupType)) = "CALLER" Then
        gFfiX86CallerCleanupBytes += gFfiX86ResolverEntries(resolverIdx).argStackBytes
    Else
        gFfiX86CalleeCleanupBytes += gFfiX86ResolverEntries(resolverIdx).argStackBytes
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecTryInvokeDirectX86I32(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0

    If SizeOf(Any Ptr) <> 4 Then Return 1
    If UCase(Trim(signatureText)) <> "I32" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 4 Then Return 1

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x86 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x86 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argValues(0 To 3) As Integer
    Dim ai As Integer
    For ai = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos + ai)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: x86 direct invoke argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If

        argValues(ai) = ExecEvalNode(ps, es, argNode, errText)
        If errText <> "" Then
            errText = "exec: x86 direct invoke argument eval failed: " & errText
            Return 0
        End If
    Next ai

    Dim conv As String
    conv = UCase(Trim(callingConventionText))
    If conv = "" Then conv = "CDECL"

    If conv = "STDCALL" Then
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fnStd0 As ExecFnNoArgStdcallI32
            fnStd0 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd0()
        Case 1
            Dim fnStd1 As ExecFnArg1StdcallI32
            fnStd1 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd1(argValues(0))
        Case 2
            Dim fnStd2 As ExecFnArg2StdcallI32
            fnStd2 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd2(argValues(0), argValues(1))
        Case 3
            Dim fnStd3 As ExecFnArg3StdcallI32
            fnStd3 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd3(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2))
        Case 4
            Dim fnStd4 As ExecFnArg4StdcallI32
            fnStd4 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnStd4(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2), argValues(3))
        End Select
    Else
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fnC0 As ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32
            fnC0 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC0()
        Case 1
            Dim fnC1 As ExecFnArg1CdeclI32
            fnC1 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC1(argValues(0))
        Case 2
            Dim fnC2 As ExecFnArg2CdeclI32
            fnC2 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC2(argValues(0), argValues(1))
        Case 3
            Dim fnC3 As ExecFnArg3CdeclI32
            fnC3 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC3(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2))
        Case 4
            Dim fnC4 As ExecFnArg4CdeclI32
            fnC4 = procAddr
            valueOut = fnC4(argValues(0), argValues(1), argValues(2), argValues(3))
        End Select
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

#include once "exec_ffi_x64_invoke_helpers.fbs"

Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64I32(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0

    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() <> "WIN64-MSABI" Then Return 1

    Dim sig As String
    sig = UCase(Trim(signatureText))
    If sig <> "I32" And sig <> "U64" And sig <> "F64" And sig <> "PTR" And sig <> "STRPTR" And sig <> "BYREF" And sig <> "BYVAL" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 4 Then Return 1

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"

Patch tasarýmý ve çalýþma detaylarý
Bir yamanýn inþa edilmesi gerekiyor ama önce kaynak kodunu incelemem gerek. Linux üzerinde derleme hattý mevcut olmayabilir (FreeBasic yüklü deðil). Statik testler yapabileceðiz. Çalýþma zamanýnda 10 argüman desteklemek zor çünkü büyük bir kombinasyon söz konusu. Verilen parametrelere göre, her DLL için uyumlu köprüler tasarlamak daha verimli olabilir.

Fonksiyon wrapper'larýný ve parametreleri denetleme
Uniform çaðrýlarý 10 argümana kadar uzatabiliriz (I32/U64/PTR/STRPTR), ancak karýþýk F64 kullanmak istemiyoruz. Wrappers fonksiyonlarýna 4'ten fazla parametre ve karýþýk türler var. Wrapper'larý ve parametreleri audit edip, sabitler yerine string literaller kullanmalý mýyýz, ona karar vermeliyiz.
