Private Function ExecBuiltinArgAsTypeName(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef callName As String, ByRef typeNameOut As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim a0 As Integer
    a0 = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 0)
    If a0 = -1 Then
        errText = "exec: " & callName & " type arg missing"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim n As ASTNode
    n = ps.ast.nodes(a0)
    If UCase(n.kind) <> "IDENT" And UCase(n.kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
        errText = "exec: " & callName & " expects type name"
        Return 0
    End If

    typeNameOut = n.value
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(ByRef signatureText As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = UCase(Trim(signatureText))

    Select Case s
    Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL", "CDECL", "STDCALL"
        Return 1
    End Select

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecIsDllCallingConvention(ByRef conventionText As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = UCase(Trim(conventionText))

    Select Case s
    Case "CDECL", "STDCALL"
        Return 1
    End Select

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() As String
#Ifdef __FB_64BIT__
    Return "WIN64-MSABI"
#Else
    Return "X86-32"
#EndIf
End Function

Private Function ExecAreDllCallingConventionsCompatible(ByRef requestedConventionText As String, ByRef allowedConventionText As String) As Integer
    Dim req As String
    Dim allow As String
    req = UCase(Trim(requestedConventionText))
    allow = UCase(Trim(allowedConventionText))

    If req = "" Then req = "CDECL"
    If allow = "" Or allow = "*" Then Return 1
    If allow = req Then Return 1

#Ifdef __FB_64BIT__
    If (allow = "CDECL" Or allow = "STDCALL") And (req = "CDECL" Or req = "STDCALL") Then
        Return 1
    End If
#EndIf

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecContainsInvalidDllChars(ByRef dllName As String) As Integer
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 1 To Len(dllName)
        Dim ch As Integer
        ch = Asc(Mid(dllName, i, 1))

        If ch < 32 Then Return 1

        Select Case ch
        Case 34, 42, 59, 60, 62, 63, 124
            Return 1
        End Select
    Next i

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecIsAbsoluteDllPath(ByRef dllName As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = Trim(dllName)

    If s = "" Then Return 0
    If Left(s, 1) = "\" Or Left(s, 1) = "/" Then Return 1

    If Len(s) >= 2 Then
        Dim firstCh As Integer
        firstCh = Asc(UCase(Left(s, 1)))
        If firstCh >= Asc("A") And firstCh <= Asc("Z") Then
            If Mid(s, 2, 1) = ":" Then Return 1
        End If
    End If

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecHasDllPathSegments(ByRef dllName As String) As Integer
    If Instr(dllName, "..") > 0 Then Return 1
    If Instr(dllName, "\") > 0 Then Return 1
    If Instr(dllName, "/") > 0 Then Return 1
    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecNormalizeDllName(ByRef dllNameIn As String, ByRef dllNameOut As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    dllNameOut = Trim(dllNameIn)

    If dllNameOut = "" Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) library cannot be empty (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_LIB)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    If ExecIsAbsoluteDllPath(dllNameOut) <> 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) library must not be absolute path (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_ABSOLUTE_PATH)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    If ExecHasDllPathSegments(dllNameOut) <> 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) library path segments not allowed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_PATH_SEGMENTS)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    If ExecContainsInvalidDllChars(dllNameOut) <> 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) library contains invalid chars (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_INVALID_CHARS)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    dllNameOut = UCase(dllNameOut)
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecNormalizeFfiHashToken(ByRef hashIn As String, ByRef hashOut As String) As Integer
    hashOut = UCase(Trim(hashIn))
    If hashOut = "" Then Return 0
    If hashOut = "*" Then Return 1
    If Len(hashOut) <> 64 Then Return 0

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 1 To Len(hashOut)
        Dim ch As Integer
        ch = Asc(Mid(hashOut, i, 1))
        If (ch >= Asc("0") And ch <= Asc("9")) Or (ch >= Asc("A") And ch <= Asc("F")) Then
            ' valid
        Else
            Return 0
        End If
    Next i

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecNormalizeFfiSignerToken(ByRef signerIn As String, ByRef signerOut As String) As Integer
    signerOut = UCase(Trim(signerIn))
    If signerOut = "" Then Return 0
    If Instr(signerOut, "|") > 0 Then Return 0
    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub ExecFfiAllowlistAppend(ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByRef hashText As String, ByRef signerText As String)
    If gFfiAllowEntryCount = 0 Then
        ReDim gFfiAllowEntries(0)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount)
    End If

    gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount).dllName = UCase(Trim(dllName))
    gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount).symbolName = UCase(Trim(symbolName))
    gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount).signatureText = UCase(Trim(signatureText))
    gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount).callingConventionText = UCase(Trim(callingConventionText))
    gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount).hashText = UCase(Trim(hashText))
    gFfiAllowEntries(gFfiAllowEntryCount).signerText = UCase(Trim(signerText))
    gFfiAllowEntryCount += 1
End Sub

Private Sub ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist()
    Dim dll0 As String
    Dim symbol0 As String
    Dim sig0 As String
    Dim conv0 As String
    Dim hash0 As String
    Dim signer0 As String
    dll0 = "KERNEL32.DLL"
    symbol0 = "GETTICKCOUNT"
    sig0 = "I32"
    conv0 = "*"
    If FfiBuildTrustedWindowsDllAttestation(dll0, hash0, signer0) = 0 Then
        hash0 = "*"
        signer0 = "CN=MICROSOFT WINDOWS"
    End If
    ExecFfiAllowlistAppend dll0, symbol0, sig0, conv0, hash0, signer0

    dll0 = "KERNEL32.DLL"
    symbol0 = "SLEEP"
    sig0 = "I32"
    conv0 = "*"
    If FfiBuildTrustedWindowsDllAttestation(dll0, hash0, signer0) = 0 Then
Fonksiyon çaðrýlarýný analiz etme
Verimli bir þekilde ilerlemeliyiz. exec_eval_builtin_categories fonksiyonunu inceleyip, gerçek çaðrýlarýn nasýl parse edildiðini anlamam gerekiyor. Bu, çaðrý parametrelerinin doðru þekilde iþlenmesini saðlamaya yardýmcý olacak ve sonraki adýmlarýmýzý kolaylaþtýracaktýr.

Ýncelemede exec_eval_builtin_categories fonksiyonu
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
sed -n '150,310p' src/runtime/exec/exec_eval_builtin_categories.fbs
sed -n '760,960p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs

        If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL/API) invoke path unavailable signature=" & UCase(Trim(signatureText)) & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))
            Return 0
        End If
        UxbWarn "FFI invoke report-only fallback signature=" & UCase(Trim(signatureText)) & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))
        Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecEvalBuiltinFfiCategory(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callName As String, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim callKey As String
    callKey = UCase(Trim(callName))

    Select Case callKey
    Case "DLL", "CALL"
        Dim ffiArgCount As Integer
        ffiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)
        Dim ffiBasePos As Integer
        ffiBasePos = 0
        Dim callMarker As String
        callMarker = "DLL"
        If callKey = "CALL" Then
            Dim markerNode As Integer
            markerNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 0)
            If markerNode = -1 Then
                errText = "exec: unsupported call CALL"
                Return 0
            End If
            callMarker = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(markerNode).value))
            If callMarker <> "DLL" And callMarker <> "API" Then
                errText = "exec: unsupported call CALL"
                Return 0
            End If
            ffiBasePos = 1
        End If

        If ffiArgCount < ffiBasePos + 3 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) expects at least 4 arguments (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_ARITY)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim dllNode As Integer
        dllNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos)
        If dllNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(dllNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) library must be string literal (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_LIB)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim dllNameRaw As String
        dllNameRaw = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(dllNode).value)
        If callMarker = "API" And InStr(dllNameRaw, ".") = 0 Then dllNameRaw &= ".dll"

        Dim dllName As String
        If ExecNormalizeDllName(dllNameRaw, dllName, errText) = 0 Then
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim symbolNode As Integer
        symbolNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos + 1)
        If symbolNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(symbolNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) symbol must be string literal (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SYMBOL)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim symbolName As String
        symbolName = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(symbolNode).value)
        If symbolName = "" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) symbol cannot be empty (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SYMBOL)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim sigNode As Integer
        sigNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos + 2)
        If sigNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(sigNode).kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) signature token must be keyword (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SIGNATURE)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim signatureText As String
        signatureText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(sigNode).value))
        If ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(signatureText) = 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid signature token " & signatureText & " (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SIGNATURE)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim callingConventionText As String
        callingConventionText = "CDECL"

        Dim argStartPos As Integer
        argStartPos = ffiBasePos + 3

        If ffiArgCount >= ffiBasePos + 4 Then
            Dim convNode As Integer
            convNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos + 3)
            If convNode <> -1 And UCase(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).kind) = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
                Dim convCandidate As String
                convCandidate = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).value))
                If ExecIsDllCallingConvention(convCandidate) = 0 Then
                    errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid calling convention token " & convCandidate & " (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SIGNATURE)) & ")"
                    Return 0
                End If
                callingConventionText = convCandidate
                argStartPos = ffiBasePos + 4
            End If
        End If

        Return ExecEvalDllCallCore(ps, es, callNodeIdx, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, argStartPos, errText)
    Case "API"
        Dim apiArgCount As Integer
        apiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)
        If apiArgCount < 3 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) expects at least 3 arguments"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiLibNode As Integer
        apiLibNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 0)
        If apiLibNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(apiLibNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) library must be string literal"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiDllNameRaw As String
        apiDllNameRaw = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(apiLibNode).value)
        If apiDllNameRaw = "" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) library cannot be empty"
            Return 0
        End If

        If InStr(apiDllNameRaw, ".") = 0 Then apiDllNameRaw &= ".dll"

        Dim apiDllName As String
        If ExecNormalizeDllName(apiDllNameRaw, apiDllName, errText) = 0 Then
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSymbolNode As Integer
        apiSymbolNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 1)
        If apiSymbolNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(apiSymbolNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) symbol must be string literal"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSymbolName As String
        apiSymbolName = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(apiSymbolNode).value)
        If apiSymbolName = "" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) symbol cannot be empty"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSigNode As Integer
        apiSigNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 2)
        If apiSigNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(apiSigNode).kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) signature token must be keyword"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSignature As String
        apiSignature = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(apiSigNode).value))
        If ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(apiSignature) = 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) invalid signature token " & apiSignature
        Return 0
    End If

    ExecX64DispatchInvoke procAddr, sig, argCount, argValuesI32(), argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argValuesStrPtr(), valueOut

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(ByRef tokenText As String, ByRef typeOut As String) As Integer
    typeOut = UCase(Trim(tokenText))
    If typeOut = "BYVAL" Then typeOut = "VOID"
    If typeOut = "CDECL" Or typeOut = "STDCALL" Then Return 0
    Select Case typeOut
    Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF"
        Return 1
    End Select
    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecFfiParseArgTypeList(ByRef listText As String, argTypes() As String, ByRef argTypeCount As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    argTypeCount = 0
    Dim src As String
    src = Trim(listText)
    If src = "" Or Instr(src, ",") = 0 Then Return 0

    Dim scanPos As Integer
    scanPos = 1
    Do While scanPos <= Len(src)
        Dim commaPos As Integer
        commaPos = Instr(scanPos, src, ",")
        Dim part As String
        If commaPos = 0 Then
            part = Mid(src, scanPos)
            scanPos = Len(src) + 1
        Else
            part = Mid(src, scanPos, commaPos - scanPos)
            scanPos = commaPos + 1
        End If

        Dim typeName As String
        If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(part, typeName) = 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid argument type list token " & Trim(part)
            Return -1
        End If

        If argTypeCount > UBound(argTypes) Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) argument type list too large"
            Return -1
        End If

        argTypes(argTypeCount) = typeName
        argTypeCount += 1
    Loop

    Return IIf(argTypeCount > 0, 1, 0)
End Function

Private Function ExecTryReadDllArgTypeList(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByVal totalChildCount As Integer, argTypes() As String, ByRef argTypeCount As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    argTypeCount = 0
    If argStartPos < 0 Or argStartPos >= totalChildCount Then Return 0

    Dim listNode As Integer
    listNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos)
    If listNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(listNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then Return 0

    Dim status As Integer
    status = ExecFfiParseArgTypeList(ps.ast.nodes(listNode).value, argTypes(), argTypeCount, errText)
    If status <= 0 Then Return status

    Dim actualArgs As Integer
    actualArgs = totalChildCount - argStartPos - 1
    If actualArgs <> argTypeCount Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) argument type list count mismatch expected=" & LTrim(Str(argTypeCount)) & " actual=" & LTrim(Str(actualArgs))
        Return -1
    End If

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecEvalDllArgAsMixedSlot(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal argNode As Integer, ByRef argType As String, ByRef stringKeepAlive As String, ByRef u64Out As ULongInt, ByRef f64Out As Double, ByRef isF64Out As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim t As String
    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(argType, t) = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid mixed argument type " & Trim(argType)
        Return 0
    End If

    stringKeepAlive = ""
    u64Out = 0
    f64Out = 0#
    isF64Out = 0

    Select Case t
    Case "F64"
        Dim fv As ExecValue
        If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, fv, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If fv.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) F64 requires numeric argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If fv.valueType = "DOUBLE" Then
            f64Out = fv.floatValue
        Else
            f64Out = CDbl(fv.intValue)
        End If
        isF64Out = 1
        Return 1

    Case "STRPTR"
        If ExecEvalNodeAsText(ps, es, argNode, stringKeepAlive, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
#Ifdef __FB_64BIT__
        u64Out = CULngInt(CLngInt(CPtr(Any Ptr, StrPtr(stringKeepAlive))))
#Else
        u64Out = CULngInt(CULng(CPtr(Any Ptr, StrPtr(stringKeepAlive))))
#EndIf
        Return 1

    Case Else
        Dim iv As ExecValue
        If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, iv, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If iv.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) " & t & " requires numeric/pointer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If t = "U64" And iv.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

        If t = "I32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CLng(iv.intValue))
        Else
            u64Out = CULngInt(iv.intValue)
        End If
        Return 1
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef retTypeText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, argTypes() As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0
    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() <> "WIN64-MSABI" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 4 Then Return 1

    Dim retType As String
    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(retTypeText, retType) = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 3) As ULongInt
    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 3) As Double
    Dim argStrings(0 To 3) As String
    Dim argMask As Integer
    argMask = 0
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos + i)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) mixed argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If
        Dim isF64 As Integer
        If ExecEvalDllArgAsMixedSlot(ps, es, argNode, argTypes(i), argStrings(i), argValuesU64(i), argValuesF64(i), isF64, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If isF64 <> 0 Then argMask = argMask Or (1 Shl i)
    Next i

    If ExecX64DispatchMixedInvoke(procAddr, retType, argCount, argMask, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), valueOut, errText) = 0 Then
        Return 0
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub ExecNormalizeError(ByRef errText As String)
    If errText = "" Then Exit Sub

    Dim t As String
    t = LCase(LTrim(errText))
    If Left(t, Len(EXEC_ERR_PREFIX)) <> EXEC_ERR_PREFIX Then
        errText = EXEC_ERR_PREFIX & errText
    End If
End Sub

Private Function ExecConsumeLoopExit(ByRef es As ExecState, ByRef loopKind As String) As Integer
    If es.runtime.exitRequest = "" Then Return 0

    Dim req As String
Yamanýn yapýlmasý ve dosya düzeni
Gerçek bir patch dosyasý oluþturmak gerekiyor. FFI wrapper'larýnýn eksik imzalarýný anlamalýyým. 13 wrapper'ý manuel olarak deðiþtirmek yerine, fonksiyon tanýmlarý ve C baþlýklarýna dayalý olarak bir script yazabilir

Yapýlandýrma ve derleme düzenlemeleri
Sanýrým, kaynak düzeni yerine manifest kullanarak ilerlemek daha uygun olacak. Þu konularý ele almamýz gerekecek:

Birleþik derleme programý (Python) ve BAT/PS1 sarmalayýcýlarýyla DLL derleme ve çýktý aþamalarý.

Gerekli dizin yapýlarý.

Baðýmlýlýk yönetimi ve yedekleme.

DLL ABI imzalarý ve parametre düzenlemeleri, özellikle 8-10 parametreli opsiyonel yapýlar.

Patch generation for build and DLL ABI signatures
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
rg -n 'Private Function ExecEvalDllCallCore|Function ExecEvalDllCallCore' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs
sed -n '1400,1605p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs
sed -n '1605,1765p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs
                    If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
                        gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
                        gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "header-invalid"
                        UxbWarn "FFI policy header invalid, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
                    Else
                        ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
                        UxbWarn "FFI policy header invalid, defaults active path=" & policyPath
                    End If
                    Exit Sub
                End If

                headerValid = 1
            Else
                Dim dllName As String
                Dim symbolName As String
                Dim signatureText As String
                Dim callingConventionText As String
                Dim hashText As String
                Dim signerText As String
                If ExecParseFfiAllowlistLine(lineText, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, hashText, signerText) <> 0 Then
                    ExecFfiAllowlistAppend dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, hashText, signerText
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Loop

    Close #f

    If headerValid = 0 Then
        If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "header-missing"
            UxbWarn "FFI policy header missing, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
        Else
            ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
            UxbWarn "FFI policy header missing, defaults active path=" & policyPath
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If

    If gFfiAllowEntryCount = 0 Then
        If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "no-valid-entries"
            UxbWarn "FFI policy had no valid entries, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
        Else
            ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
            UxbWarn "FFI policy had no valid entries, defaults active path=" & policyPath
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If

    UxbInfo "FFI policy loaded path=" & policyPath & " entries=" & LTrim(Str(gFfiAllowEntryCount))
End Sub

Private Function ExecPolicyDenyCode(ByRef reasonText As String) As Integer
    Select Case LCase(Trim(reasonText))
    Case "deny-policy-load-failed"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_LOAD_FAILED
    Case "deny-attestation-required"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_ATTEST_REQUIRED
    Case "deny-hash-mismatch"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_MISMATCH
    Case "deny-signer-mismatch"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_MISMATCH
    Case "deny-hash-extract-failed"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_EXTRACT_FAILED
    Case "deny-signer-extract-failed"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_EXTRACT_FAILED
    Case "deny-not-allowlisted"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_DENY
    Case Else
        Return 0
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecPolicyDenyPhase(ByRef reasonText As String) As String
    Select Case LCase(Trim(reasonText))
    Case "deny-policy-load-failed"
        Return "policy-load"
    Case "deny-hash-mismatch", "deny-hash-extract-failed"
        Return "hash-verify"
    Case "deny-signer-mismatch", "deny-signer-extract-failed"
        Return "signer-verify"
    Case "deny-attestation-required"
        Return "attestation-required"
    Case "deny-not-allowlisted"
        Return "allowlist-lookup"
    Case Else
        Return "policy"
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecValidateDllMarshalArg(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal argNode As Integer, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim argValue As ExecValue
    If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, argValue, errText) = 0 Then Return 0

    Dim sig As String
    sig = UCase(Trim(signatureText))

    Select Case sig
    Case "I32"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) I32 requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "U64"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If argValue.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "F64"
        If argValue.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) F64 requires numeric argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "PTR"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) PTR requires pointer/integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "STRPTR"
        If argValue.valueType <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) STRPTR requires string argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "BYVAL"
        ' BYVAL is value-passing marker; no extra runtime type guard here.

    Case "BYREF"
        If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argNode).kind) <> "IDENT" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) BYREF requires addressable target argument"
            Return 0
        End If
    End Select

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecTryParseAliasFfiTarget(ByRef targetText As String, ByRef ffiKindOut As String, ByRef dllNameOut As String, ByRef symbolNameOut As String, ByRef signatureOut As String, ByRef conventionOut As String) As Integer
    ffiKindOut = ""
    dllNameOut = ""
    symbolNameOut = ""
    signatureOut = ""
    conventionOut = "CDECL"

    Dim lx As LexerState
    LexerInit lx, targetText

    Dim i As Integer
    i = 0

    While i < lx.tokens.count And (lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "EOL")
        i += 1
    Wend

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    Dim headKind As String
    headKind = UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind)
    If (headKind <> "KEYWORD" And headKind <> "IDENT") Or UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme) <> "CALL" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "(" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    Dim ffiHeadKind As String
    ffiHeadKind = UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind)
    If (ffiHeadKind <> "KEYWORD" And ffiHeadKind <> "IDENT") Then Return 0
    ffiKindOut = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
    If ffiKindOut <> "DLL" And ffiKindOut <> "API" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    dllNameOut = ""
    Do While i < lx.tokens.count
        If lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then Exit Do
        dllNameOut &= lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme
        i += 1
    Loop
    dllNameOut = Trim(dllNameOut)
    If dllNameOut = "" Then Return 0

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    symbolNameOut = ""
    Do While i < lx.tokens.count
        If lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then Exit Do
        symbolNameOut &= lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme
        i += 1
    Loop
    symbolNameOut = Trim(symbolNameOut)
    If symbolNameOut = "" Then Return 0


    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    If UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind) <> "KEYWORD" And UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind) <> "IDENT" Then Return 0
    signatureOut = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
    i += 1

    If ffiKindOut = "DLL" And i < lx.tokens.count And lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then
        i += 1
        If i < lx.tokens.count Then
            Dim conv As String
            conv = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
            If ExecIsDllCallingConvention(conv) <> 0 Then
                conventionOut = conv
            End If
        End If
    End If

    If ffiKindOut = "API" Then
        conventionOut = "CDECL"
        If InStr(dllNameOut, ".") = 0 Then dllNameOut &= ".dll"
    End If

    Return ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(signatureOut)
End Function

Private Function ExecTryParseAliasDllTarget(ByRef targetText As String, ByRef dllNameOut As String, ByRef symbolNameOut As String, ByRef signatureOut As String, ByRef conventionOut As String) As Integer
    Dim ffiKind As String
    If ExecTryParseAliasFfiTarget(targetText, ffiKind, dllNameOut, symbolNameOut, signatureOut, conventionOut) = 0 Then Return 0
    Return IIf(ffiKind = "DLL", 1, 0)
End Function

Private Function ExecDllCallIsAllowlisted(ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal requireAttestation As Integer, ByRef denyReasonOut As String) As Integer
    ExecLoadFfiAllowlistIfNeeded

    If gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed <> 0 Then
        denyReasonOut = "deny-policy-load-failed"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim dllKey As String
    Dim symbolKey As String
    Dim sigKey As String
    Dim convKey As String

    dllKey = UCase(Trim(dllName))
    symbolKey = UCase(Trim(symbolName))
    sigKey = UCase(Trim(signatureText))
    convKey = UCase(Trim(callingConventionText))

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To gFfiAllowEntryCount - 1
        If dllKey = gFfiAllowEntries(i).dllName And symbolKey = gFfiAllowEntries(i).symbolName And sigKey = gFfiAllowEntries(i).signatureText Then
            Dim allowConv As String
            allowConv = UCase(Trim(gFfiAllowEntries(i).callingConventionText))
            If ExecAreDllCallingConventionsCompatible(convKey, allowConv) = 0 Then
                GoTo NextAllowEntry
            End If

            If requireAttestation <> 0 Then
                If gFfiAllowEntries(i).hashText = "" Or gFfiAllowEntries(i).signerText = "" Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-attestation-required"
                    Return 0
                End If

                If gFfiAllowEntries(i).hashText = "*" Or gFfiAllowEntries(i).signerText = "*" Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-attestation-required"
                    Return 0
                End If

                Dim hashState As Integer
                hashState = FfiHashMatchState(gFfiAllowEntries(i).hashText, dllName)
                If hashState < 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-hash-extract-failed"
                    Return 0
                End If

                If hashState = 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-hash-mismatch"
                    Return 0
                End If

                Dim signerState As Integer
                signerState = FfiSignerMatchState(gFfiAllowEntries(i).signerText, dllName)
                If signerState < 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-signer-extract-failed"
                    Return 0
                End If

                If signerState = 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-signer-mismatch"
                    Return 0
                End If
            End If

            denyReasonOut = ""
            Return 1
        End If
NextAllowEntry:
    Next i

    denyReasonOut = "deny-not-allowlisted"
    Return 0
End Function

Private Sub ExecAuditDllPolicy(ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByRef modeText As String, ByVal allowDecision As Integer, ByRef reasonText As String)
    Dim msgText As String
    Dim denyCode As Integer
    denyCode = ExecPolicyDenyCode(reasonText)

    Dim phaseText As String
    phaseText = ExecPolicyDenyPhase(reasonText)

    msgText = "FFI CALL(DLL) event=ffi_policy_decision mode=" & modeText & " decision="
    If allowDecision <> 0 Then
        msgText &= "allow"
    Else
        msgText &= "deny"
    End If

    msgText &= " lib=" & dllName & " symbol=" & symbolName & " sig=" & signatureText & " conv=" & callingConventionText
    msgText &= " abi=" & ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName()
    msgText &= " allow=" & LTrim(Str(allowDecision)) & " deny_code=" & LTrim(Str(denyCode))
    msgText &= " phase=" & phaseText
    If reasonText <> "" Then msgText &= " reason=" & reasonText

    If allowDecision <> 0 Then
        UxbInfo msgText
    Else
        UxbWarn msgText
    End If
End Sub

Baþlangýç kategorilerini incelemek
Eval kategorilerinin baþýný incelemem gerekiyor. Muhtemelen burada, deðerlendirme iþlemine iliþkin grup ya da fonksiyonlar hakkýnda bazý temel bilgiler yer alýyor. En doðru þekilde ilerlemek için bu baþlanðýcý anlamam önemli olacak.

Ýlk deðerlendirme kategorilerini görüntüleme
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
sed -n '1,170p' src/runtime/exec/exec_eval_builtin_categories.fbs
Private Function ExecEvalDllCallCore(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim ffiArgCount As Integer
    ffiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)

    Dim effectiveArgStartPos As Integer
    effectiveArgStartPos = argStartPos

    Dim mixedArgTypes(0 To 15) As String
    Dim mixedArgTypeCount As Integer
    Dim mixedTypeStatus As Integer
    mixedTypeStatus = ExecTryReadDllArgTypeList(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos, ffiArgCount, mixedArgTypes(), mixedArgTypeCount, errText)
    If mixedTypeStatus < 0 Then Return 0
    If mixedTypeStatus > 0 Then effectiveArgStartPos = argStartPos + 1

    If signatureText = "BYREF" Then
        Dim byrefNode As Integer
        byrefNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos)
        If byrefNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(byrefNode).kind) <> "IDENT" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) BYREF requires addressable target argument (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BYREF_TARGET)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
    End If

    Dim ai As Integer
    For ai = effectiveArgStartPos To ffiArgCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ai)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim argSig As String
        If mixedTypeStatus > 0 Then
            argSig = mixedArgTypes(ai - effectiveArgStartPos)
        Else
            argSig = signatureText
        End If

        If ExecValidateDllMarshalArg(ps, es, argNode, argSig, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
    Next ai

    Dim denyReason As String
    Dim allowDecision As Integer
    Dim requireAttestation As Integer
    Dim ffiMode As String
    ffiMode = ExecGetFfiPolicyMode()
    requireAttestation = IIf(ffiMode = "ENFORCE", 1, 0)
    allowDecision = ExecDllCallIsAllowlisted(dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, requireAttestation, denyReason)
    ExecAuditDllPolicy dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiMode, allowDecision, denyReason

    If allowDecision = 0 And ffiMode = "ENFORCE" Then
        If denyReason = "deny-policy-load-failed" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy load failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-attestation-required" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy attestation required (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_ATTEST_REQUIRED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-hash-extract-failed" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy hash extraction failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_EXTRACT_FAILED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-hash-mismatch" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy hash mismatch (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_MISMATCH)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-signer-extract-failed" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy signer extraction failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_EXTRACT_FAILED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-signer-mismatch" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy signer mismatch (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_MISMATCH)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_DENY)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim resolverMode As String
    resolverMode = ExecGetFfiResolverMode()

    Dim ffiArgRuntimeCount As Integer
    ffiArgRuntimeCount = ffiArgCount - effectiveArgStartPos
    If ffiArgRuntimeCount < 0 Then ffiArgRuntimeCount = 0

    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() = "WIN64-MSABI" Then
        Dim x64InvokedValue As Integer
        Dim x64InvokedOut As Integer
        Dim x64InvokeErr As String
        If mixedTypeStatus > 0 Then
            If ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, mixedArgTypes(), ffiArgRuntimeCount, x64InvokedValue, x64InvokedOut, x64InvokeErr) = 0 Then
                If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                    errText = x64InvokeErr
                    Return 0
                End If
                If x64InvokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x64 invoke report-only: " & x64InvokeErr
                Return 0
            End If
        ElseIf ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64I32(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, x64InvokedValue, x64InvokedOut, x64InvokeErr) = 0 Then
            If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                errText = x64InvokeErr
                Return 0
            End If
            If x64InvokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x64 invoke report-only: " & x64InvokeErr
            Return 0
        End If

        If x64InvokedOut <> 0 Then
            Return x64InvokedValue
        End If

        Return 0
    End If

    Dim cleanupType As String
    Dim resolverErr As String
        If ExecResolveX86InteropTarget(dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, cleanupType, resolverErr) = 0 Then
            If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                errText = resolverErr
                Return 0
            End If
            If resolverErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x86 resolver fallback: " & resolverErr

            Dim directInvokedValue As Integer
            Dim directInvokedOut As Integer
            Dim directInvokeErr As String
            If ExecTryInvokeDirectX86I32(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, directInvokedValue, directInvokedOut, directInvokeErr) = 0 Then
                If directInvokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x86 direct invoke report-only: " & directInvokeErr
            ElseIf directInvokedOut <> 0 Then
                Return directInvokedValue
            End If
        Else
            UxbInfo "FFI x86 resolver matched cleanup=" & cleanupType & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))

        Dim invokedValue As Integer
        Dim invokedOut As Integer
        Dim invokeErr As String
            If ExecTryInvokeResolvedX86I32(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, invokedValue, invokedOut, invokeErr) = 0 Then
                If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                    errText = invokeErr
                    Return 0
                End If
                If invokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x86 invoke proof report-only: " & invokeErr
            ElseIf invokedOut <> 0 Then
                Return invokedValue
            End If
        End If

        If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL/API) invoke path unavailable signature=" & UCase(Trim(signatureText)) & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))
            Return 0
        End If
        UxbWarn "FFI invoke report-only fallback signature=" & UCase(Trim(signatureText)) & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))
        Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecEvalBuiltinFfiCategory(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callName As String, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim callKey As String
    callKey = UCase(Trim(callName))

    Select Case callKey
    Case "DLL", "CALL"
        Dim ffiArgCount As Integer
        ffiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)
        Dim ffiBasePos As Integer
        ffiBasePos = 0
        Dim callMarker As String
        callMarker = "DLL"
Wrapper fonksiyonlarýný güncellemek ve iþaretçi listesi oluþturmak
Wrapper fonksiyonlarýnýn sayýsýný ve maksimum parametre sayýsýný bilmemiz gerek. Tüm CALL(DLL) ifadelerini analiz etmek için Python regex kullanabilirim. Eski sözdizimiyle yazýlmýþ wrapper'lardan doðru iþlevler oluþturul

Deðiþken argüman sayýsý ile çalýþmak
MSYS2, libffi saðlayabilir, ancak bu yeni bir baðýmlýlýk ve derleme entegrasyonu ekler. Alternatif olarak, Windows x64 ABI kullanarak dinamik çaðrýlar yapmasý için bir yardýmcý DLL (uxb_ffi_bridge.dll) yazýlabilir. FreeBasic runtime, sabit köprü iþlevine 6 veya daha fazla argümanla doðrudan çaðrý yapabilir ama mevcut sýnýrlama 4 argüman.

Auditing FreeBasic DLL çaðrý fonksiyonlarýný
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
python - <<'PY'
import re, pathlib, json
root=pathlib.Path('libsx')
for f in sorted(root.rglob('*.bas')):
    txt=f.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore')
    # Parse routines simplistic
    current=None
    rows=[]
    for ln,line in enumerate(txt.splitlines(),1):
        m=re.match(r'\s*(FUNCTION|SUB)\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*\((.*?)\)\s*(?:AS\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+))?', line, re.I)
        if m:
            kind,name,params,ret=m.groups(); current=(kind.upper(),name,params, (ret or ('VOID' if kind.upper()=='SUB' else 'I32')).upper())
        if 'CALL(DLL' in line.upper():
            # collect content balanced maybe line only
            s=line[line.upper().find('CALL(DLL'):]
            rows.append((ln,current,s.strip()))
        if re.match(r'\s*END\s+(FUNCTION|SUB)',line,re.I): current=None
    if rows:
        print('\n',f)
        for ln,c,s in rows:
            kind,name,params,ret=c if c else ('?','?','','?')
            ptypes=[]
            if params.strip():
                for p in params.split(','):
                    mm=re.search(r'\bAS\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',p,re.I)
                    ptypes.append(mm.group(1).upper() if mm else '?')
            print(f'{ln:4d} {kind} {name} ret={ret} argc={len(ptypes)} types={ptypes} :: {s[:130]}')
PY

Proje için kapsam belirleme ve iyileþtirme
Çýktý büyük ama yeterli! Þu an runtime'ý tamamen düzeltmeye gerek yok. Kullanýcýnýn gereksinimlerine göre bir plan oluþturabiliriz. Öncelikle iþleyebileceðimiz birkaç seçenek üretmeliyiz:

Tek tip bir Python derleme betiði.

Klasik dizinleri ekleyen bir yama.

10 parametreyi destekleyecek þekilde analizciyi güncelleme.

Daha Esnek Çaðrýlar için libffi Kullanýmý
Belki de CALL(DLL) fonksiyonunuzu, 10 parametreye kadar karýþýk imzalara sahip iþlevleri çaðýrabilecek þekilde libffi kullan

Plan for mixed argument handling and uxffi bridge
Zaten FreeBasic'te argümanlar stringleri ZString olarak tutuyor. Bu yüzden uxffi_request yapýsýnda hata mesajlarý gibi yerler için doðru eþleþmeleri saðlamak önemli. Argümanlar için uxffi_bridge 'i kullanmayý 10 parametreyle sýnýrlý kadar düþünebiliriz.

Handling fallback for optional system and support for I64
Ýþe yarar bir sistem kurmak için, eðer uxffi.dll yoksa ve argüman sayýsý 4'ten azsa mevcut direkt çaðrýyý kullanabiliriz. Eðer 4'ten büyükse, hata dönebiliriz. I64 desteði eklemek de önemli görünüyor; I64 ve U64 arasýnda geçiþ yaparak daha saðlam hale getirebiliriz.

Analiz edilen fonksiyonlarýn parametre ve dönüþ türleri
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
python - <<'PY'
import re,pathlib,collections
rets=collections.Counter(); args=collections.Counter(); maxarg=0; over=[]
for f in pathlib.Path('libsx').rglob('*.bas'):
 txt=f.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore')
 for m in re.finditer(r'^\s*(FUNCTION|SUB)\s+\w+\s*\((.*?)\)\s*(?:AS\s+(\w+))?',txt,re.I|re.M):
  kind,ps,ret=m.groups(); ret=(ret or ('VOID' if kind.upper()=='SUB' else 'I32')).upper(); rets[ret]+=1
  ats=[]
  for p in [x.strip() for x in ps.split(',') if x.strip()]:
   mm=re.search(r'\bAS\s+(\w+)',p,re.I); t=mm.group(1).upper() if mm else '?'; args[t]+=1; ats.append(t)
  if len(ats)>maxarg:maxarg=len(ats)
  if len(ats)>4:over.append((str(f),m.group(0).splitlines()[0].strip(),len(ats),ats))
print('returns',rets)
print('args',args)
print('maxarg',maxarg,'over4',len(over))
for x in over: print(x)
PY

Handling patch and wrapper functionality
Bir yamanýn yapýlmasý gerekiyor ve belki de benzersiz bir wrapper konum adý eklemek faydalý olabilir. Parser’ý I64, U32 ve STRING gibi iþaretçileri kabul edecek þekilde deðiþtirebiliriz. STRING dönüþ deðerini hali hazýrda const char* fonksiyonlarýndan alýp STRPTR olarak eþlemek gerekebilir. Ancak F64 ve string türleriyle ilgili problemi çözmek için sol baþka düzenlemeler yapýlabilir.

Directory links and patch methods
Yeni dizin baðlantýlarý oluþturdum: libraries/native, libraries/wrappers, libraries/include. Bunlar eski yollarý bozmadan organize bir eriþim saðlar. Eðer baðlantý oluþturulamazsa, dizinler ve .path iþaretçileri oluþturulur. Ayrýca, yamanýn uygulanmasý için bir PowerShell scripti de ekledim.

Fonksiyon imzalarýný eþleþtirme
Mevcut çaðrýlarý çoðunlukla bir satýrlýk olarak kullanabiliriz. Yalnýzca geçerli imzasý eksik olan çaðrýlarý dönüþtürmemiz gerekecek. C baþlýklarýný parse etmek daha doðru olabilir, belki Clang veya pycparser kullanabilirim. C türlerini FFI türlerine dönüþtürmek için basit bir regex yapabiliriz.

Baþlangýç prototiplerini haritalama ve analiz etme

Yazýlým düzeni ve derleyiciler
Projeyi hýzla ama saðlam bir þekilde devam ettirmek için kök yolu ve testleri incelememiz gerekebilir. Jestleri deðiþtirmemeye özen gösterebiliriz fakat C baþlýklarýyla bir Python jeneratörü oluþturabiliriz. Regex ile baþlýklarý analiz edip, eksik imzasý olan her çaðrýyý eþleþtirebiliriz.

Ffi köprüsü ve dönüþüm türleri
Yeni bir köprü dosyasý (exec_ffi_bridge10.fbs) ekleyebiliriz ve fonksiyonlardan önce dahil edebiliriz. Bu, ExecValue ve eval fonksiyonlarýna eriþim gerektiriyor. Ancak dönüþ deðeri türünü, mevcut fonksiyonlarý ve CInt dönüþümünü yanlýþlýkla V64'ün yerine sýfýrlama sorununu aþmak önemli.

C köprü ve FreeBasic entegrasyonu
Köprü fonksiyonunu C kullanarak libffi ile yazmamýz ve FreeBasic entegrasyonu saðlamamýz gerekiyor. Ýlk olarak exec_ffi_runtime.fbs dosyasýndaki önemli dahil edilenleri gözden geçirelim. Bu, doðru yapýlandýrma için gerekli adýmlarý ve baðlamý doðru þekilde belirlememize yardýmcý olabilir.

Ýnceleme: exec_ffi_runtime.fbs baþlýklarý
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base
sed -n '1,80p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs

Sub ExecSetFfiPolicyPath(ByRef pathText As String)
    Dim p As String
    p = Trim(pathText)
    If p = "" Then
        gFfiDefaultPolicyPath = "build/manual/config/ffi_allowlist.txt"
    Else
        gFfiDefaultPolicyPath = p
    End If
    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyPath = gFfiDefaultPolicyPath

    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then
        gFfiAllowlistLoaded = 0
        gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 0
        gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = ""
        gFfiAllowEntryCount = 0
        Erase gFfiAllowEntries
    End If
End Sub

Private Function ExecGetFfiPolicyPath() As String
    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then
        If Trim(gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyPath) = "" Then
            gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyPath = gFfiDefaultPolicyPath
            If Trim(gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyPath) = "" Then gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyPath = "build/manual/config/ffi_allowlist.txt"
        End If
        Return gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyPath
    End If
    If Trim(gFfiDefaultPolicyPath) = "" Then gFfiDefaultPolicyPath = "build/manual/config/ffi_allowlist.txt"
    Return gFfiDefaultPolicyPath
End Function

Sub ExecSetFfiPolicyMode(ByRef modeText As String)
    Dim m As String
    m = UCase(Trim(modeText))
    If m = "ENFORCE" Then
        gFfiDefaultPolicyEnforce = 1
    Else
        gFfiDefaultPolicyEnforce = 0
    End If
    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyEnforce = gFfiDefaultPolicyEnforce
End Sub

Private Function ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() As String
    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then
        If gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiPolicyEnforce <> 0 Then Return "ENFORCE"
        Return "REPORT_ONLY"
    End If
    If gFfiDefaultPolicyEnforce <> 0 Then Return "ENFORCE"
    Return "REPORT_ONLY"
End Function

Sub ExecSetFfiResolverPath(ByRef pathText As String)
    Dim p As String
    p = Trim(pathText)
    If p = "" Then
        gFfiDefaultResolverPath = "build/manual/interop/ffi_call_x86_resolver.csv"
    Else
        gFfiDefaultResolverPath = p
    End If
    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then gActiveRuntimeCtx->ffiResolverPath = gFfiDefaultResolverPath

    If gActiveRuntimeCtx <> 0 Then
        gFfiX86ResolverLoaded = 0
        gFfiX86ResolverLoadFailed = 0
        gFfiX86ResolverLoadFailReason = ""
        gFfiX86ResolverEntryCount = 0
        Erase gFfiX86ResolverEntries
        gFfiX86ResolvedEntryCount = 0
        Erase gFfiX86ResolvedEntries
        gFfiX86SymptrMapEntryCount = 0
        Erase gFfiX86SymptrMapEntries
        gFfiX86InvokeCount = 0
        gFfiX86LastInvokeStubId = 0
        gFfiX86LastInvokeConv = ""
        gFfiX86CallerCleanupBytes = 0
        gFfiX86CalleeCleanupBytes = 0
        gFfiX86SymptrWriteCount = 0
    End If
End Sub

Type ExecValue
    valueType As String  ' INTEGER, FLOAT, STRING

    floatValue As Double

    ' Array and object support removed for simplicity
End Type

    ' typeBinding As TypeBinding
End Type

Adýmlarý dikkatle uygulama
Sanýrým bu projeyi dikkatli bir þekilde uygulamak için birkaç çözüm yolu var. Kullanýcýya net bir yol haritasý sunmalý ve gereksinimleri doðrultusunda bir yamanýn ilk aþamasýný hazýrlamalýyým. Bununla birlikte, runtime entegrasyonu eklemek yerine sadece kaynak kodundaki düzeltmeleri yapabilirim. Bu, somut bir çözüm saðlamak için iyi bir baþlangýç olabilir!

Devam ediyorum, fakat dikkatli olmalýyým
Þimdi devam edebiliriz. Öncelikle deðiþtirilmiþ bir kopya ve betikler oluþturmalýyým. Python kullanarak, mevcut sarmalayýcýlarý dönüþtürmeden önce doðru sarmalayýcýlarý tespit ederek prototip haritasý oluþturacaðým. Sembol haritasýný kullanarak, çalýþýr bir kopyada kodu geliþtirebiliriz.

Transforming and patching wrapper functions
bash -lc set -e
rm -rf /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/mod /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patch
cp -a /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/mod
mkdir -p /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patch
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import re, json
root=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/mod')

# Parse C prototypes from runtime_ext headers.
protos={}

def clean_type(t):
    t=re.sub(r'__declspec\s*\([^)]*\)',' ',t)
    t=re.sub(r'\b(?:UX[A-Z0-9_]*_API|UXD_EXPORT|UXM_EXPORT|UXSTATS_API|UXS2_API|UXT_EXPORT|UXNN_EXPORT|UXONNX_EXPORT|UXG_API|UXC_API|UXLLAMA_API|UXDF_EXPORT)\b',' ',t)
    t=re.sub(r'\b(?:extern|static|inline)\b',' ',t)
    return ' '.join(t.replace('\t',' ').split())

def map_type(t, is_ret=False):
    s=clean_type(t).lower().replace('const ','').strip()
    # remove arg name if present later handled separately
    if s in ('void',''):
        return 'VOID'
    if 'char' in s and '*' in s:
        return 'STRPTR'
    if '*' in s:
        return 'PTR'
    if 'double' in s or 'float64' in s:
        return 'F64'
    if 'uint64_t' in s or 'unsigned long long' in s or 'uintptr_t' in s or 'size_t' in s:
        return 'U64'
    if 'int64_t' in s or s == 'long long' or 'signed long long' in s:
        return 'I64'
    if 'uint32_t' in s or 'unsigned int' in s:
        return 'U32'
    if 'int32_t' in s or s == 'int' or 'signed int' in s or s == 'long':
        return 'I32'
    if 'uint16_t' in s or 'uint8_t' in s:
        return 'U32'
    if 'int16_t' in s or 'int8_t' in s:
        return 'I32'
    return 'PTR' if '*' in s else 'I32'

for h in root.glob('runtime_ext/*/*.h'):
    txt=h.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore')
    txt=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/',' ',txt,flags=re.S)
    txt=re.sub(r'//.*',' ',txt)
    # Join lines, keep semicolon boundaries.
    for stmt in txt.split(';'):
        s=' '.join(stmt.split())
        if '(' not in s or ')' not in s or s.lstrip().startswith('#'):
            continue
        # Strip preprocessor fragments before likely declaration.
        m=re.search(r'(?P<ret>(?:__declspec\s*\([^)]*\)\s*)?(?:[A-Za-z_][\w]*\s+|\*\s*)+?)\s*(?P<name>[A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\((?P<args>[^()]*)\)\s*$',s)
        if not m:
            continue
        name=m.group('name')
        if name in ('if','for','while','switch'):
            continue
        ret=map_type(m.group('ret'),True)
        args=[]
        argtxt=m.group('args').strip()
        if argtxt and argtxt.lower()!='void':
            for a in [x.strip() for x in argtxt.split(',')]:
                # Remove parameter name while preserving pointer stars.
                aa=re.sub(r'\b[A-Za-z_]\w*\s*(\[[^]]*\])?\s*$','',a).strip()
                args.append(map_type(aa))
        protos[name]=(ret,args,str(h.relative_to(root)))

# Manual aliases/exports if regex missed due macros or formatting.
print('prototype count',len(protos))

# Helpers for balanced CALL expression on one line.
def split_top(s):
    out=[]; cur=[]; depth=0; quote=False; i=0
    while i<len(s):
        ch=s[i]
        if ch=='"':
            quote=not quote; cur.append(ch)
        elif not quote:
            if ch=='(':
                depth+=1; cur.append(ch)
            elif ch==')':
                depth-=1; cur.append(ch)
            elif ch==',' and depth==0:
                out.append(''.join(cur).strip()); cur=[]
            else: cur.append(ch)
        else: cur.append(ch)
        i+=1
    out.append(''.join(cur).strip())
    return out

changed=[]; unresolved=[]
for f in sorted(root.glob('libsx/*/*.bas')):
    if 'PLACEHOLDER' in f.name.upper():
        continue
    lines=f.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').splitlines()
    # DLL constants
    consts={m.group(1).upper():m.group(2) for line in lines if (m:=re.match(r'\s*CONST\s+(\w+)\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"',line,re.I))}
    new=[]
    for ln,line in enumerate(lines,1):
        up=line.upper()
        pos=up.find('CALL(DLL,')
        if pos<0 or line.lstrip().startswith("'"):
            new.append(line); continue
        # Find balanced CALL(DLL,...)
        start=pos
        p=line.find('(',start)
        depth=0; quote=False; end=None
        for i in range(p,len(line)):
            ch=line[i]
            if ch=='"': quote=not quote
            elif not quote:
                if ch=='(': depth+=1
                elif ch==')':
                    depth-=1
                    if depth==0:
                        end=i; break
        if end is None:
            unresolved.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,'multiline/unbalanced'))
            new.append(line); continue
        inside=line[p+1:end]
        parts=split_top(inside)
        # parts[0] DLL
        if len(parts)<3 or parts[0].strip().upper()!='DLL':
            new.append(line); continue
        libexpr=parts[1].strip()
        if libexpr.startswith('"'):
            dll=libexpr.strip('"')
        else:
            dll=consts.get(libexpr.upper(), f.parent.name+'.dll')
        symm=re.match(r'"([^"]+)"',parts[2].strip())
        if not symm:
            unresolved.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,'nonliteral symbol'))
            new.append(line); continue
        sym=symm.group(1)
        proto=protos.get(sym)
        if not proto:
            unresolved.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,'prototype missing '+sym))
            new.append(line); continue
        ret,args,_=proto
        # If already canonical (4th is valid return token), keep but normalize only unsupported aliases optionally.
        valid={'VOID','I32','U32','I64','U64','F64','PTR','STRPTR','BYREF','BYVAL'}
        if len(parts)>=4 and parts[3].strip().upper() in valid:
            new.append(line); continue
        actual=parts[3:]
        if len(actual)!=len(args):
            # Some wrappers pass nested expanded args unlike wrapper signature; compare actual call count to C prototype.
            unresolved.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,f'arg mismatch {sym}: actual {len(actual)} proto {len(args)}'))
            new.append(line); continue
        canon=['DLL',f'"{dll}"',f'"{sym}"',ret,'CDECL']
        if args:
            canon.append('"'+','.join(args)+'"')
            canon.extend(actual)
        replacement='CALL('+', '.join(canon)+')'
        newline=line[:start]+replacement+line[end+1:]
        new.append(newline)
        changed.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,sym,ret,args))
    f.write_text('\n'.join(new)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')

Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/wrapper_transform_report.json').write_text(json.dumps({'changed':changed,'unresolved':unresolved,'prototype_count':len(protos)},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
print('changed calls',len(changed),'unresolved',len(unresolved))
for x in unresolved[:60]: print('UNRES',x)
PY
Command failed because it timed out.
Daha basit bir yol izlemeliyim
Buradaki timeout oldukça ilginç, belki regex karmaþýklaþtýrýyor. Eski iþlemi öldürmek ve süreci basitleþtirmek daha iyi olacak gibi görünüyor. Hadi, daha optimize edilmiþ bir þekilde, ifadeli regex ile tekrar deneyelim.

Optimizing and transforming DLL function calls
bash -lc set -e
rm -rf /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/mod
cp -a /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/mod
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import re, json
root=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/mod')
protos={}
macro_words={'UXAI_API','UXD_EXPORT','UXM_EXPORT','UXSTATS_API','UXS2_API','UXT_EXPORT','UXNN_EXPORT','UXONNX_EXPORT','UXG_API','UXC_API','UXLLAMA_API','UXDF_EXPORT','UXMATH_API','UXMC_API'}

def typemap(s):
 s=re.sub(r'__declspec\([^)]*\)',' ',s)
 for w in macro_words:s=s.replace(w,' ')
 s=' '.join(s.lower().replace('const',' ').split())
 if s.strip()=='void':return 'VOID'
 if 'char' in s and '*' in s:return 'STRPTR'
 if '*' in s:return 'PTR'
 if 'double' in s:return 'F64'
 if 'uint64_t' in s or 'unsigned long long' in s or 'uintptr_t' in s or 'size_t' in s:return 'U64'
 if 'int64_t' in s or 'long long' in s:return 'I64'
 if 'uint32_t' in s or 'unsigned int' in s:return 'U32'
 return 'I32'

for h in root.glob('runtime_ext/*/*.h'):
 txt=h.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore')
 txt=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/',' ',txt,flags=re.S)
 txt=re.sub(r'//[^\n]*',' ',txt)
 # remove preprocessor lines
 txt='\n'.join(line for line in txt.splitlines() if not line.lstrip().startswith('#'))
 for stmt in txt.split(';'):
  s=' '.join(stmt.split())
  if not s or '(' not in s or ')' not in s:continue
  left=s[:s.find('(')].strip(); args_s=s[s.find('(')+1:s.rfind(')')].strip()
  toks=left.split()
  if len(toks)<2:continue
  name=toks[-1].lstrip('*')
  if not re.match(r'^[A-Za-z_]\w*$',name):continue
  ret=left[:left.rfind(toks[-1])].strip()
  if not ret:continue
  args=[]
  if args_s and args_s.lower()!='void':
   for a in args_s.split(','):
    a=a.strip()
    # drop final identifier / array suffix
    a=re.sub(r'\b[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\[[^]]*\])?\s*$','',a).strip()
    args.append(typemap(a))
  protos[name]=(typemap(ret),args,str(h.relative_to(root)))
print('prototypes',len(protos))

def split_top(s):
 out=[];cur=[];dep=0;q=False
 for ch in s:
  if ch=='"':q=not q;cur.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch=='(':dep+=1;cur.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch==')':dep-=1;cur.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch==',' and dep==0:out.append(''.join(cur).strip());cur=[]
  else:cur.append(ch)
 out.append(''.join(cur).strip());return out
changed=[];un=[]
valid={'VOID','I32','U32','I64','U64','F64','PTR','STRPTR','BYREF','BYVAL'}
for f in sorted(root.glob('libsx/*/*.bas')):
 if 'PLACEHOLDER' in f.name.upper():continue
 lines=f.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').splitlines()
 consts={}
 for line in lines:
  m=re.match(r'\s*CONST\s+(\w+)\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"',line,re.I)
  if m:consts[m.group(1).upper()]=m.group(2)
 new=[]
 for ln,line in enumerate(lines,1):
  pos=line.upper().find('CALL(DLL,')
  if pos<0 or line.lstrip().startswith("'"):new.append(line);continue

  for i,ch in enumerate(line[p:],p):
   if ch=='"':q=not q
   elif not q and ch=='(':dep+=1
   elif not q and ch==')':
    dep-=1
    if dep==0:end=i;break
  if end is None:un.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,'unbalanced'));new.append(line);continue
  parts=split_top(line[p+1:end])

  if len(parts)>=4 and parts[3].upper() in valid:new.append(line);continue

  mm=re.fullmatch(r'"([^"]+)"',parts[2])
  if not mm:un.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,'symbol'));new.append(line);continue
  sym=mm.group(1); proto=protos.get(sym)
  if not proto:un.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,'missing proto '+sym));new.append(line);continue

  if len(actual)!=len(args):un.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,f'argc {sym} {len(actual)}!={len(args)}'));new.append(line);continue
  arr=['DLL',f'"{dll}"',f'"{sym}"',ret,'CDECL']
  if args:arr += ['"'+','.join(args)+'"']+actual
  rep='CALL('+', '.join(arr)+')'
  new.append(line[:pos]+rep+line[end+1:]);changed.append((str(f.relative_to(root)),ln,sym,ret,args))
 f.write_text('\n'.join(new)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')

Yavaþ iþlem ve dosya kopyalama sorunlarý çözülmeli
61MB'lýk bir kopyalama iþlemi çok dosya içeriyor, bu yüzden 120 saniyeyi aþmýþ olabilir. Doðrudan rsync kullanmayý deneyebiliriz ama aslýnda tam bir kopya yapmamýza gerek yok. Yalnýzca deðiþiklikleri içeren bir patch hazýrlayýp, gerekli dosyalarý temel dosyadan alarak iþleme yapacaðým.

def typemap(s):
 s=re.sub(r'__declspec\([^)]*\)',' ',s)
 for w in macro_words:s=s.replace(w,' ')
 s=' '.join(s.lower().replace('const',' ').split())
 if s.strip()=='void':return 'VOID'
 if 'char' in s and '*' in s:return 'STRPTR'
 if '*' in s:return 'PTR'
 if 'double' in s:return 'F64'
 if 'uint64_t' in s or 'unsigned long long' in s or 'uintptr_t' in s or 'size_t' in s:return 'U64'
 if 'int64_t' in s or 'long long' in s:return 'I64'
 if 'uint32_t' in s or 'unsigned int' in s:return 'U32'
 return 'I32'
for h in base.glob('runtime_ext/*/*.h'):
 txt=h.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore')
 txt=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/',' ',txt,flags=re.S); txt=re.sub(r'//[^\n]*',' ',txt)
 txt='\n'.join(line for line in txt.splitlines() if not line.lstrip().startswith('#'))
 for stmt in txt.split(';'):
  s=' '.join(stmt.split())
  if not s or '(' not in s or ')' not in s:continue
  a=s.find('('); b=s.rfind(')'); left=s[:a].strip(); arg_s=s[a+1:b].strip()
  toks=left.split()
  if len(toks)<2:continue
  name=toks[-1].lstrip('*')
  if not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z_]\w*',name):continue
  ret=left[:left.rfind(toks[-1])].strip()
  if not ret:continue
  args=[]
  if arg_s and arg_s.lower()!='void':
   for arg in arg_s.split(','):
    arg=re.sub(r'\b[A-Za-z_]\w*(?:\[[^]]*\])?\s*$','',arg.strip()).strip()
    args.append(typemap(arg))
  protos[name]=(typemap(ret),args,str(h.relative_to(base)))
print('prototypes',len(protos),flush=True)
def split_top(s):
 out=[];cur=[];dep=0;q=False
 for ch in s:
  if ch=='"': q=not q;cur.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch=='(':dep+=1;cur.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch==')':dep-=1;cur.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch==',' and dep==0:out.append(''.join(cur).strip());cur=[]
  else:cur.append(ch)
 out.append(''.join(cur).strip());return out
changed=[];un=[]
valid={'VOID','I32','U32','I64','U64','F64','PTR','STRPTR','BYREF','BYVAL'}
for f in sorted(base.glob('libsx/*/*.bas')):

 new=[];filechanged=False

  for i in range(p,len(line)):

   if ch=='"':q=not q
   elif not q and ch=='(':dep+=1
   elif not q and ch==')':
    dep-=1
    if dep==0:end=i;break
  if end is None:un.append((str(f.relative_to(base)),ln,'unbalanced'));new.append(line);continue
  parts=split_top(line[p+1:end])

  if len(parts)>=4 and parts[3].upper() in valid:new.append(line);continue
  libexpr=parts[1];dll=libexpr[1:-1] if libexpr.startswith('"') and libexpr.endswith('"') else consts.get(libexpr.upper(),f.parent.name+'.dll')
  mm=re.fullmatch(r'"([^"]+)"',parts[2])
  if not mm:un.append((str(f.relative_to(base)),ln,'symbol'));new.append(line);continue
  sym=mm.group(1); proto=protos.get(sym)
  if not proto:un.append((str(f.relative_to(base)),ln,'missing proto '+sym));new.append(line);continue
  ret,args,_=proto;actual=parts[3:]
  if len(actual)!=len(args):un.append((str(f.relative_to(base)),ln,f'argc {sym} {len(actual)}!={len(args)}'));new.append(line);continue
  arr=['DLL',f'"{dll}"',f'"{sym}"',ret,'CDECL']

  rep='CALL('+', '.join(arr)+')'

 if filechanged:

print('changed',len(changed),'unresolved',len(un),flush=True)

// uXBasic uxcollections.dll - GLib-backed collection wrapper
// Provides List/Stack/Queue/Deque/Dict/Set/Tree/Node primitives as DLL calls.
// Graph and DataFrame are intentionally separate modules: uxgraph (igraph) and uxdataframe (DuckDB/Arrow).
#include <glib.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#define UX_EXPORT __declspec(dllexport)
#else
#define UX_EXPORT
#endif

typedef enum { UXV_NULL=0, UXV_I64=1, UXV_F64=2, UXV_STR=3, UXV_PTR=4 } UXVKind;
typedef struct UXValue { UXVKind kind; int64_t i64; double f64; void* ptr; char* str; } UXValue;

typedef enum { C_LIST=1, C_STACK=2, C_QUEUE=3, C_DEQUE=4, C_DICT=5, C_SET=6, C_TREE=7, C_NODE=8 } UXCKind;
typedef struct UXCollection { UXCKind kind; void* obj; } UXCollection;

static UXValue* val_new_null(void){ UXValue* v=(UXValue*)g_malloc0(sizeof(UXValue)); return v; }
static UXValue* val_new_i64(int64_t x){ UXValue* v=val_new_null(); v->kind=UXV_I64; v->i64=x; return v; }
static UXValue* val_new_f64(double x){ UXValue* v=val_new_null(); v->kind=UXV_F64; v->f64=x; return v; }
static UXValue* val_new_ptr(void* x){ UXValue* v=val_new_null(); v->kind=UXV_PTR; v->ptr=x; return v; }
static UXValue* val_new_str(const char* s){ UXValue* v=val_new_null(); v->kind=UXV_STR; v->str=g_strdup(s?s:""); return v; }
static void val_free(gpointer p){ UXValue* v=(UXValue*)p; if(!v) return; if(v->kind==UXV_STR) g_free(v->str); g_free(v); }
static UXCollection* coll_new(UXCKind k, void* obj){ UXCollection* c=(UXCollection*)g_malloc0(sizeof(UXCollection)); c->kind=k; c->obj=obj; return c; }
static UXCollection* as_coll(void* h, UXCKind k){ UXCollection* c=(UXCollection*)h; if(!c || c->kind!=k) return NULL; return c; }
static GPtrArray* as_list(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_LIST); return c?(GPtrArray*)c->obj:NULL; }
static GQueue* as_queue_kind(void* h, UXCKind k){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,k); return c?(GQueue*)c->obj:NULL; }
static GHashTable* as_hash(void* h, UXCKind k){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,k); return c?(GHashTable*)c->obj:NULL; }
static GTree* as_tree(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_TREE); return c?(GTree*)c->obj:NULL; }
static int str_cmp(gconstpointer a, gconstpointer b, gpointer user_data){ (void)user_data; return g_strcmp0((const char*)a,(const char*)b); }
static void node_destroy(gpointer data){ val_free(data); }

UX_EXPORT int uxcollections_version(void){ return 1; }
UX_EXPORT int uxcollections_uses_glib(void){ return 1; }

// ---------- LIST ----------
UX_EXPORT void* uxc_list_new(void){ return coll_new(C_LIST, g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(val_free)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_list_free(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_LIST); if(!c) return; g_ptr_array_free((GPtrArray*)c->obj, TRUE); g_free(c); }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_list_count(void* h){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); return a?(int64_t)a->len:0; }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_list_clear(void* h){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(a) g_ptr_array_set_size(a,0); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_list_push_i64(void* h,int64_t x){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(a) g_ptr_array_add(a,val_new_i64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_list_push_f64(void* h,double x){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(a) g_ptr_array_add(a,val_new_f64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_list_push_str(void* h,const char* s){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(a) g_ptr_array_add(a,val_new_str(s)); }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_list_remove_at(void* h,int64_t idx){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(!a||idx<0||idx>=(int64_t)a->len) return 0; g_ptr_array_remove_index(a,(guint)idx); return 1; }
static UXValue* list_val(void* h,int64_t idx){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(!a||idx<0||idx>=(int64_t)a->len) return NULL; return (UXValue*)g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx); }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_list_get_i64(void* h,int64_t idx){ UXValue* v=list_val(h,idx); if(!v) return 0; if(v->kind==UXV_I64) return v->i64; if(v->kind==UXV_F64) return (int64_t)v->f64; return 0; }
UX_EXPORT double uxc_list_get_f64(void* h,int64_t idx){ UXValue* v=list_val(h,idx); if(!v) return 0; if(v->kind==UXV_F64) return v->f64; if(v->kind==UXV_I64) return (double)v->i64; return 0; }
UX_EXPORT const char* uxc_list_get_str(void* h,int64_t idx){ UXValue* v=list_val(h,idx); if(!v) return ""; if(v->kind==UXV_STR) return v->str?v->str:""; return ""; }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_list_set_i64(void* h,int64_t idx,int64_t x){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(!a||idx<0||idx>=(int64_t)a->len) return 0; val_free(g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx)); g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx)=val_new_i64(x); return 1; }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_list_set_f64(void* h,int64_t idx,double x){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(!a||idx<0||idx>=(int64_t)a->len) return 0; val_free(g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx)); g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx)=val_new_f64(x); return 1; }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_list_set_str(void* h,int64_t idx,const char* s){ GPtrArray* a=as_list(h); if(!a||idx<0||idx>=(int64_t)a->len) return 0; val_free(g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx)); g_ptr_array_index(a,(guint)idx)=val_new_str(s); return 1; }

// ---------- QUEUE / STACK / DEQUE ----------
static void free_queue_values(GQueue* q){ if(!q) return; while(!g_queue_is_empty(q)) val_free(g_queue_pop_head(q)); g_queue_free(q); }
UX_EXPORT void* uxc_stack_new(void){ return coll_new(C_STACK,g_queue_new()); }
UX_EXPORT void* uxc_queue_new(void){ return coll_new(C_QUEUE,g_queue_new()); }
UX_EXPORT void* uxc_deque_new(void){ return coll_new(C_DEQUE,g_queue_new()); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_stack_free(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_STACK); if(c){ free_queue_values((GQueue*)c->obj); g_free(c);} }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_queue_free(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_QUEUE); if(c){ free_queue_values((GQueue*)c->obj); g_free(c);} }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_deque_free(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_DEQUE); if(c){ free_queue_values((GQueue*)c->obj); g_free(c);} }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_stack_count(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_STACK); return q?g_queue_get_length(q):0; }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_queue_count(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_QUEUE); return q?g_queue_get_length(q):0; }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_deque_count(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_DEQUE); return q?g_queue_get_length(q):0; }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_stack_push_f64(void* h,double x){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_STACK); if(q) g_queue_push_head(q,val_new_f64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT double uxc_stack_pop_f64(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_STACK); if(!q||g_queue_is_empty(q)) return 0; UXValue* v=(UXValue*)g_queue_pop_head(q); double r=(v->kind==UXV_F64)?v->f64:(double)v->i64; val_free(v); return r; }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_queue_push_f64(void* h,double x){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_QUEUE); if(q) g_queue_push_tail(q,val_new_f64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT double uxc_queue_pop_f64(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_QUEUE); if(!q||g_queue_is_empty(q)) return 0; UXValue* v=(UXValue*)g_queue_pop_head(q); double r=(v->kind==UXV_F64)?v->f64:(double)v->i64; val_free(v); return r; }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_deque_push_front_f64(void* h,double x){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_DEQUE); if(q) g_queue_push_head(q,val_new_f64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_deque_push_back_f64(void* h,double x){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_DEQUE); if(q) g_queue_push_tail(q,val_new_f64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT double uxc_deque_pop_front_f64(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_DEQUE); if(!q||g_queue_is_empty(q)) return 0; UXValue* v=(UXValue*)g_queue_pop_head(q); double r=(v->kind==UXV_F64)?v->f64:(double)v->i64; val_free(v); return r; }
UX_EXPORT double uxc_deque_pop_back_f64(void* h){ GQueue* q=as_queue_kind(h,C_DEQUE); if(!q||g_queue_is_empty(q)) return 0; UXValue* v=(UXValue*)g_queue_pop_tail(q); double r=(v->kind==UXV_F64)?v->f64:(double)v->i64; val_free(v); return r; }

// ---------- DICT / SET ----------
UX_EXPORT void* uxc_dict_new(void){ return coll_new(C_DICT,g_hash_table_new_full(g_str_hash,g_str_equal,g_free,val_free)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_dict_free(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_DICT); if(c){ g_hash_table_destroy((GHashTable*)c->obj); g_free(c);} }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_dict_count(void* h){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); return t?g_hash_table_size(t):0; }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_dict_set_f64(void* h,const char* k,double x){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); if(t) g_hash_table_replace(t,g_strdup(k?k:""),val_new_f64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_dict_set_i64(void* h,const char* k,int64_t x){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); if(t) g_hash_table_replace(t,g_strdup(k?k:""),val_new_i64(x)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_dict_set_str(void* h,const char* k,const char* s){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); if(t) g_hash_table_replace(t,g_strdup(k?k:""),val_new_str(s)); }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_dict_has(void* h,const char* k){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); return t?g_hash_table_contains(t,k?k:""):0; }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_dict_remove(void* h,const char* k){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); return t?g_hash_table_remove(t,k?k:""):0; }
UX_EXPORT double uxc_dict_get_f64(void* h,const char* k){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); UXValue* v=t?g_hash_table_lookup(t,k?k:""):NULL; if(!v) return 0; return v->kind==UXV_F64?v->f64:(double)v->i64; }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_dict_get_i64(void* h,const char* k){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); UXValue* v=t?g_hash_table_lookup(t,k?k:""):NULL; if(!v) return 0; return v->kind==UXV_I64?v->i64:(int64_t)v->f64; }
UX_EXPORT const char* uxc_dict_get_str(void* h,const char* k){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_DICT); UXValue* v=t?g_hash_table_lookup(t,k?k:""):NULL; if(!v||v->kind!=UXV_STR) return ""; return v->str?v->str:""; }

UX_EXPORT void* uxc_set_new(void){ return coll_new(C_SET,g_hash_table_new_full(g_str_hash,g_str_equal,g_free,NULL)); }
UX_EXPORT void uxc_set_free(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_SET); if(c){ g_hash_table_destroy((GHashTable*)c->obj); g_free(c);} }

UX_EXPORT void uxc_set_add(void* h,const char* k){ GHashTable* t=as_hash(h,C_SET); if(t) g_hash_table_add(t,g_strdup(k?k:"")); }

UX_EXPORT void* uxc_node_append_child_str(void* h,const char* s){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_NODE); if(!c) return NULL; GNode* child=g_node_new(val_new_str(s)); g_node_append((GNode*)c->obj,child); return coll_new(C_NODE,child); }
UX_EXPORT int64_t uxc_node_child_count(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_NODE); return c?g_node_n_children((GNode*)c->obj):0; }
