UX_EXPORT const char* uxc_node_get_str(void* h){ UXCollection* c=as_coll(h,C_NODE); if(!c) return ""; GNode* n=(GNode*)c->obj; UXValue* v=(UXValue*)n->data; return (v&&v->kind==UXV_STR&&v->str)?v->str:""; }

// ---------- Future module gates ----------
UX_EXPORT int uxc_graph_backend_available(void){ return 0; }
UX_EXPORT int uxc_dataframe_backend_available(void){ return 0; }
Düzenlemeler ve iþlevsellik düþüncesi
C kaynak açýklamalarýndan koleksiyonlarý manuel olarak eþleþtirebiliriz, .c prototiplerini kullanarak dönüþüm iþlemini daha kolay hale getirebiliriz. Ancak matrislerle ilgili sorunlar devam ediyor, çünkü mevcut sarmalayýcýlarýn semantik hatalar içeriyor. Çözüm olarak yeni C iþlevleri ekleyebiliriz, örneðin QR için daha uygun olanlar.

Yapýlacak deðiþiklikler ve kaynak düzenlemeleri
Fonksiyon çaðrýsý VARPTR(qOut) ile geçmeli. Syntax, BYREF ile uyumlu görünüyor. QR fonksiyonunu ve diðer iþlevleri (SVD, Eigen) buna göre uyarlayabiliriz. Veri koleksiyonlarý üzerinde dönüþüm iþlemini daha basitleþtirip, C kaynaklarýný kullanarak yapýlan eþlemeleri manuel olarak dönüþtürmeye karar verdik.

Transformasyon ve kaynak deðiþiklikleri oluþturma
bash -lc cd /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base && sed -n '290,335p' src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs

        If gFfiX86ResolverEntries(i).argStackBytes <> gFfiX86ResolverEntries(i).argCount * 4 Then
            errText = "exec: x86 cleanup contract arg_stack_bytes mismatch"
            Return 0
        End If

        If conv = "STDCALL" Then
            If cleanup <> "CALLEE" Then
                errText = "exec: x86 cleanup contract stdcall must be CALLEE"
                Return 0
            End If
        Else
            If cleanup <> "CALLER" Then
                errText = "exec: x86 cleanup contract cdecl must be CALLER"
                Return 0
            End If
        End If
    Next i

    Return 1
End Function

#ifndef UXB_WIN32_LOADER_DECLARED
#define UXB_WIN32_LOADER_DECLARED
Declare Function LoadLibraryA Lib "kernel32" Alias "LoadLibraryA" (ByVal lpLibFileName As ZString Ptr) As Any Ptr
Declare Function GetProcAddress Lib "kernel32" Alias "GetProcAddress" (ByVal hModule As Any Ptr, ByVal lpProcName As ZString Ptr) As Any Ptr
#endif
Type ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32 As Function Cdecl () As Integer
Type ExecFnNoArgStdcallI32 As Function Stdcall () As Integer
Type ExecFnArg1CdeclI32 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg2CdeclI32 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Integer, ByVal a1 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg3CdeclI32 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Integer, ByVal a1 As Integer, ByVal a2 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg4CdeclI32 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Integer, ByVal a1 As Integer, ByVal a2 As Integer, ByVal a3 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg1StdcallI32 As Function Stdcall (ByVal a0 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg2StdcallI32 As Function Stdcall (ByVal a0 As Integer, ByVal a1 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg3StdcallI32 As Function Stdcall (ByVal a0 As Integer, ByVal a1 As Integer, ByVal a2 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnArg4StdcallI32 As Function Stdcall (ByVal a0 As Integer, ByVal a1 As Integer, ByVal a2 As Integer, ByVal a3 As Integer) As Integer
Type ExecFnNoArgCdeclU64 As Function Cdecl () As ULongInt
Type ExecFnArg1CdeclU64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As ULongInt) As ULongInt
Type ExecFnArg2CdeclU64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As ULongInt, ByVal a1 As ULongInt) As ULongInt
Type ExecFnArg3CdeclU64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As ULongInt, ByVal a1 As ULongInt, ByVal a2 As ULongInt) As ULongInt
Type ExecFnArg4CdeclU64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As ULongInt, ByVal a1 As ULongInt, ByVal a2 As ULongInt, ByVal a3 As ULongInt) As ULongInt
Type ExecFnNoArgCdeclF64 As Function Cdecl () As Double
Type ExecFnArg1CdeclF64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Double) As Double
Type ExecFnArg2CdeclF64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Double, ByVal a1 As Double) As Double
Type ExecFnArg3CdeclF64 As Function Cdecl (ByVal a0 As Double, ByVal a1 As Double, ByVal a2 As Double) As Double
Kaynak deðiþikliklerini dikkatlice yapma
Kaynak kodunda dikkatli deðiþiklikler yapmam gerekiyor. FreeBasic köprüsünde, struct kullanarak pointer dizileriyle iþlem yapmalý. Parametre türlerini belirtilen þekilde geçirebilirim. FreeBasic tipi için x64 uyumunu saðlamalýyým. Þu an zaten mevcut olan FFI çaðrýsýndaki sonuç yolunu takip edebiliriz. Patçayý oluþturmaya baþlayalým.

Patch için FreeBasic köprü ve yapý güncellemeleri
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,re,hashlib,os,textwrap
base=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base')
out=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb')

def write(rel, text):
 p=out/rel; p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); p.write_text(text,encoding='utf-8',newline='\n')

# 1) Parser: dynamic argument traversal, extended scalar types.
rel=Path('src/parser/parser/parser_stmt_dispatch.fbs')
t=(base/rel).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
t=t.replace('Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL"',
            'Case "VOID", "I32", "U32", "I64", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL"')
old='''    Dim argCount As Integer
    Dim argIdx(5) As Integer
    argIdx(0) = -1
    argIdx(1) = -1
    argIdx(2) = -1
    argIdx(3) = -1
    argIdx(4) = -1
    argIdx(5) = -1

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(callNode).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        If argCount <= 5 Then argIdx(argCount) = ch
        argCount += 1
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop
'''
new='''    ' CALL(DLL) has three/four metadata fields plus up to ten runtime arguments.
    ' Keep validation independent from a fixed six-element scratch array.
    Const UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS As Integer = 10
    Const UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN As Integer = UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS + 5

    Dim argCount As Integer
    Dim argIdx(0 To UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN - 1) As Integer
    Dim initIdx As Integer
    For initIdx = 0 To UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN - 1
        argIdx(initIdx) = -1
    Next initIdx

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(callNode).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        If argCount < UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then argIdx(argCount) = ch
        argCount += 1
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop

    If argCount > UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): supports at most 10 runtime arguments"
        Return 0
    End If
'''
if old not in t: raise SystemExit('parser block not found')
t=t.replace(old,new)
t=t.replace('If argStartPos > 5 Or argIdx(argStartPos) = -1 Then','If argStartPos >= UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Or argIdx(argStartPos) = -1 Then')
write(rel,t)

# 2) Runtime type support and bridge integration.
rel=Path('src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs')
t=(base/rel).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
t=t.replace('Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL", "CDECL", "STDCALL"',
            'Case "VOID", "I32", "U32", "I64", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL", "CDECL", "STDCALL"')
t=t.replace('Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF"',
            'Case "VOID", "I32", "U32", "I64", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF"')
# add validation cases
needle='''    Case "U64"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If argValue.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If
'''
repl='''    Case "U32", "U64"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) " & sig & " requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If argValue.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) " & sig & " argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "I64"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) I64 requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
'''
if needle not in t: raise SystemExit('runtime validation block missing')
t=t.replace(needle,repl)
# normalize mixed slot cases
needle='''        If t = "U64" And iv.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

        If t = "I32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CLng(iv.intValue))
        Else
            u64Out = CULngInt(iv.intValue)
        End If
'''
repl='''        If (t = "U32" Or t = "U64") And iv.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) " & t & " argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

        If t = "I32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CLng(iv.intValue))
        ElseIf t = "U32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CUInt(iv.intValue))
        Else
            u64Out = CULngInt(iv.intValue)
        End If
'''
if needle not in t: raise SystemExit('mixed slot block missing')
t=t.replace(needle,repl)
# Extend mixed arrays and limit, then call optional bridge.
t=t.replace('If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 4 Then Return 1\n\n    Dim retType As String\n    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(retTypeText, retType) = 0 Then Return 1',
'''If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 10 Then Return 1

    Dim retType As String
    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(retTypeText, retType) = 0 Then Return 1''',1)
t=t.replace('Dim argValuesU64(0 To 3) As ULongInt\n    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 3) As Double\n    Dim argStrings(0 To 3) As String',
            'Dim argValuesU64(0 To 9) As ULongInt\n    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 9) As Double\n    Dim argStrings(0 To 9) As String',1)
needle='''    If ExecX64DispatchMixedInvoke(procAddr, retType, argCount, argMask, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), valueOut, errText) = 0 Then
        Return 0
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function
'''
repl='''    ' Prefer the optional libffi bridge for five-to-ten arguments.  For
    ' zero-to-four arguments it may also be used when present; otherwise the
    ' established direct dispatcher remains the compatibility fallback.
    Dim bridgeInvoked As Integer
    Dim bridgeStatus As Integer
    bridgeStatus = ExecFfiBridge10Invoke(dllName, symbolName, retType, argTypes(), argCount, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argMask, valueOut, bridgeInvoked, errText)
    If bridgeStatus = 0 Then Return 0
    If bridgeInvoked <> 0 Then
        invokedOut = 1
        Return 1
    End If

    If argCount > 4 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) 5..10 mixed arguments require optional uxffi.dll"
        Return 0
    End If

    If ExecX64DispatchMixedInvoke(procAddr, retType, argCount, argMask, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), valueOut, errText) = 0 Then
        Return 0
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function
'''
if needle not in t: raise SystemExit('invoke tail missing')
t=t.replace(needle,repl,1)
# include bridge after loader declarations
needle='#endif\nType ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32'
t=t.replace(needle,'#endif\n#include once "exec_ffi_bridge10.fbs"\nType ExecFnNoArgCdeclI32',1)
write(rel,t)

# 3) New FreeBasic optional bridge loader.
write('src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_bridge10.fbs',r'''#ifndef UXB_EXEC_FFI_BRIDGE10_FBS
#define UXB_EXEC_FFI_BRIDGE10_FBS

' Optional libffi based bridge.  The compiler/interpreter does not require
' uxffi.dll for the legacy 0..4 argument path.  When installed, this bridge
' accepts mixed scalar/pointer signatures with at most ten arguments.

Const UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_ABI_VERSION As UInteger = 1
Const UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_MAX_ARGS As Integer = 10

Type UXBFfiBridge10Request
    abiVersion As UInteger
    structSize As UInteger
    argCount As UInteger
    flags As UInteger
    dllName As ZString Ptr
    symbolName As ZString Ptr
    returnType As ZString Ptr
    argTypesCsv As ZString Ptr
    u64Args As ULongInt Ptr
    f64Args As Double Ptr
    f64Mask As ULongInt
    resultU64 As ULongInt
    resultF64 As Double
    resultKind As UInteger
    statusCode As Integer
    errorText As ZString * 512
End Type

Type UXBFfiBridge10InvokeFn As Function Cdecl(ByVal requestPtr As UXBFfiBridge10Request Ptr) As Integer

Dim Shared gUXBFfiBridge10Tried As Integer
Dim Shared gUXBFfiBridge10Module As Any Ptr
Dim Shared gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke As UXBFfiBridge10InvokeFn

Private Sub ExecFfiBridge10LoadIfNeeded()
    If gUXBFfiBridge10Tried <> 0 Then Exit Sub
    gUXBFfiBridge10Tried = 1
    gUXBFfiBridge10Module = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr("uxffi.dll"))
    If gUXBFfiBridge10Module = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(gUXBFfiBridge10Module, StrPtr("uxffi_invoke_v1"))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        gUXBFfiBridge10Module = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke = procAddr
End Sub

Private Function ExecFfiBridge10Invoke( _
    ByRef dllName As String, _
    ByRef symbolName As String, _
    ByRef returnType As String, _
    argTypes() As String, _
    ByVal argCount As Integer, _
    u64Args() As ULongInt, _
    f64Args() As Double, _
    ByVal f64Mask As Integer, _
    ByRef valueOut As Integer, _
    ByRef invokedOut As Integer, _
    ByRef errText As String _
) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_MAX_ARGS Then
        errText = "exec: uxffi bridge supports at most 10 arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    ExecFfiBridge10LoadIfNeeded
    If gUXBFfiBridge10Module = 0 Or gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim csv As String
    csv = ""
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        If i > 0 Then csv &= ","
        csv &= UCase(Trim(argTypes(i)))
    Next i

    Dim req As UXBFfiBridge10Request
    req.abiVersion = UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_ABI_VERSION
    req.structSize = SizeOf(UXBFfiBridge10Request)
    req.argCount = argCount
    req.flags = 0
    req.dllName = StrPtr(dllName)
    req.symbolName = StrPtr(symbolName)
    req.returnType = StrPtr(returnType)
    req.argTypesCsv = StrPtr(csv)
    If argCount > 0 Then
        req.u64Args = @u64Args(0)
        req.f64Args = @f64Args(0)
    Else
        req.u64Args = 0
        req.f64Args = 0
    End If
    req.f64Mask = CULngInt(f64Mask)

    Dim rc As Integer
    rc = gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke(@req)
    If rc = 0 Or req.statusCode <> 0 Then
        errText = "exec: uxffi bridge failed"
        If req.errorText <> "" Then errText &= ": " & req.errorText
        Return 0
    End If

    Select Case UCase(Trim(returnType))
    Case "F64"
        valueOut = CInt(req.resultF64)
    Case Else
        valueOut = CInt(req.resultU64)
    End Select
    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

#endif
''')

# 4) C/libffi bridge.
write('runtime_ext/uxffi/uxffi.h',r'''#ifndef UXFFI_H
#define UXFFI_H
#include <stdint.h>
#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif
#if defined(_WIN32)
#define UXFFI_API __declspec(dllexport)
#else
#define UXFFI_API
#endif
#define UXFFI_ABI_VERSION 1u
#define UXFFI_MAX_ARGS 10u

typedef struct uxffi_request_v1 {
    uint32_t abi_version;
    uint32_t struct_size;
    uint32_t arg_count;
    uint32_t flags;
    const char *dll_name;
    const char *symbol_name;
    const char *return_type;
    const char *arg_types_csv;
    const uint64_t *u64_args;
    const double *f64_args;
    uint64_t f64_mask;
    uint64_t result_u64;
    double result_f64;
    uint32_t result_kind;
    int32_t status_code;
    char error_text[512];
} uxffi_request_v1;

UXFFI_API int uxffi_version(void);
UXFFI_API int uxffi_invoke_v1(uxffi_request_v1 *request);
#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
#endif
''')
write('runtime_ext/uxffi/uxffi.c',r'''#include "uxffi.h"
#include <ffi.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <ctype.h>

static void set_error(uxffi_request_v1 *r, int code, const char *msg) {
    if (!r) return;
    r->status_code = code;
    snprintf(r->error_text, sizeof(r->error_text), "%s", msg ? msg : "unknown error");
}

static int eqi(const char *a, const char *b) {
    if (!a || !b) return 0;
    while (*a && *b) {
        if (toupper((unsigned char)*a) != toupper((unsigned char)*b)) return 0;
        ++a; ++b;
    }
    return *a == 0 && *b == 0;
}

static ffi_type *ffi_type_for(const char *name) {
    if (eqi(name,"I32")) return &ffi_type_sint32;
    if (eqi(name,"U32")) return &ffi_type_uint32;
    if (eqi(name,"I64")) return &ffi_type_sint64;
    if (eqi(name,"U64")) return &ffi_type_uint64;
    if (eqi(name,"F64")) return &ffi_type_double;
    if (eqi(name,"PTR") || eqi(name,"STRPTR") || eqi(name,"BYREF")) return &ffi_type_pointer;
    if (eqi(name,"VOID")) return &ffi_type_void;
    return NULL;
}

static int parse_types(const char *csv, char tokens[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS][16], uint32_t expected) {
    uint32_t count = 0;
    const char *p = csv ? csv : "";
    while (*p && count < UXFFI_MAX_ARGS) {
        while (*p == ' ' || *p == '\t' || *p == ',') ++p;
        if (!*p) break;
        size_t n = 0;
        while (*p && *p != ',') {
            if (!isspace((unsigned char)*p) && n + 1 < 16) tokens[count][n++] = *p;
            ++p;
        }
        tokens[count][n] = 0;
        ++count;
    }
    return count == expected ? 1 : 0;
}

UXFFI_API int uxffi_version(void) { return 1; }

UXFFI_API int uxffi_invoke_v1(uxffi_request_v1 *r) {
    if (!r) return 0;
    r->status_code = 0; r->error_text[0] = 0; r->result_u64 = 0; r->result_f64 = 0.0; r->result_kind = 0;
    if (r->abi_version != UXFFI_ABI_VERSION || r->struct_size < sizeof(*r)) { set_error(r,1,"ABI/struct mismatch"); return 0; }
    if (r->arg_count > UXFFI_MAX_ARGS) { set_error(r,2,"too many arguments"); return 0; }
    if (!r->dll_name || !r->symbol_name || !r->return_type) { set_error(r,3,"missing target metadata"); return 0; }

    HMODULE mod = LoadLibraryA(r->dll_name);
    if (!mod) { set_error(r,4,"LoadLibraryA failed"); return 0; }
    FARPROC proc = GetProcAddress(mod, r->symbol_name);
    if (!proc) { set_error(r,5,"GetProcAddress failed"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }

    char tokens[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS][16] = {{0}};
    if (r->arg_count && !parse_types(r->arg_types_csv,tokens,r->arg_count)) { set_error(r,6,"argument type count mismatch"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }

    ffi_type *atypes[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    void *avalues[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    int32_t i32v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS]; uint32_t u32v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    int64_t i64v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS]; uint64_t u64v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    double f64v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS]; void *ptrv[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];

    for (uint32_t i=0;i<r->arg_count;i++) {
        atypes[i] = ffi_type_for(tokens[i]);
        if (!atypes[i] || atypes[i] == &ffi_type_void) { set_error(r,7,"invalid argument type"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }
        if (eqi(tokens[i],"I32")) { i32v[i]=(int32_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&i32v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"U32")) { u32v[i]=(uint32_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&u32v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"I64")) { i64v[i]=(int64_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&i64v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"U64")) { u64v[i]=r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&u64v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"F64")) { f64v[i]=r->f64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&f64v[i]; }
        else { ptrv[i]=(void*)(uintptr_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&ptrv[i]; }
    }

    ffi_type *rtype=ffi_type_for(r->return_type);
    if (!rtype) { set_error(r,8,"invalid return type"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }
    ffi_cif cif;
    if (ffi_prep_cif(&cif,FFI_DEFAULT_ABI,r->arg_count,rtype,atypes)!=FFI_OK) { set_error(r,9,"ffi_prep_cif failed"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }

    union { int32_t i32; uint32_t u32; int64_t i64; uint64_t u64; double f64; void *ptr; } result;
    memset(&result,0,sizeof(result));
    ffi_call(&cif,FFI_FN(proc),rtype==&ffi_type_void?NULL:&result,avalues);
    if (rtype==&ffi_type_double) { r->result_f64=result.f64; r->result_kind=2; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_pointer) { r->result_u64=(uint64_t)(uintptr_t)result.ptr; r->result_kind=3; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_sint32) { r->result_u64=(uint64_t)(int64_t)result.i32; r->result_kind=1; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_uint32) { r->result_u64=result.u32; r->result_kind=1; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_sint64) { r->result_u64=(uint64_t)result.i64; r->result_kind=1; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_uint64) { r->result_u64=result.u64; r->result_kind=1; }
    FreeLibrary(mod);
    return 1;
}
''')
write('runtime_ext/uxffi/build_uxffi_dll.bat',r'''@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0\..\..\.."
set "GCC=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\gcc.exe"
set "PKG=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\pkg-config.exe"
set "LEGACY_OUT=uxb\dist\runtime_ext"
set "CANON_OUT=uxb\dist\libraries\bin"
if not exist "%GCC%" (
  echo ERROR: MSYS2 UCRT64 gcc not found: %GCC%
  exit /b 2
)
if not exist "%PKG%" (
  echo ERROR: pkg-config not found: %PKG%
  exit /b 2
)
for /f "usebackq tokens=*" %%I in (`"%PKG%" --cflags --libs libffi`) do set "FFIFLAGS=%%I"
if "%FFIFLAGS%"=="" (
  echo ERROR: libffi not found. Install: pacman -S mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-libffi pkgconf
  exit /b 2
)
if not exist "%LEGACY_OUT%" mkdir "%LEGACY_OUT%"
if not exist "%CANON_OUT%" mkdir "%CANON_OUT%"
"%GCC%" -O2 -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -shared -o "%LEGACY_OUT%\uxffi.dll" "uxb\runtime_ext\uxffi\uxffi.c" %FFIFLAGS%
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
copy /y "%LEGACY_OUT%\uxffi.dll" "%CANON_OUT%\uxffi.dll" >nul
copy /y "C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\libffi-8.dll" "%CANON_OUT%\libffi-8.dll" >nul 2>nul
echo OK: %CANON_OUT%\uxffi.dll
endlocal
''')

  'build':'build/libraries','binary_output':'dist/libraries/bin','dependency_output':'dist/libraries/deps','logs':'dist/libraries/logs'},
 'compatibility':{'native_source':'runtime_ext','wrapper_source':'libsx','headers':'include/modules','legacy_binary_output':'dist/runtime_ext'},
 'max_ffi_args':10,
 'libraries':[]}
items=[
 ('uxffi','CORE','runtime_ext/uxffi/build_uxffi_dll.bat','uxffi.dll','libffi'),

 manifest['libraries'].append({'name':n,'tier':tier,'build_script':build,'dll':dll,'dependencies':deps,'wrapper':f'libsx/{n}/{n}.bas','header':f'include/modules/{n}.uxmh'})

write('libraries/README.md',r'''# uXBasic kütüphane düzeni

Bu dizin kütüphanelerin kanonik görünümüdür. `APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.ps1` þu baðlantýlarý oluþturur:

- `libraries/native` ? `runtime_ext`
- `libraries/wrappers` ? `libsx`
- `libraries/include` ? `include/modules`

Eski yollar kaldýrýlmaz; böylece mevcut build ve smoke betikleri bozulmaz. Yeni DLL çýktýlarý `dist/libraries/bin`, baðýmlýlýklar `dist/libraries/deps`, loglar `dist/libraries/logs` altýnda toplanýr. `dist/runtime_ext` geriye uyumluluk staging alanýdýr.
''')

write('tools/library_tools/uxb_library_build.py',r'''#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, json, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

def root_from_here() -> Path:
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description="uXBasic birleþik DLL üreticisi")
    ap.add_argument('--tier',choices=['core','all'],default='core')
    ap.add_argument('--library',action='append',default=[])
    ap.add_argument('--strict-optional',action='store_true')
    ap.add_argument('--no-build',action='store_true')
    ns=ap.parse_args()
    root=root_from_here(); manifest=json.loads((root/'libraries/library_manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    out=root/'dist/libraries/bin'; logs=root/'dist/libraries/logs'; legacy=root/'dist/runtime_ext'
    out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); logs.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); legacy.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    selected=[]
    names=set(ns.library)
    for item in manifest['libraries']:
        if names and item['name'] not in names: continue
        if not names and ns.tier=='core' and item['tier']!='CORE': continue
        selected.append(item)
    rows=[]; hard_fail=0
    for item in selected:
        start=time.time(); script=root/item['build_script']; dll=item['dll']; rc=None; status=''
        log=logs/f"{item['name']}.log"
        if ns.no_build:
            status='SKIPPED'
        elif not script.exists():
            status='SCRIPT_MISSING'; rc=127
        else:
            with log.open('w',encoding='utf-8',errors='replace') as fp:
                cp=subprocess.run(['cmd','/c',str(script)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            rc=cp.returncode; status='BUILT' if rc==0 else 'BUILD_FAILED'
        src=legacy/dll
        if src.exists():
            shutil.copy2(src,out/dll)
            if status=='SKIPPED':status='EXISTS'
        elif status=='BUILT': status='OUTPUT_MISSING'
        required=item['tier']=='CORE' or ns.strict_optional
        if required and status not in ('BUILT','EXISTS'): hard_fail+=1
        rows.append({**item,'status':status,'exit_code':rc,'seconds':round(time.time()-start,3),'output':str(out/dll),'log':str(log)})
        print(f"{item['name']}: {status}")
    report={'schema':'uxb.library_build_report.v1','ok':hard_fail==0,'tier':ns.tier,'hard_failures':hard_fail,'results':rows}
    (root/'dist/libraries/library_build_report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
    return 0 if hard_fail==0 else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())
''')
write('compiler/scripts/build_libraries.bat',r'''@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0\..\..\.."
python uxb\tools\library_tools\uxb_library_build.py %*
exit /b %errorlevel%
''')
write('compiler/scripts/build_libraries.ps1',r'''param([ValidateSet("core","all")][string]$Tier="core",[string[]]$Library=@(),[switch]$StrictOptional,[switch]$NoBuild)
$ErrorActionPreference="Stop"
$repo=(Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\..\..")).Path
$args=@("uxb\tools\library_tools\uxb_library_build.py","--tier",$Tier)
foreach($n in $Library){$args += @("--library",$n)}
if($StrictOptional){$args += "--strict-optional"}
if($NoBuild){$args += "--no-build"}
Push-Location $repo
try { & python @args; exit $LASTEXITCODE } finally { Pop-Location }
''')

# 6) Patch application scripts create junctions and can build.
write('../APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.ps1',r'''param([string]$ProjectRoot="",[switch]$BuildCore,[switch]$BuildAll)
$ErrorActionPreference="Stop"
$patchRoot=Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ProjectRoot)){$ProjectRoot=(Get-Location).Path}
$uxb=Join-Path $ProjectRoot "uxb"
if(!(Test-Path $uxb)){throw "uxb klasoru bulunamadi: $uxb"}
$payload=Join-Path $patchRoot "uxb"
$stamp=Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"
$backup=Join-Path $uxb "patch_backups\library_layout_$stamp"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $backup | Out-Null
Get-ChildItem $payload -Recurse -File | ForEach-Object {
  $rel=$_.FullName.Substring($payload.Length).TrimStart('\')
  $dst=Join-Path $uxb $rel
  if(Test-Path $dst){$bak=Join-Path $backup $rel; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $bak) | Out-Null; Copy-Item $dst $bak -Force}
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $dst) | Out-Null
  Copy-Item $_.FullName $dst -Force
}
$links=@{native="runtime_ext";wrappers="libsx";include="include\modules"}
foreach($name in $links.Keys){
  $link=Join-Path $uxb "libraries\$name"; $target=Join-Path $uxb $links[$name]
  if(!(Test-Path $link)){
    try { New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path $link -Target $target | Out-Null }
    catch { Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 (Join-Path (Split-Path $link) ($name+".path")) $target }
  }
}
Write-Host "PATCH OK. Backup: $backup"
if($BuildAll){& (Join-Path $uxb "compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1") -Tier all; exit $LASTEXITCODE}
if($BuildCore){& (Join-Path $uxb "compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1") -Tier core; exit $LASTEXITCODE}
''')
write('../APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.bat',r'''@echo off
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.ps1" %*
exit /b %errorlevel%
''')

# 7) Static ABI audit.
write('tools/library_tools/uxb_library_abi_audit.py',r'''#!/usr/bin/env python3
from pathlib import Path
import re,json,sys
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
valid={'VOID','I32','U32','I64','U64','F64','PTR','STRPTR','BYREF','BYVAL'}
rows=[];bad=0
for f in sorted((root/'libsx').glob('*/*.bas')):
    for no,line in enumerate(f.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').splitlines(),1):
        if line.lstrip().startswith("'"): continue
        p=line.upper().find('CALL(DLL,')
        if p<0: continue
        # Contract check only: literal dll/symbol + return token + optional CDECL + type list.
        ok=bool(re.search(r'CALL\(DLL,\s*"[^"]+",\s*"[^"]+",\s*(VOID|I32|U32|I64|U64|F64|PTR|STRPTR|BYREF|BYVAL)\b',line,re.I))
        rows.append({'file':str(f.relative_to(root)),'line':no,'ok':ok,'text':line.strip()})
        if not ok: bad+=1
out=root/'dist/libraries';out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
(out/'wrapper_abi_audit.json').write_text(json.dumps({'schema':'uxb.wrapper_abi_audit.v1','ok':bad==0,'bad':bad,'rows':rows},indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
print(f'CALL(DLL)={len(rows)} bad={bad}')
raise SystemExit(0 if bad==0 else 1)
''')

# 8) Manual repair uxm/collections wrappers.
# Collections explicit based on C implementation.
coll=(base/'libsx/uxcollections/uxcollections.bas').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
proto={
'uxcollections_version':('I32',[]),'uxc_list_new':('PTR',[]),'uxc_list_free':('VOID',['PTR']),
'uxc_list_count':('I64',['PTR']),'uxc_list_push_f64':('VOID',['PTR','F64']),'uxc_list_get_f64':('F64',['PTR','I64']),
'uxc_stack_new':('PTR',[]),'uxc_stack_free':('VOID',['PTR']),'uxc_stack_push_f64':('VOID',['PTR','F64']),'uxc_stack_pop_f64':('F64',['PTR']),
'uxc_queue_new':('PTR',[]),'uxc_queue_free':('VOID',['PTR']),'uxc_queue_push_f64':('VOID',['PTR','F64']),'uxc_queue_pop_f64':('F64',['PTR']),
'uxc_dict_new':('PTR',[]),'uxc_dict_free':('VOID',['PTR']),'uxc_dict_set_f64':('VOID',['PTR','STRPTR','F64']),'uxc_dict_get_f64':('F64',['PTR','STRPTR']),'uxc_dict_has':('I32',['PTR','STRPTR']),
'uxc_set_new':('PTR',[]),'uxc_set_free':('VOID',['PTR']),'uxc_set_add':('VOID',['PTR','STRPTR']),'uxc_set_contains':('I32',['PTR','STRPTR']),
'uxc_tree_new':('PTR',[]),'uxc_tree_free':('VOID',['PTR']),'uxc_tree_set_f64':('VOID',['PTR','STRPTR','F64']),'uxc_tree_get_f64':('F64',['PTR','STRPTR'])}
def split_top(s):
 r=[];c=[];d=0;q=False
 for ch in s:
  if ch=='"':q=not q;c.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch=='(':d+=1;c.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch==')':d-=1;c.append(ch)
  elif not q and ch==',' and d==0:r.append(''.join(c).strip());c=[]
  else:c.append(ch)
 r.append(''.join(c).strip());return r
ls=[]
for line in coll.splitlines():
 pos=line.upper().find('CALL(DLL,')
 if pos<0 or line.lstrip().startswith("'"):ls.append(line);continue
 p=line.find('(',pos);d=0;q=False;end=None
 for i in range(p,len(line)):
  ch=line[i]
  if ch=='"':q=not q
  elif not q and ch=='(':d+=1
  elif not q and ch==')':
   d-=1
   if d==0:end=i;break
 parts=split_top(line[p+1:end]); sym=parts[2].strip('"'); ret,args=proto[sym];actual=parts[3:]
 arr=['DLL','"uxcollections.dll"',f'"{sym}"',ret,'CDECL']
 if args:arr += ['"'+','.join(args)+'"']+actual
 ls.append(line[:pos]+'CALL('+', '.join(arr)+')'+line[end+1:])
write('libsx/uxcollections/uxcollections.bas','\n'.join(ls)+'\n')

# Matrix use already automatically transformed output if exists, then replace bad three routines.
matrix_path=out/'libsx/uxmatrix/uxmatrix.bas'
mt=(matrix_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8') if matrix_path.exists() else (base/'libsx/uxmatrix/uxmatrix.bas').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
mt=re.sub(r'FUNCTION QR\(a AS U64\) AS I32\s+RETURN CALL\(DLL, UXMATRIX_DLL, "uxmatrix_qr", a\)\s+END FUNCTION',
'''FUNCTION QR(a AS U64, BYREF qOut AS U64, BYREF rOut AS U64) AS I32
    RETURN CALL(DLL, "uxmatrix.dll", "uxmatrix_qr", I32, CDECL, "PTR,PTR,PTR", a, VARPTR(qOut), VARPTR(rOut))
END FUNCTION''',mt,flags=re.S|re.I)
mt=re.sub(r'FUNCTION SVD\(a AS U64\) AS I32\s+RETURN CALL\(DLL, UXMATRIX_DLL, "uxmatrix_svd", a\)\s+END FUNCTION',
'''FUNCTION SVD(a AS U64, BYREF uOut AS U64, BYREF sOut AS U64, BYREF vtOut AS U64) AS I32
    RETURN CALL(DLL, "uxmatrix.dll", "uxmatrix_svd", I32, CDECL, "PTR,PTR,PTR,PTR", a, VARPTR(uOut), VARPTR(sOut), VARPTR(vtOut))
END FUNCTION''',mt,flags=re.S|re.I)
mt=re.sub(r'FUNCTION EigenSymmetric\(a AS U64\) AS I32\s+RETURN CALL\(DLL, UXMATRIX_DLL, "uxmatrix_eigen_symmetric", a\)\s+END FUNCTION',
'''FUNCTION EigenSymmetric(a AS U64, BYREF valuesOut AS U64, BYREF vectorsOut AS U64) AS I32
    RETURN CALL(DLL, "uxmatrix.dll", "uxmatrix_eigen_symmetric", I32, CDECL, "PTR,PTR,PTR", a, VARPTR(valuesOut), VARPTR(vectorsOut))
END FUNCTION''',mt,flags=re.S|re.I)
write('libsx/uxmatrix/uxmatrix.bas',mt)

# 9) Add user-facing patch notes.
write('../PATCH_NOTES.md',r'''# uXBasic kütüphane düzeni ve ABI10 yamasý

Bu ZIP yalnýz yeni/deðiþen dosyalarý içerir. Proje köküne çýkarýldýðýnda `uxb/` üzerine yazýlýr.

## Saðlananlar

- Kanonik kütüphane görünümü: `libraries/native`, `libraries/wrappers`, `libraries/include` junctionlarý.
- Birleþik DLL üreticisi: `compiler/scripts/build_libraries.ps1|bat`.
- Kanonik binary dizini: `dist/libraries/bin`.
- Mevcut `dist/runtime_ext` geriye uyumlu staging alaný olarak korunur.
- Wrapper `CALL(DLL)` imzalarý literal DLL/sembol, dönüþ tipi, CDECL ve argüman tip listesiyle üretilmiþtir.
- Parser metadata alanlarýndan sonra en fazla **10 gerçek DLL argümanýný** kabul eder.
- Ýsteðe baðlý `uxffi.dll` libffi köprüsü, x64 karma tipli 5–10 argüman çaðrýlarýný saðlar.
- `uxffi.dll` yoksa mevcut 0–4 argüman doðrudan çaðrý yolu çalýþmaya devam eder.

## Uygulama

```powershell
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.ps1 -ProjectRoot C:\...\UXBc_Clean\UXBc
Çekirdek DLL’leri de üretmek için -BuildCore, tümünü denemek için -BuildAll eklenir.

Gereksinim
uxffi.dll için MSYS2 UCRT64 ortamýnda mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-libffi ve pkgconf gerekir. Harici aðýr kütüphaneler kendi baðýmlýlýklarýný korur.

Bilinen sýnýr
Bu yama FFI çaðrý taþýmasýný 10 argümana çýkarýr. AST interpreter’ýn F64/U64/STRPTR dönüþlerini genel ExecValue üzerinden eksiksiz taþýmasý ayrý bir yürütücü dönüþ-deðeri sprintidir; mevcut kaynakta bu dönüþler birçok yolda Integer üzerinden geçmektedir.
''')

print('files generated')

Parser doðrulamasýný düzeltme
Parser'ýn en fazla çocuk sayýsý sorununu kontrol etmem gerekiyor. Metadata alanlarý ile tip listesine dikkat etmedim. Þu an en fazla 15 çocuk destekleniyor, ancak runtime argümanlarý 10 ile sýnýrlý. Daha doðru dinamik bir kontrol eklemem gerek; bunun için argüman baþlangýç noktasýný ve tip listesini dikkate alacaðým.

Parser doðrulamasýndaki iyileþtirmeler
Eðer parametrelerde virgül yoksa, token'ý normalleþtirip geçerli ise baþarýyla ayarlayýp döndürüyoruz; deðilse, 0 döndürüyoruz. String argümanlarla ilgili sorunlar giderildi. FreeBasic struct uyumlarý, ZString karþýlaþtýrmalarý ve potansiyel fonksiyon göstergesi karþýlaþtýrma sorunlarý için kontrolleri yapacaðým.

Düþünceler üzerinde revizyon
Mevcut derleyici backend'inin nasýl çalýþtýðýný gözden geçireceðim. Geriye dönük çözümler için, eksik bir FP kütüphanesini manifestoya eklemem gerektiðini fark ettim. Ayrýca, ana çalýþma zamaný uxb_runtime.dll ile ilgili eksiklikleri de araþtýrmam gerekebilir.

Derlemeyi kontrol etme
Adým adým ilerlemem gerekiyor. Derleme betikleri ve kök varsayýmlarý gözden geçirmem gerek. Linux üzerinde C köprü derlemesini yapabilir miyim? Windows baþlýklarý eriþilemez, ancak belki mingw çapraz derleyicisiyle statik sözdizimi ile yapýlabilir. Kurulumlarý kontrol etmeliyim.

Kontrol edilen derleyici ve baðýmlýlýklar
bash -lc command -v x86_64-w64-mingw32-gcc || true; command -v gcc; pkg-config --modversion libffi 2>/dev/null || true; command -v fbc || true; command -v pwsh || true
/usr/bin/gcc
Gözden geçirme ve düzeltme adýmlarý
Windows dýþý derleme yapýlabilir mi? dlfcn desteði eklenebilir mi? Ancak hedef Windows. Derleme hatalarýný düzeltmeliyim, örneðin tek tokenli tür listesi, analiz çalýþma zamanýný sayma, FP kütüphaneleri için manifest ve yapýlandýrma yollarý. Ayrýca eski komut çalýþtýrma davranýþlarýný gözden geçirmek gerek.

Devam etme ve düzeltme yapma süreci
Sanýrým kullanýcýnýn þu an için oluþturulmasý talebini açýkça sormadýðýna odaklanmamalýyým, ama yine de makul olarak bunu yapmalýyýz. Runtime sayýsýný ve tek-token tür listesini düzelterek, FP manifesti ve yedek baðýmlýlýklarý da eklemeliyiz. Ayrýca, manifesti düzelterek uxstats2 wrapper yapmamýz gerek.

Yamalarý uygulama ve yapýlandýrma düzeltmeleri
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,re
root=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb')
# parser runtime count precise
p=root/'src/parser/parser/parser_stmt_dispatch.fbs'
t=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
# remove early broad max block and replace with metadata hard cap only
old='''    If argCount > UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): supports at most 10 runtime arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    If argCount < 4 Then
'''
new='''    If argCount > UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): call metadata plus arguments exceed the ABI10 limit"
        Return 0
    End If

    If argCount < 4 Then
'''
t=t.replace(old,new)
needle='''    If signatureText = "BYREF" Then
        If argStartPos >= UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Or argIdx(argStartPos) = -1 Then
'''
insert='''    Dim runtimeArgStartPos As Integer
    runtimeArgStartPos = argStartPos
    ' An optional string immediately after CDECL/STDCALL is the per-argument
    ' type list.  A single type token is valid as well as comma-separated lists.
    If runtimeArgStartPos < argCount Then
        Dim typeListNode As Integer
        typeListNode = argIdx(runtimeArgStartPos)
        If typeListNode <> -1 And UCase(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).kind) = "STRING" Then
            Dim typeListText As String
            typeListText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).value))
            If typeListText = "I32" Or typeListText = "U32" Or typeListText = "I64" Or typeListText = "U64" Or _
               typeListText = "F64" Or typeListText = "PTR" Or typeListText = "STRPTR" Or typeListText = "BYREF" Or _
               Instr(typeListText, ",") > 0 Then
                runtimeArgStartPos += 1
            End If
        End If
    End If

    If argCount - runtimeArgStartPos > UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): supports at most 10 runtime arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    If signatureText = "BYREF" Then
        argStartPos = runtimeArgStartPos
        If argStartPos >= UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Or argIdx(argStartPos) = -1 Then
'''
if needle not in t: raise SystemExit('needle parser byref not found')
t=t.replace(needle,insert)
p.write_text(t,encoding='utf-8')

# runtime single-token type list and arg count limit message
p=root/'src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs'; t=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
old='''    src = Trim(listText)
    If src = "" Or Instr(src, ",") = 0 Then Return 0

    Dim scanPos As Integer
'''
new='''    src = Trim(listText)
    If src = "" Then Return 0

    ' A one-argument signature such as "PTR" is a real type list.  Treat a
    ' non-type string as the first runtime string argument instead.
    If Instr(src, ",") = 0 Then
        Dim singleType As String
        If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(src, singleType) = 0 Then Return 0
        If UBound(argTypes) < 0 Then Return -1
        argTypes(0) = singleType
        argTypeCount = 1
        Return 1
    End If

    Dim scanPos As Integer
'''
if old not in t: raise SystemExit('single list block missing')
t=t.replace(old,new)
p.write_text(t,encoding='utf-8')

# Manifest: add FP and correct wrappers/fallback/deps scripts
p=root/'libraries/library_manifest.json'; m=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
existing={x['name'] for x in m['libraries']}
fp=[
 {'name':'uxb_fp80','tier':'CORE','build_script':'runtime_ext/fp80/build_fp80_dll.bat','dll':'uxb_fp80.dll','dependencies':'gcc,long-double','wrapper':'','header':'runtime_ext/fp80/uxb_fp80.bi'},
 {'name':'uxb_fp128','tier':'CORE','build_script':'runtime_ext/fp128/build_fp128_dll.bat','dll':'uxb_fp128.dll','dependencies':'gcc,quadmath','wrapper':'','header':'runtime_ext/fp128/uxb_fp128.h'},
 {'name':'uxb_bigfp','tier':'CORE','build_script':'runtime_ext/bigfp/build_bigfp_dll.bat','dll':'uxb_bigfp.dll','dependencies':'gcc,mpfr,gmp','wrapper':'','header':'runtime_ext/bigfp/uxb_bigfp.h'},
]
m['libraries']=fp+m['libraries']
fallbacks={
 'uxaimath':'runtime_ext/uxaimath/build_uxaimath_fallback_dll.bat','uxgraph':'runtime_ext/uxgraph/build_uxgraph_fallback_dll.bat',
 'uxmatrix':'runtime_ext/uxmatrix/build_uxmatrix_fallback_dll.bat','uxnn':'runtime_ext/uxnn/build_uxnn_fallback_dll.bat',
 'uxtensor':'runtime_ext/uxtensor/build_uxtensor_fallback_dll.bat'}
copydeps={
 'uxaimath':'runtime_ext/uxaimath/copy_uxaimath_runtime_deps.bat','uxdataframe':'runtime_ext/uxdataframe/copy_uxdataframe_runtime_deps.bat',
 'uxdataset':'runtime_ext/uxdataset/copy_uxdataset_runtime_deps.bat','uxllama':'runtime_ext/uxllama/copy_uxllama_runtime_deps.bat',
 'uxnn':'runtime_ext/uxnn/copy_uxnn_runtime_deps.bat','uxnn2':'runtime_ext/uxnn2/copy_uxnn2_runtime_deps.bat',
 'uxnn3':'runtime_ext/uxnn3/copy_uxnn3_runtime_deps.bat','uxonnx':'runtime_ext/uxonnx/copy_uxonnx_runtime_deps.bat',
 'uxonnx2':'runtime_ext/uxonnx2/copy_uxonnx2_runtime_deps.bat','uxtensor':'runtime_ext/uxtensor/copy_uxtensor_runtime_deps.bat'}
for x in m['libraries']:
 if x['name'] in fallbacks:x['fallback_script']=fallbacks[x['name']]
 if x['name'] in copydeps:x['copy_dependencies_script']=copydeps[x['name']]
 if x['name']=='uxstats2':x['wrapper']='libsx/uxstats/uxstats2.bas'
 if x['name']=='uxffi':x['wrapper']='';x['header']='runtime_ext/uxffi/uxffi.h'
p.write_text(json.dumps(m,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')

# Build tool fallback/dependency staging and cmd.exe robust invocation
p=root/'tools/library_tools/uxb_library_build.py'; t=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
t=t.replace("cp=subprocess.run(['cmd','/c',str(script)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)\n            rc=cp.returncode; status='BUILT' if rc==0 else 'BUILD_FAILED'",
"""cp=subprocess.run(['cmd.exe','/d','/c',str(script)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            rc=cp.returncode
            if rc != 0 and item.get('fallback_script'):
                fallback=root/item['fallback_script']
                if fallback.exists():
                    fp.write('\\n=== FALLBACK ===\\n')
                    fp.flush()
                    cp=subprocess.run(['cmd.exe','/d','/c',str(fallback)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
                    rc=cp.returncode
            status='BUILT' if rc==0 else 'BUILD_FAILED'""")
needle="""        if src.exists():
            shutil.copy2(src,out/dll)
            if status=='SKIPPED':status='EXISTS'
        elif status=='BUILT': status='OUTPUT_MISSING'
"""
repl="""        if src.exists():
            shutil.copy2(src,out/dll)
            if status=='SKIPPED':status='EXISTS'
            dep_script=item.get('copy_dependencies_script')
            if dep_script and not ns.no_build:
                ds=root/dep_script
                if ds.exists():
                    with log.open('a',encoding='utf-8',errors='replace') as fp:
                        fp.write('\\n=== COPY DEPENDENCIES ===\\n')
                        subprocess.run(['cmd.exe','/d','/c',str(ds)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        elif status=='BUILT': status='OUTPUT_MISSING'
"""
if needle not in t: raise SystemExit('builder stage block missing')
t=t.replace(needle,repl)
p.write_text(t,encoding='utf-8')

# uxffi build robust libffi DLL copy wildcard
p=root/'runtime_ext/uxffi/build_uxffi_dll.bat'; t=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
t=t.replace('copy /y "C:\\msys64\\ucrt64\\bin\\libffi-8.dll" "%CANON_OUT%\\libffi-8.dll" >nul 2>nul',
'''for %%F in (C:\\msys64\\ucrt64\\bin\\libffi-*.dll) do copy /y "%%F" "%CANON_OUT%\\%%~nxF" >nul 2>nul''')
p.write_text(t,encoding='utf-8')
print('patched refinements')
PY
patched refinements
Devam etme ve araçlarý deðerlendir
Öncelikle dosya sistemini incelemeliyim, ama belki önce kiþisel baðlamý gözden geçirmeliyim. Her kullanýcý mesajýnda nedenleri anlamam önemli olmalý, ancak tüm baðlama sahip olduðunu düþünüyorum, o yüzden araç kutusunu deðerlendirip, yapýlmasý gereken düzeltmeleri sýralayalým.

Doðrulama yapmalýyým
Þimdi doðrulamayý yapmam gerekiyor. Gerekli adýmlarýn tamamlanýp tamamlanmadýðýný kontrol etmeliyim. Bu, ilerlemek için önemli bir aþama olacak. Hadi bakalým, gereken her þeyi gözden geçirelim.

Geçerliliði kontrol etme
bash -lc set -e
python -m py_compile \
 /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/tools/library_tools/uxb_library_build.py \
 /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/tools/library_tools/uxb_library_abi_audit.py
python - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/libraries/library_manifest.json')
d=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
print('manifest schema:', d.get('schema'))
print('libraries:', len(d.get('libraries', [])))
for x in d.get('libraries', []):
    if not x.get('name') or not x.get('dll'):
        print('BAD', x)

sed -n '1,280p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/tools/library_tools/uxb_library_build.py
manifest schema: uxb.library_manifest.v1
libraries: 21
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, json, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path

def root_from_here() -> Path:
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description="uXBasic birleþik DLL üreticisi")
    ap.add_argument('--tier',choices=['core','all'],default='core')
    ap.add_argument('--library',action='append',default=[])
    ap.add_argument('--strict-optional',action='store_true')
    ap.add_argument('--no-build',action='store_true')
    ns=ap.parse_args()
    root=root_from_here(); manifest=json.loads((root/'libraries/library_manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    out=root/'dist/libraries/bin'; logs=root/'dist/libraries/logs'; legacy=root/'dist/runtime_ext'
    out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); logs.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); legacy.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    selected=[]
    names=set(ns.library)
    for item in manifest['libraries']:
        if names and item['name'] not in names: continue
        if not names and ns.tier=='core' and item['tier']!='CORE': continue
        selected.append(item)
    rows=[]; hard_fail=0
    for item in selected:
        start=time.time(); script=root/item['build_script']; dll=item['dll']; rc=None; status=''
        log=logs/f"{item['name']}.log"
        if ns.no_build:
            status='SKIPPED'
        elif not script.exists():
            status='SCRIPT_MISSING'; rc=127
        else:
            with log.open('w',encoding='utf-8',errors='replace') as fp:
                cp=subprocess.run(['cmd.exe','/d','/c',str(script)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            rc=cp.returncode
            if rc != 0 and item.get('fallback_script'):
                fallback=root/item['fallback_script']
                if fallback.exists():
                    fp.write('\n=== FALLBACK ===\n')
                    fp.flush()
                    cp=subprocess.run(['cmd.exe','/d','/c',str(fallback)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
                    rc=cp.returncode
            status='BUILT' if rc==0 else 'BUILD_FAILED'
        src=legacy/dll
        if src.exists():
            shutil.copy2(src,out/dll)
            if status=='SKIPPED':status='EXISTS'
            dep_script=item.get('copy_dependencies_script')
            if dep_script and not ns.no_build:
                ds=root/dep_script
                if ds.exists():
                    with log.open('a',encoding='utf-8',errors='replace') as fp:
                        fp.write('\n=== COPY DEPENDENCIES ===\n')
                        subprocess.run(['cmd.exe','/d','/c',str(ds)],cwd=root.parent,stdout=fp,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        elif status=='BUILT': status='OUTPUT_MISSING'
        required=item['tier']=='CORE' or ns.strict_optional
        if required and status not in ('BUILT','EXISTS'): hard_fail+=1
        rows.append({**item,'status':status,'exit_code':rc,'seconds':round(time.time()-start,3),'output':str(out/dll),'log':str(log)})
        print(f"{item['name']}: {status}")
    report={'schema':'uxb.library_build_report.v1','ok':hard_fail==0,'tier':ns.tier,'hard_failures':hard_fail,'results':rows}
    (root/'dist/libraries/library_build_report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
    return 0 if hard_fail==0 else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())
Hata düzeltmeleri ve kontroller yapýlmalý
Bir hata var gibi görünüyor: fp kullanýmý dýþarýda, bir with bloðu kapandýðýnda fp deðiþkeni kapanýyor ve bu, fallback durumunda fp.write'in ValueError hatasý vermesine neden oluyor. Ayrýca, kök klasör (cwd) yolunu doðrulamalýyým. manifesti gözden geçirmek gerek.

grep -RIn --include='*.fbs' --include='*.bas' 'Field *=\|Type .* Field' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src | head -80
#ifndef UXB_EXEC_FFI_BRIDGE10_FBS
#define UXB_EXEC_FFI_BRIDGE10_FBS

' Optional libffi based bridge.  The compiler/interpreter does not require
' uxffi.dll for the legacy 0..4 argument path.  When installed, this bridge
' accepts mixed scalar/pointer signatures with at most ten arguments.

Const UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_ABI_VERSION As UInteger = 1
Const UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_MAX_ARGS As Integer = 10

Type UXBFfiBridge10Request
    abiVersion As UInteger
    structSize As UInteger
    argCount As UInteger
    flags As UInteger
    dllName As ZString Ptr
    symbolName As ZString Ptr
    returnType As ZString Ptr
    argTypesCsv As ZString Ptr
    u64Args As ULongInt Ptr
    f64Args As Double Ptr
    f64Mask As ULongInt
    resultU64 As ULongInt
    resultF64 As Double
    resultKind As UInteger
    statusCode As Integer
    errorText As ZString * 512
End Type

Type UXBFfiBridge10InvokeFn As Function Cdecl(ByVal requestPtr As UXBFfiBridge10Request Ptr) As Integer

Dim Shared gUXBFfiBridge10Tried As Integer
Dim Shared gUXBFfiBridge10Module As Any Ptr
Dim Shared gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke As UXBFfiBridge10InvokeFn

Private Sub ExecFfiBridge10LoadIfNeeded()
    If gUXBFfiBridge10Tried <> 0 Then Exit Sub
    gUXBFfiBridge10Tried = 1
    gUXBFfiBridge10Module = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr("uxffi.dll"))
    If gUXBFfiBridge10Module = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(gUXBFfiBridge10Module, StrPtr("uxffi_invoke_v1"))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        gUXBFfiBridge10Module = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke = procAddr
End Sub

Private Function ExecFfiBridge10Invoke( _
    ByRef dllName As String, _
    ByRef symbolName As String, _
    ByRef returnType As String, _
    argTypes() As String, _
    ByVal argCount As Integer, _
    u64Args() As ULongInt, _
    f64Args() As Double, _
    ByVal f64Mask As Integer, _
    ByRef valueOut As Integer, _
    ByRef invokedOut As Integer, _
    ByRef errText As String _
) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_MAX_ARGS Then
        errText = "exec: uxffi bridge supports at most 10 arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    ExecFfiBridge10LoadIfNeeded
    If gUXBFfiBridge10Module = 0 Or gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim csv As String
    csv = ""
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        If i > 0 Then csv &= ","
        csv &= UCase(Trim(argTypes(i)))
    Next i

    Dim req As UXBFfiBridge10Request
    req.abiVersion = UXB_FFI_BRIDGE10_ABI_VERSION
    req.structSize = SizeOf(UXBFfiBridge10Request)
    req.argCount = argCount
    req.flags = 0
    req.dllName = StrPtr(dllName)
    req.symbolName = StrPtr(symbolName)
    req.returnType = StrPtr(returnType)
    req.argTypesCsv = StrPtr(csv)
    If argCount > 0 Then
        req.u64Args = @u64Args(0)
        req.f64Args = @f64Args(0)
    Else
        req.u64Args = 0
        req.f64Args = 0
    End If
    req.f64Mask = CULngInt(f64Mask)

    Dim rc As Integer
    rc = gUXBFfiBridge10Invoke(@req)
    If rc = 0 Or req.statusCode <> 0 Then
        errText = "exec: uxffi bridge failed"
        If req.errorText <> "" Then errText &= ": " & req.errorText
        Return 0
    End If

    Select Case UCase(Trim(returnType))
    Case "F64"
        valueOut = CInt(req.resultF64)
    Case Else
        valueOut = CInt(req.resultU64)
    End Select
    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

#endif

--- C ---
#include "uxffi.h"
#include <ffi.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <ctype.h>

static void set_error(uxffi_request_v1 *r, int code, const char *msg) {
    if (!r) return;
    r->status_code = code;
    snprintf(r->error_text, sizeof(r->error_text), "%s", msg ? msg : "unknown error");
}

static int eqi(const char *a, const char *b) {
    if (!a || !b) return 0;
    while (*a && *b) {
        if (toupper((unsigned char)*a) != toupper((unsigned char)*b)) return 0;
        ++a; ++b;
    }
    return *a == 0 && *b == 0;
}

static ffi_type *ffi_type_for(const char *name) {
    if (eqi(name,"I32")) return &ffi_type_sint32;
    if (eqi(name,"U32")) return &ffi_type_uint32;
    if (eqi(name,"I64")) return &ffi_type_sint64;
    if (eqi(name,"U64")) return &ffi_type_uint64;
    if (eqi(name,"F64")) return &ffi_type_double;
    if (eqi(name,"PTR") || eqi(name,"STRPTR") || eqi(name,"BYREF")) return &ffi_type_pointer;
    if (eqi(name,"VOID")) return &ffi_type_void;
    return NULL;
}

static int parse_types(const char *csv, char tokens[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS][16], uint32_t expected) {
    uint32_t count = 0;
    const char *p = csv ? csv : "";
    while (*p && count < UXFFI_MAX_ARGS) {
        while (*p == ' ' || *p == '\t' || *p == ',') ++p;
        if (!*p) break;
        size_t n = 0;
        while (*p && *p != ',') {
            if (!isspace((unsigned char)*p) && n + 1 < 16) tokens[count][n++] = *p;
            ++p;
        }
        tokens[count][n] = 0;
        ++count;
    }
    return count == expected ? 1 : 0;
}

UXFFI_API int uxffi_version(void) { return 1; }

UXFFI_API int uxffi_invoke_v1(uxffi_request_v1 *r) {
    if (!r) return 0;
    r->status_code = 0; r->error_text[0] = 0; r->result_u64 = 0; r->result_f64 = 0.0; r->result_kind = 0;
    if (r->abi_version != UXFFI_ABI_VERSION || r->struct_size < sizeof(*r)) { set_error(r,1,"ABI/struct mismatch"); return 0; }
    if (r->arg_count > UXFFI_MAX_ARGS) { set_error(r,2,"too many arguments"); return 0; }
    if (!r->dll_name || !r->symbol_name || !r->return_type) { set_error(r,3,"missing target metadata"); return 0; }

    HMODULE mod = LoadLibraryA(r->dll_name);
    if (!mod) { set_error(r,4,"LoadLibraryA failed"); return 0; }
    FARPROC proc = GetProcAddress(mod, r->symbol_name);
    if (!proc) { set_error(r,5,"GetProcAddress failed"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }

    char tokens[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS][16] = {{0}};
    if (r->arg_count && !parse_types(r->arg_types_csv,tokens,r->arg_count)) { set_error(r,6,"argument type count mismatch"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }

    ffi_type *atypes[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    void *avalues[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    int32_t i32v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS]; uint32_t u32v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    int64_t i64v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS]; uint64_t u64v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];
    double f64v[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS]; void *ptrv[UXFFI_MAX_ARGS];

    for (uint32_t i=0;i<r->arg_count;i++) {
        atypes[i] = ffi_type_for(tokens[i]);
        if (!atypes[i] || atypes[i] == &ffi_type_void) { set_error(r,7,"invalid argument type"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }
        if (eqi(tokens[i],"I32")) { i32v[i]=(int32_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&i32v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"U32")) { u32v[i]=(uint32_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&u32v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"I64")) { i64v[i]=(int64_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&i64v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"U64")) { u64v[i]=r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&u64v[i]; }
        else if (eqi(tokens[i],"F64")) { f64v[i]=r->f64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&f64v[i]; }
        else { ptrv[i]=(void*)(uintptr_t)r->u64_args[i]; avalues[i]=&ptrv[i]; }
    }

    ffi_type *rtype=ffi_type_for(r->return_type);
    if (!rtype) { set_error(r,8,"invalid return type"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }
    ffi_cif cif;
    if (ffi_prep_cif(&cif,FFI_DEFAULT_ABI,r->arg_count,rtype,atypes)!=FFI_OK) { set_error(r,9,"ffi_prep_cif failed"); FreeLibrary(mod); return 0; }

    union { int32_t i32; uint32_t u32; int64_t i64; uint64_t u64; double f64; void *ptr; } result;
    memset(&result,0,sizeof(result));
    ffi_call(&cif,FFI_FN(proc),rtype==&ffi_type_void?NULL:&result,avalues);
    if (rtype==&ffi_type_double) { r->result_f64=result.f64; r->result_kind=2; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_pointer) { r->result_u64=(uint64_t)(uintptr_t)result.ptr; r->result_kind=3; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_sint32) { r->result_u64=(uint64_t)(int64_t)result.i32; r->result_kind=1; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_uint32) { r->result_u64=result.u32; r->result_kind=1; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_sint64) { r->result_u64=(uint64_t)result.i64; r->result_kind=1; }
    else if (rtype==&ffi_type_uint64) { r->result_u64=result.u64; r->result_kind=1; }
    FreeLibrary(mod);
    return 1;
}

--- H ---

    uint32_t arg_count;
    uint32_t flags;
    const char *dll_name;
    const char *symbol_name;
    const char *return_type;
    const char *arg_types_csv;
    const uint64_t *u64_args;
    const double *f64_args;
    uint64_t f64_mask;
    uint64_t result_u64;
    double result_f64;
    uint32_t result_kind;
    int32_t status_code;

} uxffi_request_v1;

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
#endif

FFI yapý hizalamasýný deðerlendirme
FreeBasic'in Win64'teki varsayýlan hizalamasýnýn 8 olup olmadýðýný deðerlendirmem gerek. Yapý hizalamasý uyuþmazlýklarý geçiþin baþarýsýz olabilmesine yol açabilir. Type ... Field = 1 gibi hizalanmýþ yapýlar kullanmayý da düþünebilirim, ancak FreeBasic belgelerinin yetersizliði bir sorun olabilir.

FreeBASIC yapý hizalama ve iþlev tasarýmý
FreeBASIC'teki Type Field = 1 yapýsýnýn hizalama gereksinimlerini inceledim. C'yi #pragma pack(push,1) ve FreeBASIC'i hizalý yapmak, tüm iþlevlerin saðlýklý iþleyiþi için yeterli. Alt yapýyý oluþturarak fonksiyon iþaretçisi ve diðer her þeyin uyumlu olmasýný saðladým.

#include once "exec_ffi_x64_invoke_helpers.fbs"

Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64I32(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0

    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() <> "WIN64-MSABI" Then Return 1

    Dim sig As String
    sig = UCase(Trim(signatureText))
    If sig <> "I32" And sig <> "U64" And sig <> "F64" And sig <> "PTR" And sig <> "STRPTR" And sig <> "BYREF" And sig <> "BYVAL" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 4 Then Return 1

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argValuesI32(0 To 3) As Integer
    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 3) As ULongInt
    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 3) As Double
    Dim argValuesStr(0 To 3) As String
    Dim argValuesStrPtr(0 To 3) As ZString Ptr
    If ExecX64EvalArgs(ps, es, callNodeIdx, argStartPos, sig, argCount, argValuesI32(), argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argValuesStr(), argValuesStrPtr(), errText) = 0 Then
        Return 0
    End If

    ExecX64DispatchInvoke procAddr, sig, argCount, argValuesI32(), argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argValuesStrPtr(), valueOut

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(ByRef tokenText As String, ByRef typeOut As String) As Integer
    typeOut = UCase(Trim(tokenText))
    If typeOut = "BYVAL" Then typeOut = "VOID"
    If typeOut = "CDECL" Or typeOut = "STDCALL" Then Return 0
    Select Case typeOut
    Case "VOID", "I32", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF"
        Return 1
    End Select
    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecFfiParseArgTypeList(ByRef listText As String, argTypes() As String, ByRef argTypeCount As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    argTypeCount = 0
    Dim src As String
    src = Trim(listText)
    If src = "" Or Instr(src, ",") = 0 Then Return 0

    Dim scanPos As Integer
    scanPos = 1
    Do While scanPos <= Len(src)
        Dim commaPos As Integer
        commaPos = Instr(scanPos, src, ",")
        Dim part As String
        If commaPos = 0 Then
            part = Mid(src, scanPos)
            scanPos = Len(src) + 1
        Else
            part = Mid(src, scanPos, commaPos - scanPos)
            scanPos = commaPos + 1
        End If

        Dim typeName As String
        If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(part, typeName) = 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid argument type list token " & Trim(part)
            Return -1
        End If

        If argTypeCount > UBound(argTypes) Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) argument type list too large"
            Return -1
        End If

        argTypes(argTypeCount) = typeName
        argTypeCount += 1
    Loop

    Return IIf(argTypeCount > 0, 1, 0)
End Function

Private Function ExecTryReadDllArgTypeList(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByVal totalChildCount As Integer, argTypes() As String, ByRef argTypeCount As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    argTypeCount = 0
    If argStartPos < 0 Or argStartPos >= totalChildCount Then Return 0

    Dim listNode As Integer
    listNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos)
    If listNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(listNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then Return 0

    Dim status As Integer
    status = ExecFfiParseArgTypeList(ps.ast.nodes(listNode).value, argTypes(), argTypeCount, errText)
    If status <= 0 Then Return status

    Dim actualArgs As Integer
    actualArgs = totalChildCount - argStartPos - 1
    If actualArgs <> argTypeCount Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) argument type list count mismatch expected=" & LTrim(Str(argTypeCount)) & " actual=" & LTrim(Str(actualArgs))
        Return -1
    End If

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecEvalDllArgAsMixedSlot(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal argNode As Integer, ByRef argType As String, ByRef stringKeepAlive As String, ByRef u64Out As ULongInt, ByRef f64Out As Double, ByRef isF64Out As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim t As String
    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(argType, t) = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid mixed argument type " & Trim(argType)
        Return 0
    End If

    stringKeepAlive = ""
    u64Out = 0
    f64Out = 0#
    isF64Out = 0

    Select Case t
    Case "F64"
        Dim fv As ExecValue
        If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, fv, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If fv.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) F64 requires numeric argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If fv.valueType = "DOUBLE" Then
            f64Out = fv.floatValue
        Else
            f64Out = CDbl(fv.intValue)
        End If
        isF64Out = 1
        Return 1

    Case "STRPTR"
        If ExecEvalNodeAsText(ps, es, argNode, stringKeepAlive, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
#Ifdef __FB_64BIT__
        u64Out = CULngInt(CLngInt(CPtr(Any Ptr, StrPtr(stringKeepAlive))))
#Else
        u64Out = CULngInt(CULng(CPtr(Any Ptr, StrPtr(stringKeepAlive))))
#EndIf
        Return 1

    Case Else
        Dim iv As ExecValue
        If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, iv, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If iv.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) " & t & " requires numeric/pointer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If t = "U64" And iv.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

        If t = "I32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CLng(iv.intValue))
        Else
            u64Out = CULngInt(iv.intValue)
        End If
        Return 1
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef retTypeText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, argTypes() As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0
    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiN[... ELLIPSIZATION ...]allingConvention(convOut) = 0 Then Return 0

    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub ExecLoadFfiAllowlistIfNeeded()
    If gFfiAllowlistLoaded <> 0 Then Exit Sub
    gFfiAllowlistLoaded = 1
    gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 0
    gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = ""
    gFfiAllowEntryCount = 0
    Erase gFfiAllowEntries

    Dim policyPath As String
    policyPath = ExecGetFfiPolicyPath()

    If Dir(policyPath) = "" Then
        If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "file-not-found"
            UxbWarn "FFI policy file not found, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
        Else
            ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
            UxbWarn "FFI policy file not found, defaults active path=" & policyPath
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim f As Integer
    f = FreeFile
    Open policyPath For Input As #f
    If Err <> 0 Then
        If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "open-failed"
            UxbWarn "FFI policy open failed, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
        Else
            ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
            UxbWarn "FFI policy open failed, defaults active path=" & policyPath
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim headerSeen As Integer
    Dim headerValid As Integer
    headerSeen = 0
    headerValid = 0

    Do While Not Eof(f)
        Dim lineText As String
        Line Input #f, lineText

        Dim lineTrim As String
        lineTrim = Trim(lineText)

        If lineTrim <> "" Then
            If headerSeen = 0 Then
                headerSeen = 1

                Dim headerText As String
                headerText = UCase(lineTrim)
                If Left(headerText, 1) = "#" Then
                    headerText = UCase(Trim(Mid(lineTrim, 2)))
                End If

                If headerText <> UXB_FFI_POLICY_HEADER_V1 Then
                    Close #f
                    If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
                        gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
                        gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "header-invalid"
                        UxbWarn "FFI policy header invalid, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
                    Else
                        ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
                        UxbWarn "FFI policy header invalid, defaults active path=" & policyPath
                    End If
                    Exit Sub
                End If

                headerValid = 1
            Else
                Dim dllName As String
                Dim symbolName As String
                Dim signatureText As String
                Dim callingConventionText As String
                Dim hashText As String
                Dim signerText As String
                If ExecParseFfiAllowlistLine(lineText, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, hashText, signerText) <> 0 Then
                    ExecFfiAllowlistAppend dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, hashText, signerText
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Loop

    Close #f

    If headerValid = 0 Then
        If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "header-missing"
            UxbWarn "FFI policy header missing, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
        Else
            ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
            UxbWarn "FFI policy header missing, defaults active path=" & policyPath
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If

    If gFfiAllowEntryCount = 0 Then
        If ExecGetFfiPolicyMode() = "ENFORCE" Then
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed = 1
            gFfiAllowlistLoadFailReason = "no-valid-entries"
            UxbWarn "FFI policy had no valid entries, enforce deny path=" & policyPath
        Else
            ExecSeedDefaultFfiAllowlist
            UxbWarn "FFI policy had no valid entries, defaults active path=" & policyPath
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If

    UxbInfo "FFI policy loaded path=" & policyPath & " entries=" & LTrim(Str(gFfiAllowEntryCount))
End Sub

Private Function ExecPolicyDenyCode(ByRef reasonText As String) As Integer
    Select Case LCase(Trim(reasonText))
    Case "deny-policy-load-failed"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_LOAD_FAILED
    Case "deny-attestation-required"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_ATTEST_REQUIRED
    Case "deny-hash-mismatch"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_MISMATCH
    Case "deny-signer-mismatch"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_MISMATCH
    Case "deny-hash-extract-failed"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_EXTRACT_FAILED
    Case "deny-signer-extract-failed"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_EXTRACT_FAILED
    Case "deny-not-allowlisted"
        Return UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_DENY
    Case Else
        Return 0
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecPolicyDenyPhase(ByRef reasonText As String) As String
    Select Case LCase(Trim(reasonText))
    Case "deny-policy-load-failed"
        Return "policy-load"
    Case "deny-hash-mismatch", "deny-hash-extract-failed"
        Return "hash-verify"
    Case "deny-signer-mismatch", "deny-signer-extract-failed"
        Return "signer-verify"
    Case "deny-attestation-required"
        Return "attestation-required"
    Case "deny-not-allowlisted"
        Return "allowlist-lookup"
    Case Else
        Return "policy"
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecValidateDllMarshalArg(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal argNode As Integer, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim argValue As ExecValue
    If ExecEvalExpression(ps, argNode, es, argValue, errText) = 0 Then Return 0

    Dim sig As String
    sig = UCase(Trim(signatureText))

    Select Case sig
    Case "I32"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) I32 requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "U64"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 requires integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If
        If argValue.intValue < 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "F64"
        If argValue.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) F64 requires numeric argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "PTR"
        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) PTR requires pointer/integer argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "STRPTR"
        If argValue.valueType <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) STRPTR requires string argument"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "BYVAL"
        ' BYVAL is value-passing marker; no extra runtime type guard here.

    Case "BYREF"
        If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argNode).kind) <> "IDENT" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) BYREF requires addressable target argument"
            Return 0
        End If
    End Select

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecTryParseAliasFfiTarget(ByRef targetText As String, ByRef ffiKindOut As String, ByRef dllNameOut As String, ByRef symbolNameOut As String, ByRef signatureOut As String, ByRef conventionOut As String) As Integer
    ffiKindOut = ""
    dllNameOut = ""
    symbolNameOut = ""
    signatureOut = ""
    conventionOut = "CDECL"

    Dim lx As LexerState
    LexerInit lx, targetText

    Dim i As Integer
    i = 0

    While i < lx.tokens.count And (lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "EOL")
        i += 1
    Wend

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    Dim headKind As String
    headKind = UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind)
    If (headKind <> "KEYWORD" And headKind <> "IDENT") Or UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme) <> "CALL" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "(" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    Dim ffiHeadKind As String
    ffiHeadKind = UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind)
    If (ffiHeadKind <> "KEYWORD" And ffiHeadKind <> "IDENT") Then Return 0
    ffiKindOut = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
    If ffiKindOut <> "DLL" And ffiKindOut <> "API" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    dllNameOut = ""
    Do While i < lx.tokens.count
        If lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then Exit Do
        dllNameOut &= lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme
        i += 1
    Loop
    dllNameOut = Trim(dllNameOut)
    If dllNameOut = "" Then Return 0

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    symbolNameOut = ""
    Do While i < lx.tokens.count
        If lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then Exit Do
        symbolNameOut &= lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme
        i += 1
    Loop
    symbolNameOut = Trim(symbolNameOut)
    If symbolNameOut = "" Then Return 0

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    If UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind) <> "KEYWORD" And UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind) <> "IDENT" Then Return 0
    signatureOut = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
    i += 1

    If ffiKindOut = "DLL" And i < lx.tokens.count And lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then
        i += 1
        If i < lx.tokens.count Then
            Dim conv As String
            conv = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
            If ExecIsDllCallingConvention(conv) <> 0 Then
                conventionOut = conv

Private Function ExecValidateDllMarshalArg(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal argNode As Integer, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim argValue As ExecValue

    Case "I32"

            Return 0
        End If

            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) U64 argument cannot be negative"
            Return 0
        End If

    Case "F64"
        If argValue.valueType = "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) F64 requires numeric argument"
            Return 0
        End If

        If argValue.valueType <> "INTEGER" Then

            Return 0
        End If

            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) STRPTR requires string argument"
            Return 0
        End If

        ' BYVAL is value-passing marker; no extra runtime type guard here.
