Private Function ExecTryParseAliasFfiTarget(ByRef targetText As String, ByRef ffiKindOut As String, ByRef dllNameOut As String, ByRef symbolNameOut As String, ByRef signatureOut As String, ByRef conventionOut As String) As Integer
    ffiKindOut = ""
    dllNameOut = ""
    symbolNameOut = ""
    signatureOut = ""
    conventionOut = "CDECL"

    Dim lx As LexerState
    LexerInit lx, targetText

    Dim i As Integer
    i = 0

    While i < lx.tokens.count And (lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "EOL")
        i += 1
    Wend

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    Dim headKind As String
    headKind = UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind)
    If (headKind <> "KEYWORD" And headKind <> "IDENT") Or UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme) <> "CALL" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "(" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    Dim ffiHeadKind As String
    ffiHeadKind = UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind)
    If (ffiHeadKind <> "KEYWORD" And ffiHeadKind <> "IDENT") Then Return 0
    ffiKindOut = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
    If ffiKindOut <> "DLL" And ffiKindOut <> "API" Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    dllNameOut = ""
    Do While i < lx.tokens.count
        If lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then Exit Do
        dllNameOut &= lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme
        i += 1
    Loop
    dllNameOut = Trim(dllNameOut)
    If dllNameOut = "" Then Return 0

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    symbolNameOut = ""
    Do While i < lx.tokens.count
        If lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then Exit Do
        symbolNameOut &= lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme
        i += 1
    Loop
    symbolNameOut = Trim(symbolNameOut)
    If symbolNameOut = "" Then Return 0

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Or lx.tokens.items(i).kind <> "OP" Or lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme <> "," Then Return 0
    i += 1

    If i >= lx.tokens.count Then Return 0
    If UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind) <> "KEYWORD" And UCase(lx.tokens.items(i).kind) <> "IDENT" Then Return 0
    signatureOut = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
    i += 1

    If ffiKindOut = "DLL" And i < lx.tokens.count And lx.tokens.items(i).kind = "OP" And lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme = "," Then
        i += 1
        If i < lx.tokens.count Then
            Dim conv As String
            conv = UCase(Trim(lx.tokens.items(i).lexeme))
            If ExecIsDllCallingConvention(conv) <> 0 Then
                conventionOut = conv
            End If
        End If
    End If

    If ffiKindOut = "API" Then
        conventionOut = "CDECL"
        If InStr(dllNameOut, ".") = 0 Then dllNameOut &= ".dll"
    End If

    Return ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(signatureOut)
End Function

Private Function ExecTryParseAliasDllTarget(ByRef targetText As String, ByRef dllNameOut As String, ByRef symbolNameOut As String, ByRef signatureOut As String, ByRef conventionOut As String) As Integer
    Dim ffiKind As String
    If ExecTryParseAliasFfiTarget(targetText, ffiKind, dllNameOut, symbolNameOut, signatureOut, conventionOut) = 0 Then Return 0
    Return IIf(ffiKind = "DLL", 1, 0)
End Function

Private Function ExecDllCallIsAllowlisted(ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal requireAttestation As Integer, ByRef denyReasonOut As String) As Integer
    ExecLoadFfiAllowlistIfNeeded

    If gFfiAllowlistLoadFailed <> 0 Then
        denyReasonOut = "deny-policy-load-failed"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim dllKey As String
    Dim symbolKey As String
    Dim sigKey As String
    Dim convKey As String

    dllKey = UCase(Trim(dllName))
    symbolKey = UCase(Trim(symbolName))
    sigKey = UCase(Trim(signatureText))
    convKey = UCase(Trim(callingConventionText))

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To gFfiAllowEntryCount - 1
        If dllKey = gFfiAllowEntries(i).dllName And symbolKey = gFfiAllowEntries(i).symbolName And sigKey = gFfiAllowEntries(i).signatureText Then
            Dim allowConv As String
            allowConv = UCase(Trim(gFfiAllowEntries(i).callingConventionText))
            If ExecAreDllCallingConventionsCompatible(convKey, allowConv) = 0 Then
                GoTo NextAllowEntry
            End If

            If requireAttestation <> 0 Then
                If gFfiAllowEntries(i).hashText = "" Or gFfiAllowEntries(i).signerText = "" Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-attestation-required"
                    Return 0
                End If

                If gFfiAllowEntries(i).hashText = "*" Or gFfiAllowEntries(i).signerText = "*" Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-attestation-required"
                    Return 0
                End If

                Dim hashState As Integer
                hashState = FfiHashMatchState(gFfiAllowEntries(i).hashText, dllName)
                If hashState < 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-hash-extract-failed"
                    Return 0
                End If

                If hashState = 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-hash-mismatch"
                    Return 0
                End If

                Dim signerState As Integer
                signerState = FfiSignerMatchState(gFfiAllowEntries(i).signerText, dllName)
                If signerState < 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-signer-extract-failed"
                    Return 0
                End If

                If signerState = 0 Then
                    denyReasonOut = "deny-signer-mismatch"
                    Return 0
                End If
            End If

            denyReasonOut = ""
            Return 1
        End If
NextAllowEntry:
    Next i

    denyReasonOut = "deny-not-allowlisted"
    Return 0
End Function

Private Sub ExecAuditDllPolicy(ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByRef modeText As String, ByVal allowDecision As Integer, ByRef reasonText As String)
    Dim msgText As String
    Dim denyCode As Integer
    denyCode = ExecPolicyDenyCode(reasonText)

    Dim phaseText As String
    phaseText = ExecPolicyDenyPhase(reasonText)

    msgText = "FFI CALL(DLL) event=ffi_policy_decision mode=" & modeText & " decision="
    If allowDecision <> 0 Then
        msgText &= "allow"
    Else
        msgText &= "deny"
    End If

    msgText &= " lib=" & dllName & " symbol=" & symbolName & " sig=" & signatureText & " conv=" & callingConventionText
    msgText &= " abi=" & ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName()
    msgText &= " allow=" & LTrim(Str(allowDecision)) & " deny_code=" & LTrim(Str(denyCode))
    msgText &= " phase=" & phaseText
    If reasonText <> "" Then msgText &= " reason=" & reasonText

    If allowDecision <> 0 Then
        UxbInfo msgText
    Else
        UxbWarn msgText
    End If
End Sub
Çaðrýcýyý inceleme gerekliliði
Sanýrým iþin doðru þekilde yürüyebilmesi için, çaðrýcýyý dikkatlice incelemem gerekiyor. Belki de sorunun kaynaðý burada gizli olabilir. Çaðrýlarýn nasýl yapýldýðýný anlamadan doðru bir çözüm öneremem gibi görünüyor.

Inspecting çaðýrýcý kodu
bash -lc sed -n '1,180p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src/runtime/exec/exec_eval_builtin_categories.fbs
Private Function ExecEvalDllCallCore(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef signatureText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim ffiArgCount As Integer
    ffiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)

    Dim effectiveArgStartPos As Integer
    effectiveArgStartPos = argStartPos

    Dim mixedArgTypes(0 To 15) As String
    Dim mixedArgTypeCount As Integer
    Dim mixedTypeStatus As Integer
    mixedTypeStatus = ExecTryReadDllArgTypeList(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos, ffiArgCount, mixedArgTypes(), mixedArgTypeCount, errText)
    If mixedTypeStatus < 0 Then Return 0
    If mixedTypeStatus > 0 Then effectiveArgStartPos = argStartPos + 1

    If signatureText = "BYREF" Then
        Dim byrefNode As Integer
        byrefNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos)
        If byrefNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(byrefNode).kind) <> "IDENT" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) BYREF requires addressable target argument (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BYREF_TARGET)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
    End If

    Dim ai As Integer
    For ai = effectiveArgStartPos To ffiArgCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ai)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim argSig As String
        If mixedTypeStatus > 0 Then
            argSig = mixedArgTypes(ai - effectiveArgStartPos)
        Else
            argSig = signatureText
        End If

        If ExecValidateDllMarshalArg(ps, es, argNode, argSig, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
    Next ai

    Dim denyReason As String
    Dim allowDecision As Integer
    Dim requireAttestation As Integer
    Dim ffiMode As String
    ffiMode = ExecGetFfiPolicyMode()
    requireAttestation = IIf(ffiMode = "ENFORCE", 1, 0)
    allowDecision = ExecDllCallIsAllowlisted(dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, requireAttestation, denyReason)
    ExecAuditDllPolicy dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiMode, allowDecision, denyReason

    If allowDecision = 0 And ffiMode = "ENFORCE" Then
        If denyReason = "deny-policy-load-failed" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy load failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-attestation-required" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy attestation required (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_ATTEST_REQUIRED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-hash-extract-failed" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy hash extraction failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_EXTRACT_FAILED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-hash-mismatch" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy hash mismatch (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_HASH_MISMATCH)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-signer-extract-failed" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy signer extraction failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_EXTRACT_FAILED)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        If denyReason = "deny-signer-mismatch" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy signer mismatch (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_SIGNER_MISMATCH)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) denied by policy (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_POLICY_DENY)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim resolverMode As String
    resolverMode = ExecGetFfiResolverMode()

    Dim ffiArgRuntimeCount As Integer
    ffiArgRuntimeCount = ffiArgCount - effectiveArgStartPos
    If ffiArgRuntimeCount < 0 Then ffiArgRuntimeCount = 0

    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() = "WIN64-MSABI" Then
        Dim x64InvokedValue As Integer
        Dim x64InvokedOut As Integer
        Dim x64InvokeErr As String
        If mixedTypeStatus > 0 Then
            If ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, mixedArgTypes(), ffiArgRuntimeCount, x64InvokedValue, x64InvokedOut, x64InvokeErr) = 0 Then
                If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                    errText = x64InvokeErr
                    Return 0
                End If
                If x64InvokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x64 invoke report-only: " & x64InvokeErr
                Return 0
            End If
        ElseIf ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64I32(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, x64InvokedValue, x64InvokedOut, x64InvokeErr) = 0 Then
            If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                errText = x64InvokeErr
                Return 0
            End If
            If x64InvokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x64 invoke report-only: " & x64InvokeErr
            Return 0
        End If

        If x64InvokedOut <> 0 Then
            Return x64InvokedValue
        End If

        Return 0
    End If

    Dim cleanupType As String
    Dim resolverErr As String
        If ExecResolveX86InteropTarget(dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, cleanupType, resolverErr) = 0 Then
            If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                errText = resolverErr
                Return 0
            End If
            If resolverErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x86 resolver fallback: " & resolverErr

            Dim directInvokedValue As Integer
            Dim directInvokedOut As Integer
            Dim directInvokeErr As String
            If ExecTryInvokeDirectX86I32(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, directInvokedValue, directInvokedOut, directInvokeErr) = 0 Then
                If directInvokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x86 direct invoke report-only: " & directInvokeErr
            ElseIf directInvokedOut <> 0 Then
                Return directInvokedValue
            End If
        Else
            UxbInfo "FFI x86 resolver matched cleanup=" & cleanupType & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))

        Dim invokedValue As Integer
        Dim invokedOut As Integer
        Dim invokeErr As String
            If ExecTryInvokeResolvedX86I32(ps, es, callNodeIdx, effectiveArgStartPos, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, ffiArgRuntimeCount, invokedValue, invokedOut, invokeErr) = 0 Then
                If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
                    errText = invokeErr
                    Return 0
                End If
                If invokeErr <> "" Then UxbWarn "FFI x86 invoke proof report-only: " & invokeErr
            ElseIf invokedOut <> 0 Then
                Return invokedValue
            End If
        End If

        If resolverMode = "ENFORCE" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL/API) invoke path unavailable signature=" & UCase(Trim(signatureText)) & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))
            Return 0
        End If
        UxbWarn "FFI invoke report-only fallback signature=" & UCase(Trim(signatureText)) & " args=" & LTrim(Str(ffiArgRuntimeCount))
        Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecEvalBuiltinFfiCategory(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callName As String, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim callKey As String
    callKey = UCase(Trim(callName))

    Select Case callKey
    Case "DLL", "CALL"
        Dim ffiArgCount As Integer
        ffiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)
        Dim ffiBasePos As Integer
        ffiBasePos = 0
        Dim callMarker As String
        callMarker = "DLL"
        If callKey = "CALL" Then
            Dim markerNode As Integer
            markerNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 0)
            If markerNode = -1 Then
                errText = "exec: unsupported call CALL"
                Return 0
            End If
            callMarker = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(markerNode).value))
            If callMarker <> "DLL" And callMarker <> "API" Then
                errText = "exec: unsupported call CALL"
Fonksiyon yapýlandýrmasýný gözden geçirme
Kullanýcý "8 veya 10 parametreli mecburi olmayan sistem" demiþ. Bu, parametre sayýsýnýn isteðe baðlý olduðunu gösteriyor. O zaman fonksiyon çaðrýsýný veya yapýyý buna göre deðiþtirmeliyim. Ayrýca builder ve manifesti düzelmem gerekebilir. AST çocuklarýný anlamaya çalýþmalý ve DLL iþaretçileri ile ilgili incelemeler yapmalýyým.

Evaluating parameter handling and function semantics
bash -lc sed -n '220,390p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/parser/parser/parser_stmt_dispatch.fbs
sed -n '180,340p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src/runtime/exec/exec_eval_builtin_categories.fbs
        If ValidateIfSelectSemanticsNode(ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left) = 0 Then Return 0
    End If

    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right <> -1 Then
        If ValidateIfSelectSemanticsNode(ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right) = 0 Then Return 0
    End If

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        If ValidateIfSelectSemanticsNode(ps, ch) = 0 Then Return 0
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ValidateIfSelectSemantics(ByRef ps As ParseState) As Integer
    If ps.rootNode = -1 Then Return 1
    Return ValidateIfSelectSemanticsNode(ps, ps.rootNode)
End Function

#include once "parser_stmt_semantic_extra.fbs"

Private Function IsValidDllSignatureToken(ByRef signatureText As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = UCase(Trim(signatureText))

    Select Case s
    Case "VOID", "I32", "U32", "I64", "U64", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF", "BYVAL"
        Return 1
    End Select

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function IsValidDllCallingConventionToken(ByRef conventionText As String) As Integer
    Dim s As String
    s = UCase(Trim(conventionText))

    Select Case s
    Case "CDECL", "STDCALL"
        Return 1
    End Select

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function IsAddressableByRefArgNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer) As Integer
    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then Return 0
    Return UCase(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind) = "IDENT"
End Function

Private Function ValidateCallDllSemanticsForNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNode As Integer) As Integer
    If UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(callNode).value)) <> "DLL" Then Return 1

    ' CALL(DLL) has three/four metadata fields plus up to ten runtime arguments.
    ' Keep validation independent from a fixed six-element scratch array.
    Const UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS As Integer = 10
    Const UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN As Integer = UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS + 5

    Dim argCount As Integer
    Dim argIdx(0 To UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN - 1) As Integer
    Dim initIdx As Integer
    For initIdx = 0 To UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN - 1
        argIdx(initIdx) = -1
    Next initIdx

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(callNode).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        If argCount < UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then argIdx(argCount) = ch
        argCount += 1
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop

    If argCount > UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): call metadata plus arguments exceed the ABI10 limit"
        Return 0
    End If

    If argCount < 4 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): expects at least 4 arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(0)).kind) <> "STRING" Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): library must be string literal"
        Return 0
    End If

    If Len(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(0)).value)) = 0 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): library cannot be empty"
        Return 0
    End If

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(1)).kind) <> "STRING" Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): symbol must be string literal"
        Return 0
    End If

    If Len(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(1)).value)) = 0 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): symbol cannot be empty"
        Return 0
    End If

    If UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(2)).kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): signature token must be a keyword"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim signatureText As String
    signatureText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(2)).value))
    If IsValidDllSignatureToken(signatureText) = 0 Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): invalid signature token: " & signatureText
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argStartPos As Integer
    argStartPos = 3

    If argCount >= 5 Then
        If argIdx(3) <> -1 And UCase(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(3)).kind) = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
            Dim conventionText As String
            conventionText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(argIdx(3)).value))
            If IsValidDllCallingConventionToken(conventionText) = 0 Then
                ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): invalid calling convention token: " & conventionText
                Return 0
            End If
            argStartPos = 4
        End If
    End If

    Dim runtimeArgStartPos As Integer
    runtimeArgStartPos = argStartPos
    ' An optional string immediately after CDECL/STDCALL is the per-argument
    ' type list.  A single type token is valid as well as comma-separated lists.
    If runtimeArgStartPos < argCount Then
        Dim typeListNode As Integer
        typeListNode = argIdx(runtimeArgStartPos)
        If typeListNode <> -1 And UCase(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).kind) = "STRING" Then
            Dim typeListText As String
            typeListText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).value))
            If typeListText = "I32" Or typeListText = "U32" Or typeListText = "I64" Or typeListText = "U64" Or _
               typeListText = "F64" Or typeListText = "PTR" Or typeListText = "STRPTR" Or typeListText = "BYREF" Or _
               Instr(typeListText, ",") > 0 Then
                runtimeArgStartPos += 1
            End If
        End If
    End If

    If argCount - runtimeArgStartPos > UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS Then
        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): supports at most 10 runtime arguments"
        Return 0
    End If

    If signatureText = "BYREF" Then
        argStartPos = runtimeArgStartPos
        If argStartPos >= UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Or argIdx(argStartPos) = -1 Then
            ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): BYREF requires addressable target argument"
            Return 0
        End If

        If IsAddressableByRefArgNode(ps, argIdx(argStartPos)) = 0 Then
            ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): BYREF requires addressable target argument"
            Return 0
        End If
    End If

    Return 1
End Function
                errText = "exec: unsupported call CALL"
                Return 0
            End If
            ffiBasePos = 1
        End If

        If ffiArgCount < ffiBasePos + 3 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) expects at least 4 arguments (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_ARITY)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim dllNode As Integer
        dllNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos)
        If dllNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(dllNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) library must be string literal (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_LIB)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim dllNameRaw As String
        dllNameRaw = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(dllNode).value)
        If callMarker = "API" And InStr(dllNameRaw, ".") = 0 Then dllNameRaw &= ".dll"

        Dim dllName As String
        If ExecNormalizeDllName(dllNameRaw, dllName, errText) = 0 Then
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim symbolNode As Integer
        symbolNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos + 1)
        If symbolNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(symbolNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) symbol must be string literal (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SYMBOL)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim symbolName As String
        symbolName = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(symbolNode).value)
        If symbolName = "" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) symbol cannot be empty (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SYMBOL)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim sigNode As Integer
        sigNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos + 2)
        If sigNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(sigNode).kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) signature token must be keyword (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SIGNATURE)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim signatureText As String
        signatureText = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(sigNode).value))
        If ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(signatureText) = 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid signature token " & signatureText & " (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SIGNATURE)) & ")"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim callingConventionText As String
        callingConventionText = "CDECL"

        Dim argStartPos As Integer
        argStartPos = ffiBasePos + 3

        If ffiArgCount >= ffiBasePos + 4 Then
            Dim convNode As Integer
            convNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, ffiBasePos + 3)
            If convNode <> -1 And UCase(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).kind) = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
                Dim convCandidate As String
                convCandidate = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).value))
                If ExecIsDllCallingConvention(convCandidate) = 0 Then
                    errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) invalid calling convention token " & convCandidate & " (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_BAD_SIGNATURE)) & ")"
                    Return 0
                End If
                callingConventionText = convCandidate
                argStartPos = ffiBasePos + 4
            End If
        End If

        Return ExecEvalDllCallCore(ps, es, callNodeIdx, dllName, symbolName, signatureText, callingConventionText, argStartPos, errText)
    Case "API"
        Dim apiArgCount As Integer
        apiArgCount = ExecChildCount(ps, callNodeIdx)
        If apiArgCount < 3 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) expects at least 3 arguments"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiLibNode As Integer
        apiLibNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 0)
        If apiLibNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(apiLibNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) library must be string literal"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiDllNameRaw As String
        apiDllNameRaw = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(apiLibNode).value)
        If apiDllNameRaw = "" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) library cannot be empty"
            Return 0
        End If

        If InStr(apiDllNameRaw, ".") = 0 Then apiDllNameRaw &= ".dll"

        Dim apiDllName As String
        If ExecNormalizeDllName(apiDllNameRaw, apiDllName, errText) = 0 Then
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSymbolNode As Integer
        apiSymbolNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 1)
        If apiSymbolNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(apiSymbolNode).kind) <> "STRING" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) symbol must be string literal"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSymbolName As String
        apiSymbolName = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(apiSymbolNode).value)
        If apiSymbolName = "" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) symbol cannot be empty"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSigNode As Integer
        apiSigNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, 2)
        If apiSigNode = -1 Or UCase(ps.ast.nodes(apiSigNode).kind) <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) signature token must be keyword"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim apiSignature As String
        apiSignature = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(apiSigNode).value))
        If ExecIsAllowedDllSignature(apiSignature) = 0 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(API) invalid signature token " & apiSignature
            Return 0
        End If

        Return ExecEvalDllCallCore(ps, es, callNodeIdx, apiDllName, apiSymbolName, apiSignature, "CDECL", 3, errText)
    End Select

    errText = "exec: unsupported call " & callKey
    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecCollectionGetHandleArg(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef callName As String, ByVal argPos As Integer, ByRef handleOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim nodeIdx As Integer
    nodeIdx = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argPos)
    If nodeIdx = -1 Then
        errText = "exec: " & callName & " handle arg missing"
        Return 0
    End If

    handleOut = ExecEvalNode(ps, es, nodeIdx, errText)
    If errText <> "" Then Return 0
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function ExecCollectionGetIntArg(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef callName As String, ByVal argPos As Integer, ByRef intOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    Dim nodeIdx As Integer
    nodeIdx = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argPos)
    If nodeIdx = -1 Then
        errText = "exec: " & callName & " integer arg missing"
        Return 0
    End If
Bridge ve DLL yönetimini gözden geçirme
Parser'ý sadece AST deðerine odaklanacak þekilde ayarlamalýyým, ve çocuk sayýsý baþladýktan sonra lib deðil, DLL iþaretçisi. Maksimum çocuk sayýsý 15 olacak, ki metaveri 5' e kadar desteklenebilir. Ayrýca, ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed fonksiyonunun, hedef DLL yüklemeden önce köprüyü yüklediðini kontrol etmeliyim.

Verifying DLL load sequence and memory leak
bash -lc sed -n '900,995p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs

        If t = "I32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CLng(iv.intValue))
        ElseIf t = "U32" Then
            u64Out = CULngInt(CUInt(iv.intValue))
        Else
            u64Out = CULngInt(iv.intValue)
        End If
        Return 1
    End Select
End Function

Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef retTypeText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, argTypes() As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0
    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() <> "WIN64-MSABI" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 10 Then Return 1

    Dim retType As String
    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(retTypeText, retType) = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 9) As ULongInt
    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 9) As Double
    Dim argStrings(0 To 9) As String
    Dim argMask As Integer
    argMask = 0
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos + i)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) mixed argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If
        Dim isF64 As Integer
        If ExecEvalDllArgAsMixedSlot(ps, es, argNode, argTypes(i), argStrings(i), argValuesU64(i), argValuesF64(i), isF64, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If isF64 <> 0 Then argMask = argMask Or (1 Shl i)
    Next i

    ' Prefer the optional libffi bridge for five-to-ten arguments.  For
    ' zero-to-four arguments it may also be used when present; otherwise the
    ' established direct dispatcher remains the compatibility fallback.
    Dim bridgeInvoked As Integer
    Dim bridgeStatus As Integer
    bridgeStatus = ExecFfiBridge10Invoke(dllName, symbolName, retType, argTypes(), argCount, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argMask, valueOut, bridgeInvoked, errText)
    If bridgeStatus = 0 Then Return 0
    If bridgeInvoked <> 0 Then
        invokedOut = 1
        Return 1
    End If

    If argCount > 4 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) 5..10 mixed arguments require optional uxffi.dll"
        Return 0
    End If

    If ExecX64DispatchMixedInvoke(procAddr, retType, argCount, argMask, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), valueOut, errText) = 0 Then
        Return 0
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub ExecNormalizeError(ByRef errText As String)
    If errText = "" Then Exit Sub

    Dim t As String
    t = LCase(LTrim(errText))
    If Left(t, Len(EXEC_ERR_PREFIX)) <> EXEC_ERR_PREFIX Then
        errText = EXEC_ERR_PREFIX & errText
    End If
End Sub

Private Function ExecConsumeLoopExit(ByRef es As ExecState, ByRef loopKind As String) As Integer
    If es.runtime.exitRequest = "" Then Return 0

    Dim req As String
    req = UCase(es.runtime.exitRequest)

    If req = "ANY" Or req = UCase(loopKind) Then
        es.runtime.exitRequest = ""
Bridge öncesi hedef yüklemeyi gözden geçirme
Köprü çaðrýsý, parametre evalue edilmeli ama hedef yüklenmemeli. Köprüyü hedef LoadLibrary öncesine alarak tekrar yüklemeyi önleriz ve köprüyü hedefin yüklenmesinde kullanabiliriz. Bu kurulumu güncellemeden sonra, hModule sýzýntýsý engellenir. Ancak mevcut doðrudan yükleme hala sýzdýrýyor; FreeLibrary çaðrýsý gerekip gerekmediðini kontrol etmeliyim.

Arama ve FreeLibrary bildirimlerini inceleme
bash -lc grep -RIn "Declare Function FreeLibrary\|FreeLibrary(" /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src/runtime | head -30
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src/runtime/native/uxb_runtime_loader.fbs:14:Declare Function FreeLibrary Lib "kernel32" Alias "FreeLibrary" (ByVal hLibModule As Any Ptr) As Integer
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src/runtime/native/uxb_runtime_loader.fbs:80:        FreeLibrary(rt.hModule)
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/src/runtime/native/uxb_runtime_loader.fbs:91:        FreeLibrary(rt.hModule)
Yükleme ve yapýlandýrma düzenlemeleri
Her þeyin düzgün çalýþmasý için dikkatli olmam gerek. Yapýcýyý ve manifest katmanýný deðiþtirmem gerekiyor, yüklemeyi de yeniden konumlandýrmalýyým. Bunun için bir Python betiði kullanmam lazým. Bu çözüm, daha verimli bir iþlem süreci saðlayacak gibi görünüyor.

Modifying builder, manifest, and load tier
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json
root=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot')
# Fix builder cleanly
p=root/'uxb/tools/library_tools/uxb_library_build.py'
p.write_text('''#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


def root_from_here() -> Path:
    """Return the uxb project root for uxb/tools/library_tools/*.py."""
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def run_batch(script: Path, working_directory: Path, log_path: Path, heading: str) -> int:
    """Run a Windows batch file and append its complete output to one log."""
    cmd = shutil.which("cmd.exe") or shutil.which("cmd")
    if not cmd:
        with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as fp:
            fp.write(f"\\n=== {heading} ===\\n")
            fp.write("ERROR: cmd.exe was not found; run this builder on Windows.\\n")
        return 127

    with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as fp:
        fp.write(f"\\n=== {heading} ===\\n")
        fp.flush()
        completed = subprocess.run(
            [cmd, "/d", "/c", str(script)],
            cwd=working_directory,
            stdout=fp,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            check=False,
        )
    return completed.returncode


def stage_binary(source: Path, destination: Path) -> bool:
    if not source.exists():
        return False
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copy2(source, destination)
    return True


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="uXBasic birleþik DLL üreticisi")
    parser.add_argument("--tier", choices=["core", "all"], default="core")
    parser.add_argument("--library", action="append", default=[], help="Yalnýz seçilen kütüphaneyi üret; birden çok kez verilebilir")
    parser.add_argument("--strict-optional", action="store_true", help="Ýsteðe baðlý kütüphane hatalarýný da baþarýsýz say")
    parser.add_argument("--no-build", action="store_true", help="Derleme yapmadan mevcut DLL'leri kanonik dizine taþý")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    root = root_from_here()
    manifest_path = root / "libraries" / "library_manifest.json"
    manifest: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    output_dir = root / manifest["canonical"]["binary_output"]
    dependency_dir = root / manifest["canonical"]["dependency_output"]
    log_dir = root / manifest["canonical"]["logs"]
    legacy_dir = root / manifest["compatibility"]["legacy_binary_output"]
    report_path = root / "dist" / "libraries" / "library_build_report.json"

    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dependency_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    legacy_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    requested_names = {name.strip().lower() for name in args.library if name.strip()}
    known_names = {item["name"].lower() for item in manifest["libraries"]}
    unknown = sorted(requested_names - known_names)
    if unknown:
        print("Bilinmeyen kütüphane: " + ", ".join(unknown), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    selected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in manifest["libraries"]:
        name = item["name"].lower()
        if requested_names and name not in requested_names:
            continue
        if not requested_names and args.tier == "core" and item["tier"] != "CORE":
            continue
        selected.append(item)

    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    hard_failures = 0
    working_directory = root.parent  # Existing batch files expect a parent containing uxb\\.

    for item in selected:
        started = time.time()
        name = item["name"]
        dll_name = item["dll"]
        build_script = root / item["build_script"]
        log_path = log_dir / f"{name}.log"
        legacy_binary = legacy_dir / dll_name
        canonical_binary = output_dir / dll_name
        exit_code: int | None = None
        status = ""

        log_path.write_text(
            f"uXBasic library build\\nname={name}\\nscript={build_script}\\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        if args.no_build:
            status = "SKIPPED"
        elif not build_script.exists():
            status = "SCRIPT_MISSING"
            exit_code = 127
        else:
            exit_code = run_batch(build_script, working_directory, log_path, "PRIMARY BUILD")
            if exit_code != 0 and item.get("fallback_script"):
                fallback = root / item["fallback_script"]
                if fallback.exists():
                    exit_code = run_batch(fallback, working_directory, log_path, "FALLBACK BUILD")
            status = "BUILT" if exit_code == 0 else "BUILD_FAILED"

        staged = stage_binary(legacy_binary, canonical_binary)
        if staged:
            if status == "SKIPPED":
                status = "EXISTS"
            dependency_script = item.get("copy_dependencies_script")
            if dependency_script and not args.no_build:
                script_path = root / dependency_script
                if script_path.exists():
                    dep_exit = run_batch(script_path, working_directory, log_path, "COPY DEPENDENCIES")
                    if dep_exit != 0 and status == "BUILT":
                        status = "DEPENDENCY_COPY_FAILED"
        elif status == "BUILT":
            status = "OUTPUT_MISSING"

        required = item["tier"] == "CORE" or args.strict_optional
        if required and status not in {"BUILT", "EXISTS"}:
            hard_failures += 1

        result = {
            **item,
            "required": required,
            "status": status,
            "exit_code": exit_code,
            "seconds": round(time.time() - started, 3),
            "legacy_output": str(legacy_binary),
            "canonical_output": str(canonical_binary),
            "log": str(log_path),
        }
        results.append(result)
        print(f"{name}: {status}")

    report = {
        "schema": "uxb.library_build_report.v1",
        "ok": hard_failures == 0,
        "tier": args.tier,
        "strict_optional": args.strict_optional,
        "hard_failures": hard_failures,
        "selected_count": len(selected),
        "results": results,
    }
    report_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    report_path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Rapor: {report_path}")
    return 0 if hard_failures == 0 else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
''', encoding='utf-8')

# Optional bridge must not make core build mandatory.
mp=root/'uxb/libraries/library_manifest.json'
data=json.loads(mp.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
for item in data['libraries']:
    if item['name']=='uxffi':
        item['tier']='OPTIONAL_ABI10'
mp.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)+"\n", encoding='utf-8')

# Move optional bridge before direct LoadLibrary/GetProcAddress in mixed path.
rp=root/'uxb/src/runtime/exec/exec_ffi_runtime.fbs'
s=rp.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
old='''    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 9) As ULongInt
'''
new='''    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 9) As ULongInt
'''
# only replace occurrence in mixed function: use split around function marker
marker='Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed('
pos=s.index(marker)
head=s[:pos]; tail=s[pos:]
if old not in tail:
    raise SystemExit('mixed pre-load block not found')
tail=tail.replace(old,new,1)
insert_after='''    If argCount > 4 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) 5..10 mixed arguments require optional uxffi.dll"
        Return 0
    End If

'''
direct='''    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

'''
if insert_after not in tail:
    raise SystemExit('insert point not found')
tail=tail.replace(insert_after,insert_after+direct,1)
rp.write_text(head+tail, encoding='utf-8')
PY
python -m py_compile /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/tools/library_tools/*.py

Doðrulama ve denetleme iþlemleri
Doðrulamalarý devam ettirmem gerekiyor. Ayrýca, örtücü iþlevleri gözden geçirmem ve betiði denetlemem lazým. Bu, doðru çalýþtýðýndan emin olmama yardýmcý olacak. Detaylý bir kontrol yaparak ihtiyaç duyulan deðiþiklikleri yapmalýyým.

Denetimler ve sarmalayýcý denetimi
bash -lc sed -n '1,340p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/tools/library_tools/uxb_library_abi_audit.py
printf '\n--- sample wrappers ---\n'
for f in uxaimath uxcollections uxmatrix uxonnx2; do echo "### $f"; sed -n '1,180p' "/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/libsx/$f/$f.bas" | head -120; done
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from pathlib import Path
import re,json,sys
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
valid={'VOID','I32','U32','I64','U64','F64','PTR','STRPTR','BYREF','BYVAL'}
rows=[];bad=0
for f in sorted((root/'libsx').glob('*/*.bas')):
    for no,line in enumerate(f.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore').splitlines(),1):
        if line.lstrip().startswith("'"): continue
        p=line.upper().find('CALL(DLL,')
        if p<0: continue
        # Contract check only: literal dll/symbol + return token + optional CDECL + type list.
        ok=bool(re.search(r'CALL\(DLL,\s*"[^"]+",\s*"[^"]+",\s*(VOID|I32|U32|I64|U64|F64|PTR|STRPTR|BYREF|BYVAL)\b',line,re.I))
        rows.append({'file':str(f.relative_to(root)),'line':no,'ok':ok,'text':line.strip()})
        if not ok: bad+=1
out=root/'dist/libraries';out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
(out/'wrapper_abi_audit.json').write_text(json.dumps({'schema':'uxb.wrapper_abi_audit.v1','ok':bad==0,'bad':bad,'rows':rows},indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
print(f'CALL(DLL)={len(rows)} bad={bad}')
raise SystemExit(0 if bad==0 else 1)

### uxaimath
' uXBasic AI Math Standard Module
' Include with: INCLUDE "libs/uxaimath/uxaimath.bas"
' Optional tensor bridge: include libs/uxtensor/uxtensor.bas first, then use Tensor* functions.
' Backend: uxaimath.dll, optionally linked with OpenBLAS.

NAMESPACE uxaimath

CONST UXAIMATH_DLL = "uxaimath.dll"

    RETURN CALL(DLL, "uxaimath.dll", "uxaimath_version", I32, CDECL)
END FUNCTION

Denetim ve baþlýk kaynaklarýný inceleme
Yeterince kaynaðýmýz var. Ancak, wrapper dönüþümü biraz þüpheli görünüyor; uxmatrix U64'ü ele alýrken, C baþlýklarý muhtemelen integer türünü kullanýyor. Prototiplerle denetlememiz gerek. Dönüþüm C türlerini tahmin etmiþ olabilir. Tüm wrapper çaðrýlarýný baþlýklara karþý dikkatlice denetlemeliyim. Orijinal kaynak baþlýklarýna ulaþmalýyým.

Denetim ve baþlýklarýn gözden geçirilmesi
Muhtemelen wrapper türleri yanlýþ oluþturulmuþ; typedef'in muhtemelen void* olmasý gerekiyor. Baþlýklarý kontrol etmem gerek.

Baþlýk ve hata türlerini inceleme

# uXBasic kütüphane düzeni ve ABI10 yamasý

Bu ZIP yalnýz yeni/deðiþen dosyalarý içerir. Proje köküne çýkarýldýðýnda `uxb/` üzerine yazýlýr.

## Saðlananlar

- Kanonik kütüphane görünümü: `libraries/native`, `libraries/wrappers`, `libraries/include` junctionlarý.
- Birleþik DLL üreticisi: `compiler/scripts/build_libraries.ps1|bat`.
- Kanonik binary dizini: `dist/libraries/bin`.
- Mevcut `dist/runtime_ext` geriye uyumlu staging alaný olarak korunur.
- Wrapper `CALL(DLL)` imzalarý literal DLL/sembol, dönüþ tipi, CDECL ve argüman tip listesiyle üretilmiþtir.
- Parser metadata alanlarýndan sonra en fazla **10 gerçek DLL argümanýný** kabul eder.
- Ýsteðe baðlý `uxffi.dll` libffi köprüsü, x64 karma tipli 5–10 argüman çaðrýlarýný saðlar.
- `uxffi.dll` yoksa mevcut 0–4 argüman doðrudan çaðrý yolu çalýþmaya devam eder.

## Uygulama

```powershell
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.ps1 -ProjectRoot C:\...\UXBc_Clean\UXBc
```

Çekirdek DLL’leri de üretmek için `-BuildCore`, tümünü denemek için `-BuildAll` eklenir.

## Gereksinim

`uxffi.dll` için MSYS2 UCRT64 ortamýnda `mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-libffi` ve `pkgconf` gerekir. Harici aðýr kütüphaneler kendi baðýmlýlýklarýný korur.

## Bilinen sýnýr

Bu yama FFI çaðrý taþýmasýný 10 argümana çýkarýr. AST interpreter’ýn F64/U64/STRPTR dönüþlerini genel `ExecValue` üzerinden eksiksiz taþýmasý ayrý bir yürütücü dönüþ-deðeri sprintidir; mevcut kaynakta bu dönüþler birçok yolda `Integer` üzerinden geçmektedir.
param([string]$ProjectRoot="",[switch]$BuildCore,[switch]$BuildAll)
$ErrorActionPreference="Stop"
$patchRoot=Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ProjectRoot)){$ProjectRoot=(Get-Location).Path}
$uxb=Join-Path $ProjectRoot "uxb"
if(!(Test-Path $uxb)){throw "uxb klasoru bulunamadi: $uxb"}
$payload=Join-Path $patchRoot "uxb"
$stamp=Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"
$backup=Join-Path $uxb "patch_backups\library_layout_$stamp"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $backup | Out-Null
Get-ChildItem $payload -Recurse -File | ForEach-Object {
  $rel=$_.FullName.Substring($payload.Length).TrimStart('\')
  $dst=Join-Path $uxb $rel
  if(Test-Path $dst){$bak=Join-Path $backup $rel; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $bak) | Out-Null; Copy-Item $dst $bak -Force}
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $dst) | Out-Null
  Copy-Item $_.FullName $dst -Force
}
$links=@{native="runtime_ext";wrappers="libsx";include="include\modules"}
foreach($name in $links.Keys){
  $link=Join-Path $uxb "libraries\$name"; $target=Join-Path $uxb $links[$name]
  if(!(Test-Path $link)){
    try { New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path $link -Target $target | Out-Null }
    catch { Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 (Join-Path (Split-Path $link) ($name+".path")) $target }
  }
}
Write-Host "PATCH OK. Backup: $backup"
if($BuildAll){& (Join-Path $uxb "compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1") -Tier all; exit $LASTEXITCODE}
if($BuildCore){& (Join-Path $uxb "compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1") -Tier core; exit $LASTEXITCODE}
Baþlýklarýn ve wrapper'larýn denetimi
Wrappert'larý iyileþtirmem gerek; muhtemelen baþlýklarý düzgün denetlememiþiz. uxmatrix'in argümanlarý PTR yerine I32 ile iþlerken tip dönüþümü I32'ye yapýlmýþ gibi. Birçok wrapper'ý düzeltmem gerekli. Ayrýca saðlam bir C baþlýk ve wrapper call parser’ý yapmam lazým.

Sayfa baþlýklarý ve parametre türü ayracý analizi
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import re, collections
root=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext')
types=collections.Counter()
for h in root.glob('*/*.h'):
    text=h.read_text(errors='ignore')
    # remove comments
    text=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/',' ',text,flags=re.S)
    text=re.sub(r'//.*',' ',text)
    for m in re.finditer(r'\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*_API\s+(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\((.*?)\)\s*;',text,re.S):
        ret=' '.join(m.group(1).split()); args=m.group(3)
        types[ret]+=1
        if args.strip()!='void' and args.strip():
            for a in args.split(','):
                a=' '.join(a.split())
                # approximate strip name and array
                types['ARG:'+a]+=1
print('return types')
for k,v in types.items():
    if not k.startswith('ARG:'): print(v,k)
print('\nfloat occurrences')
for h in root.glob('*/*.h'):
    for no,l in enumerate(h.read_text(errors='ignore').splitlines(),1):
        if re.search(r'\bfloat\b',l): print(h.parent.name,no,l.strip())
PY
return types

Baþlýk yapýlarýný denetlemek ve analiz etmek
Birkaç seçeneðimiz var. Baþlýklarýn tüm yapýsýný dikkatle incelememiz gerek. Clang'ýn bir parser’ýný kullanabilir miyiz? Linux baþlýklarý çoðunlukla platform makrolarý içeriyor, gcc ile ön iþleme yapabiliriz. Ancak Windows’da __declspec tanýmlamalarý farklý olabilir. Pycparser'ý da kontrol edebiliriz.

Kontrol edilen araçlar ve baþlýk yapýlarý
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
try:
 import pycparser; print('pycparser',pycparser.__version__)
except Exception as e: print('no',e)
PY
which clang || true
which gcc || true
pycparser 3.00
/usr/local/swift/usr/bin/clang
/usr/bin/gcc
Baþlýklarý iþleme ve analiz etme
Ýlk olarak pycparser kullanarak baþlýklarý iþlemek ve makrolarý temizlemek gerekecek. GCC ile ön iþleme yapabiliriz. Ayrýca typedef iþlemleri için haritalama yapmalý ve baþlýklarda genellikle yer alan stdint gibi dosyalarý da kontrol etmemiz gerek. Regex kullanarak prototipleri ayýklayabiliriz, ancak makrolara dikkat etmemiz lazým.

Baþlýklarý ön iþleme ve inceleme
bash -lc for h in /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext/*/*.h; do echo ==== $h; sed -n '1,80p' "$h" | tail -70; done | sed -n '1,1000p'

UXTENSOR_API void* uxtensor_create2d_f64(long long rows, long long cols);
UXTENSOR_API void* uxtensor_create3d_f64(long long a, long long b, long long c);
UXTENSOR_API void* uxtensor_create4d_f64(long long a, long long b, long long c, long long d);
UXTENSOR_API void  uxtensor_free(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API void* uxtensor_clone(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API void* uxtensor_reshape_copy(void* handle, int ndim, const long long* shape);
UXTENSOR_API void* uxtensor_slice_axis0_copy(void* handle, long long start, long long count);

UXTENSOR_API int       uxtensor_dtype(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API int       uxtensor_ndim(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API long long uxtensor_dim(void* handle, int axis);
UXTENSOR_API long long uxtensor_stride(void* handle, int axis);
UXTENSOR_API long long uxtensor_size(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API long long uxtensor_elem_size(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API void*     uxtensor_data_ptr(void* handle);

UXTENSOR_API int    uxtensor_fill_f64(void* handle, double value);
UXTENSOR_API int    uxtensor_zero(void* handle);
UXTENSOR_API double uxtensor_get_flat_f64(void* handle, long long index);
Prototip analizi ve tür çözümleme
Katý bir yama yapmak gerekiyor; C baþlýklarýný manuel olarak iþleyip, API prototiplerini düzgün þekilde analiz etmeye yönelik bir parser kurmamýz lazým. Öncelikle, ön iþleme satýrlarýný, yorumlarý ayýklamak ve typedef alias’larýný doðru þekilde çözümlemek önemli. Ardýndan parametre ve dönüþ türlerini eþleþtirip, uygun türleri normalize ederiz.

Prototip ve wrapper denetimi
bash -lc cat > /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/prototype_wrapper_audit.py <<'PY'
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import re, json, shutil

BASE=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base')
PATCH=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb')

# Library -> header path. Only wrapper-backed libraries are included.
headers={
 'uxaimath':'runtime_ext/uxaimath/uxaimath.h',
 'uxcollections':'runtime_ext/uxcollections/uxcollections.h',
 'uxdataframe':'runtime_ext/uxdataframe/uxdataframe.h',
 'uxdataset':'runtime_ext/uxdataset/uxdataset.h',
 'uxgraph':'runtime_ext/uxgraph/uxgraph.h',
 'uxllama':'runtime_ext/uxllama/uxllama.h',
 'uxmath':'runtime_ext/uxmath/uxmath.h',
 'uxmathcore':'runtime_ext/uxmathcore/uxmathcore.h',
 'uxmatrix':'runtime_ext/uxmatrix/uxmatrix.h',
 'uxnn':'runtime_ext/uxnn/uxnn.h',
 'uxnn2':'runtime_ext/uxnn2/uxnn2.h',
 'uxnn3':'runtime_ext/uxnn3/uxnn3.h',
 'uxonnx':'runtime_ext/uxonnx/uxonnx.h',
 'uxonnx2':'runtime_ext/uxonnx2/uxonnx2.h',
 'uxstats':'runtime_ext/uxstats/uxstats.h',
 'uxstats2':'runtime_ext/uxstats2/uxstats2.h',
 'uxtensor':'runtime_ext/uxtensor/uxtensor.h',
}
wrapper_paths={
 'uxstats2':'libsx/uxstats/uxstats2.bas',
}

def strip_comments(s:str)->str:
 s=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/',' ',s,flags=re.S)
 s=re.sub(r'//[^\n]*',' ',s)
 return s

def clean_preprocessor(s:str)->str:
 # Keep declaration text, remove directive lines and C++ guards/braces.
 lines=[]
 for line in s.splitlines():
  if line.lstrip().startswith('#'): continue
  line=line.replace('extern "C" {',' ').replace('extern "C"',' ')
  if line.strip() in {'{','}'}: continue
  lines.append(line)
 return '\n'.join(lines)

def canonical_spaces(s:str)->str:
 return ' '.join(s.replace('\n',' ').replace('\t',' ').split())

def remove_declspec_and_api(s:str)->str:
 s=re.sub(r'__declspec\s*\([^)]*\)',' ',s)
 s=re.sub(r'\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(?:_API|_IMPORT)\b',' ',s)
 return canonical_spaces(s)

def parse_typedefs(text:str)->dict[str,str]:
 aliases={}
 for stmt in text.split(';'):
  st=remove_declspec_and_api(canonical_spaces(stmt))
  if not st.startswith('typedef '): continue
  body=st[len('typedef '):].strip()
  # Skip function pointer typedefs, structs with bodies.
  if '(' in body or '{' in body: continue
  m=re.match(r'(.+?)([A-Za-z_]\w*)$',body)
  if not m: continue
  base=m.group(1).strip(); name=m.group(2)
  aliases[name]=base
 return aliases

def resolve_alias(t:str, aliases:dict[str,str])->str:
 seen=set()
 while t in aliases and t not in seen:
  seen.add(t); t=aliases[t].strip()
 return t

def strip_param_name(raw:str)->str:
 s=canonical_spaces(raw)
 if s in {'','void'}: return s
 # arrays become pointers
 s=re.sub(r'\[[^\]]*\]\s*$',' *',s)
 # remove trailing identifier, retaining pointer stars before it
 m=re.match(r'(.+?)([A-Za-z_]\w*)$',s)
 if m:
  prefix=m.group(1).rstrip()
  # If there is only one word, it is a type not a parameter name.
  if prefix and ((' ' in s) or ('*' in s)):
   s=prefix
 return canonical_spaces(s)

def normalize_ctype(raw:str, aliases:dict[str,str])->str:
 t=strip_param_name(raw)
 t=re.sub(r'\b(const|volatile|restrict|signed)\b',' ',t)
 t=canonical_spaces(t)
 # Resolve bare aliases and aliases next to pointer stars.
 bare=t.replace('*',' ').strip()
 if bare in aliases:
  resolved=resolve_alias(bare,aliases)
  stars='*' * t.count('*')
  t=canonical_spaces(resolved+' '+stars)
 return t

def ffi_type(raw:str, aliases:dict[str,str], is_return=False)->str:
 t=normalize_ctype(raw,aliases)
 tl=t.lower().strip()
 if '*' in tl:
  # char pointers are textual; all other pointers/handles are PTR.
  if re.search(r'\bchar\b',tl): return 'STRPTR'
  return 'PTR'
 if tl in aliases:
  return ffi_type(aliases[tl],aliases,is_return)
 if tl=='void': return 'VOID'
 if tl=='double': return 'F64'
 if tl=='float': return 'F32_UNSUPPORTED'
 if tl in {'int64_t','long long','long long int','__int64'}: return 'I64'
 if tl in {'uint64_t','unsigned long long','unsigned long long int','size_t','uintptr_t'}: return 'U64'
 if tl in {'uint32_t','unsigned int','unsigned','unsigned long'}: return 'U32'
 if tl in {'int','int32_t','short','short int','long','long int','int16_t','int8_t','char'}: return 'I32'
 if tl in {'uint16_t','uint8_t','unsigned short','unsigned char'}: return 'U32'
 # A bare typedef may still need resolving.
 if t in aliases: return ffi_type(aliases[t],aliases,is_return)
 return 'UNKNOWN:'+t

def split_top(s:str, sep=',')->list[str]:
 out=[]; start=0; depth=0; quote=None; i=0
 while i<len(s):
  ch=s[i]
  if quote:
   if ch==quote:
    # BASIC double quote escaping via doubled quotes
    if i+1<len(s) and s[i+1]==quote: i+=2; continue
    quote=None
   i+=1; continue
  if ch in {'"',"'"}: quote=ch; i+=1; continue
  if ch=='(': depth+=1
  elif ch==')': depth=max(0,depth-1)
  elif ch==sep and depth==0:
   out.append(s[start:i].strip()); start=i+1
  i+=1
 out.append(s[start:].strip())
 return out

def parse_header(path:Path):
 raw=strip_comments(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore'))
 raw=clean_preprocessor(raw)
 aliases=parse_typedefs(raw)
 protos={}
 for stmt in raw.split(';'):
  st=remove_declspec_and_api(canonical_spaces(stmt))
  if not st or st.startswith('typedef ') or '(' not in st or ')' not in st: continue
  st=st.strip(' {}')
  m=re.match(r'(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\((.*)\)\s*$',st)
  if not m: continue
  ret,name,args=m.groups()
  # Avoid control/garbage.
  if name in {'if','for','while','switch'}: continue
  argraw=[] if args.strip() in {'','void'} else split_top(args)
  protos[name]={
    'ret_c':canonical_spaces(ret),
    'arg_c':[canonical_spaces(x) for x in argraw],
    'ret':ffi_type(ret,aliases,True),
    'args':[ffi_type(x,aliases,False) for x in argraw],
  }
 return aliases,protos

def extract_calls(text:str):
 # Return start/end covering CALL(...) only, with balanced parens.
 result=[]; pat=re.compile(r'CALL\s*\(',re.I)
 for m in pat.finditer(text):
  # ignore comment portions on line before match
  line_start=text.rfind('\n',0,m.start())+1
  prefix=text[line_start:m.start()]
  if prefix.lstrip().startswith("'"): continue
  openpos=text.find('(',m.start())
  depth=0; quote=None; i=openpos
  while i<len(text):
   ch=text[i]
   if quote:
    if ch==quote:
     if i+1<len(text) and text[i+1]==quote: i+=2; continue
     quote=None
    i+=1; continue
   if ch=='"': quote='"'; i+=1; continue
   if ch=='(': depth+=1
   elif ch==')':
    depth-=1
    if depth==0:
     result.append((m.start(),i+1,text[openpos+1:i]))
     break
   i+=1
 return result

def quoted_value(s:str)->str|None:
 m=re.fullmatch(r'\s*"([^"]*)"\s*',s)
 return m.group(1) if m else None

def merged_wrapper(lib:str)->tuple[Path,str]:
 rel=Path(wrapper_paths.get(lib,f'libsx/{lib}/{lib}.bas'))
 pp=PATCH/rel; bp=BASE/rel
 chosen=pp if pp.exists() else bp
 return rel,chosen.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore')

all_protos={}; parse_errors=[]
for lib,hrel in headers.items():
 aliases,protos=parse_header(BASE/hrel)
 all_protos[lib]=protos
 if not protos: parse_errors.append((lib,'no prototypes'))

rows=[]; fixes={}; errors=[]
for lib in headers:
 rel,text=merged_wrapper(lib)
 calls=extract_calls(text)
 replacements=[]
 for start,end,inside in calls:
  parts=split_top(inside)
  if not parts or parts[0].strip().upper()!='DLL': continue
  if len(parts)<4:
   errors.append({'lib':lib,'file':str(rel),'kind':'call_too_short','call':text[start:end]}); continue
  dll=quoted_value(parts[1]); sym=quoted_value(parts[2])
  if not dll or not sym:
   errors.append({'lib':lib,'file':str(rel),'kind':'nonliteral_target','call':text[start:end]}); continue
  proto=all_protos[lib].get(sym)
  if not proto:
   errors.append({'lib':lib,'file':str(rel),'kind':'symbol_missing','symbol':sym,'call':text[start:end]}); continue
  # Locate convention and optional type list, preserving remaining expressions.
  conv_idx=None
  for idx,p in enumerate(parts[3:],3):
   if p.strip().upper() in {'CDECL','STDCALL'}: conv_idx=idx; break
  if conv_idx is None:
   # old wrappers: runtime expressions start after existing return token
   expr_start=4
  else:
   expr_start=conv_idx+1
   q=quoted_value(parts[expr_start]) if expr_start<len(parts) else None
   if q is not None and all(re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_]+',x.strip()) for x in q.split(',')):
    expr_start+=1
  exprs=parts[expr_start:]
  expected=proto['args']
  if len(exprs)!=len(expected):
   errors.append({'lib':lib,'file':str(rel),'kind':'arity_mismatch','symbol':sym,'expected':len(expected),'actual':len(exprs),'args_c':proto['arg_c'],'expressions':exprs,'call':text[start:end]})
   continue
  if proto['ret'].startswith('UNKNOWN') or proto['ret']=='F32_UNSUPPORTED' or any(x.startswith('UNKNOWN') or x=='F32_UNSUPPORTED' for x in expected):
   errors.append({'lib':lib,'file':str(rel),'kind':'unsupported_type','symbol':sym,'proto':proto}); continue
  meta=['DLL',f'"{dll}"',f'"{sym}"',proto['ret'],'CDECL']
  if expected: meta.append('"'+','.join(expected)+'"')
  new='CALL('+', '.join(meta+exprs)+')'
  old=text[start:end]
  replacements.append((start,end,new))
  current_ret=parts[3].strip().upper()
  current_types=[]
  if conv_idx is not None and conv_idx+1<len(parts):
   q=quoted_value(parts[conv_idx+1])
   if q is not None: current_types=[x.strip().upper() for x in q.split(',') if x.strip()]
  rows.append({'lib':lib,'file':str(rel),'symbol':sym,'expected_ret':proto['ret'],'current_ret':current_ret,'expected_args':expected,'current_args':current_types,'arity':len(exprs),'changed':old!=new})
 # Apply generated canonical calls to patch copy whenever changes exist or patch already owns file.
 newtext=text
 for start,end,new in reversed(replacements): newtext=newtext[:start]+new+newtext[end:]
 if newtext!=text:
  out=PATCH/rel; out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True); out.write_text(newtext,encoding='utf-8'); fixes[str(rel)]=sum(1 for r in rows if r['file']==str(rel) and r['changed'])

report={'schema':'uxb.wrapper_header_abi_audit.v1','prototype_counts':{k:len(v) for k,v in all_protos.items()},'parse_errors':parse_errors,'errors':errors,'rows':rows,'fixed_files':fixes}
out=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/wrapper_header_abi_audit.json')
out.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
print('prototypes',sum(len(v) for v in all_protos.values()),'calls',len(rows),'errors',len(errors),'fixed files',len(fixes))
from collections import Counter
print('error kinds',Counter(e['kind'] for e in errors))
for e in errors[:60]: print(json.dumps(e,ensure_ascii=False)[:900])

Parser'da makro kaldýrma düzeltmesi
Makro kaldýrma iþlemini düzeltmemiz gerek. API makrolarý yalnýzca _API/_IMPORT deðil, ayný zamanda _EXPORT olarak da hesaplanmalý. Ayrýca, stats2 baþlýðýnda eksik semboller olabilir, belki de C tanýmlarýyla uyumsuz bir baþlýk var. C kaynaklarýný araþtýrarak eksiklikleri gidermeliyim.

Makro kaldýrma ve baþlýk kontrolü
bash -lc for sym in uxstats2_normal_cdf uxstats2_normal_p2 uxstats2_t_p2 uxstats2_chisq_p uxstats2_f_p uxstats2_one_sample_z_stat uxstats2_hotelling_t2_2d; do echo ==== $sym; grep -RIn "\b$sym\b" /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext/uxstats2; done
printf '\nexport macros\n'; grep -RIn '^#define .*_EXPORT\|^#define .*_API\|^#define .*_IMPORT' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext/*/*.h | sed -n '1,240p'

==== uxstats2_hotelling_t2_2d
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext/uxstats2/uxstats2.c:130:UXSTATS2_EXPORT double uxstats2_hotelling_t2_2d(void*x1h,void*x2h,void*gh,double g1,double g2){ UXStats2Vec*x1=(UXStats2Vec*)x1h,*x2=(UXStats2Vec*)x2h,*g=(UXStats2Vec*)gh; if(!valid_vec(x1)||!valid_vec(x2)||!valid_vec(g)||x1->count!=x2->count||x1->count!=g->count) return NAN; double m11=0,m12=0,m21=0,m22=0; int n1=0,n2=0; for(int i=0;i<g->count;i++){ if(fabs(g->data[i]-g1)<1e-9){m11+=x1->data[i];m12+=x2->data[i];n1++;} else if(fabs(g->data[i]-g2)<1e-9){m21+=x1->data[i];m22+=x2->data[i];n2++;} } if(n1<=2||n2<=2) return NAN; m11/=n1;m12/=n1;m21/=n2;m22/=n2; double s11=0,s22=0,s12=0; for(int i=0;i<g->count;i++){ if(fabs(g->data[i]-g1)<1e-9){s11+=sqr(x1->data[i]-m11);s22+=sqr(x2->data[i]-m12);s12+=(x1->data[i]-m11)*(x2->data[i]-m12);} else if(fabs(g->data[i]-g2)<1e-9){s11+=sqr(x1->data[i]-m21);s22+=sqr(x2->data[i]-m22);s12+=(x1->data[i]-m21)*(x2->data[i]-m22);} } double den=n1+n2-2; s11/=den;s22/=den;s12/=den; double det=s11*s22-s12*s12; if(fabs(det)<UX2_EPS) return NAN; double d1=m11-m21,d2=m12-m22; double q=(s22*d1*d1-2*s12*d1*d2+s11*d2*d2)/det; return ((double)n1*n2/(n1+n2))*q; }
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext/uxstats2/uxstats2.c:131:UXSTATS2_EXPORT double uxstats2_hotelling_p_2d(void*x1h,void*x2h,void*gh,double g1,double g2){ UXStats2Vec*g=(UXStats2Vec*)gh; int n1=0,n2=0; for(int i=0;i<g->count;i++){ if(fabs(g->data[i]-g1)<1e-9)n1++; else if(fabs(g->data[i]-g2)<1e-9)n2++; } double t2=uxstats2_hotelling_t2_2d(x1h,x2h,gh,g1,g2); if(isnan(t2)||n1+n2<=3) return NAN; double p=2.0; double f=((n1+n2-p-1.0)*t2)/(p*(n1+n2-2.0)); return uxstats2_f_p(f,p,n1+n2-p-1.0); }

Makro regex ve C kaynaklarýný düzeltme
Makro regex'ini düzeltmem gerekebilir. C kaynaðýndaki dýþa aktarým prototiplerini de baþlýklara eklemeliyim, çünkü baþlýk eksik. Fonksiyon tanýmlarýný regex ile çýkarabilirim, ancak gövdeyi ayýklamak zor olabilir. Fonksiyon prototipleri için dýþa aktarýmlarýný doðru almak önemli, özellikle UXSTATS2_EXPORT ile tanýmlanan fonksiyonlar gibi.

Dýþa aktarýlan C fonksiyonlarýný iþleme ve analiz etme
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
p='/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/prototype_wrapper_audit.py'
s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()
s=s.replace("r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(?:_API|_IMPORT)\\b'", "r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(?:_API|_IMPORT|_EXPORT)\\b'")
# insert source parser after parse_header
needle="""def extract_calls(text:str):
"""
insert=r'''def parse_exported_c_definitions(path:Path, aliases:dict[str,str])->dict[str,dict]:
 raw=strip_comments(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='ignore'))
 protos={}
 # Runtime sources consistently place exported functions at line starts.
 pattern=re.compile(r'(?m)^\s*(?:[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(?:_API|_EXPORT|_IMPORT)\s+)(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(([^{};]*)\)\s*\{')
 for m in pattern.finditer(raw):
  ret,name,args=m.groups()
  argraw=[] if args.strip() in {'','void'} else split_top(args)
  protos[name]={
   'ret_c':canonical_spaces(ret),
   'arg_c':[canonical_spaces(x) for x in argraw],
   'ret':ffi_type(ret,aliases,True),
   'args':[ffi_type(x,aliases,False) for x in argraw],
  }
 return protos

'''
s=s.replace(needle,insert+needle)
old="""for lib,hrel in headers.items():
 aliases,protos=parse_header(BASE/hrel)
 all_protos[lib]=protos
 if not protos: parse_errors.append((lib,'no prototypes'))
"""
new="""for lib,hrel in headers.items():
 aliases,protos=parse_header(BASE/hrel)
 # Some headers lag their C implementation (notably uxstats2). Exported C
 # definitions are authoritative supplemental ABI declarations.
 for cpath in sorted((BASE/'runtime_ext'/lib).glob('*.c')):
  protos.update(parse_exported_c_definitions(cpath, aliases))
 all_protos[lib]=protos
 if not protos: parse_errors.append((lib,'no prototypes'))
"""
if old not in s: raise SystemExit('loop needle missing')
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s)
PY
python /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/prototype_wrapper_audit.py
prototypes 667 calls 542 errors 0 fixed files 1
error kinds Counter()
Stats2 güncellemelerini denetleme ve raporlama
Ýlk dosyalar düzeltildi, ancak sadece stats2'yi etkileyen deðiþiklikler var gibi görünüyor. Þimdi ise 542 çaðrýyý incelemem ve deðiþiklik yapýlan tüm wrapper dosyalarýnýn dâhil olduðunu doðrulamam gerekiyor. Ayrýca, wrapper çaðrýlarýn parametrelerinin 10’dan az olduðundan emin olmalýyým ve raporu hazýrlamalýyým.

Ýnceleme ve raporlama yapýlan deðiþiklikler
bash -lc python /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/prototype_wrapper_audit.py
python - <<'PY'
import json
p='/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/wrapper_header_abi_audit.json'
d=json.load(open(p,encoding='utf-8'))
print('errors',len(d['errors']),'rows',len(d['rows']),'fixed',d['fixed_files'])
print('max args',max(r['arity'] for r in d['rows']))
from collections import Counter
print('arity distribution',dict(sorted(Counter(r['arity'] for r in d['rows']).items())))
print('return types',Counter(r['expected_ret'] for r in d['rows']))
print('arg tokens',Counter(x for r in d['rows'] for x in r['expected_args']))
print('files',len(set(r['file'] for r in d['rows'])))
print('changed still',sum(r['changed'] for r in d['rows']))

Wrapper düzenlemeleri ve tür doðrulama gereklilikleri
Patch, audit deðiþiklikleri nedeniyle temel wrapper'larý içeriyor ve tüm 17 wrapper artýk deðiþtirilmiþ durumda. Parser’ýn tür listesinin tam sayýsýný doðrulayýp doðrulamadýðýný kontrol etmem gerekebilir çünkü runtime bunu zaten yapýyor. Fonksiyonun doðru dönüþ deðerlerini kullandýðýndan ve tür eþleþmelerinin düzgün olduðundan emin olmam önemlidir.

Kontrol edilen wrapper'lar ve çaðrýlar
Bazý wrapper'lar I32 olarak tanýmlanýp C'de PTR olarak kullanýlýyor. Bu uyumsuzluklarý tespit etmek gerekebilir, çünkü doðru bildiðimiz bazý çaðrýlar wrapper'da hatalý olabilir. C header'larýný da gözden geçirebiliriz; mevcut yapýlar DLL sigortalarýný uyumlu hale getirecek mi?

Denetim ve derleme betiklerinin doðrulamasý
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json
base=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base')
patch=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb')
d=json.loads((patch/'libraries/library_manifest.json').read_text())
for item in d['libraries']:
 print(item['name'],
       'build', (patch/item['build_script']).exists() or (base/item['build_script']).exists(),
       'fallback', (not item.get('fallback_script')) or (patch/item.get('fallback_script','')).exists() or (base/item.get('fallback_script','')).exists(),
       'deps', (not item.get('copy_dependencies_script')) or (patch/item.get('copy_dependencies_script','')).exists() or (base/item.get('copy_dependencies_script','')).exists(),
       'wrapper', (not item.get('wrapper')) or (patch/item.get('wrapper','')).exists() or (base/item.get('wrapper','')).exists(),
       'header', (not item.get('header')) or (patch/item.get('header','')).exists() or (base/item.get('header','')).exists())
PY

Baþlýk ve modül kontrolü
Baþlýk için uxstats2 stub'ý eksik gibi görünüyor. Þu anki uxstats2 baþlýðý modül stub'ý olmadan çalýþýyor. Eðer düzgün bir baþlýk yapýsý istiyorsak, runtime_ext/uxstats2/uxstats2.h gibi bir inceleme yapabiliriz. Yeni uxmh dosyasý oluþturmak, kullanýcýnýn istediði düzeni saðlayabilir.

Dosya yolu ve modül düzeni
Bu stublarýn yanlýþ yol kullanýmý var (libs/), ayrýca wrapper'larý içermiyor. Kullanýcý düzenin saðlanmasýný istiyor. Tüm dosyalarý gözden geçirip, sadece deðiþiklik yapýlanlarý patch'leyeceðiz. .uxmh dosyalarýnda #include once gibi uygun sözdizimini eklemeliyiz. Ýçeriði kaynak dosyalarýna yönlendiren baþlýklar oluþturulabilir.

Ýnclude kullanýmýný inceleme ve analiz etme
bash -lc grep -RIn --include='*.uxb' --include='*.bas' --include='*.uxmh' 'include/modules\|uxmath\.uxmh\|uxstats\.uxmh\|INCLUDE "libsx' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base | head -200

Kontrol etme zamaný
Aþýrýya kaçmamak lazým. include/libs kýsmýný kontrol etmem gerek.

Dosyadaki include/libs kontrolü
bash -lc cat /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/include/libs.uxmh
' Auto-generated UXBc all-libraries header
#ifndef UXB_ALL_LIBS_UXMH
#define UXB_ALL_LIBS_UXMH

INCLUDE "modules/uxaimath.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxcollections.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxdataframe.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxdataset.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxgraph.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxllama.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxllamax.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxmath.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxmathcore.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxmatrix.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxnn.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxnn2.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxnn3.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxonnx.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxonnx2.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxstats.uxmh"
INCLUDE "modules/uxtensor.uxmh"

#endif
Düzenleme ve korumalar ekleme zamaný
.uxmh dosyalarýný daha iþlevsel yapmak için, her modülü doðru þekilde include ettiklerinden emin olmalýyým. Path'in kök klasörle uyumlu olduðunu varsayýyorum. 17 wrapper'ý ve uxstats2'yi düzenlerken, gerekli korumalarý eklemem gerekecek. uxaimath ve uxtensor arasýndaki baðýmlýlýðý da göz önünde bulundurarak include sýralamalarýný yapmalýyým.

Geriye dönelim ve kontrol edelim
Dosya içeriði bir "backtick" içeriyor gibi görünüyor. Þu kodda:
for /f "usebackq tokens=*" %%I in ("%PKG%" --cflags --libs libffi) do
Sanýrým shell nedeniyle backtick kullanýlýyor. Hadi, bunun ne olduðunu belirlemek için hexadecimal formatýnda inceleyelim.

Backtick karakterlerini kontrol etme
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
p='/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/runtime_ext/uxffi/build_uxffi_dll.bat'
for l in open(p,encoding='utf-8'):
 if 'for /f' in l: print(repr(l))
PY
'for /f "usebackq tokens=*" %%I in (`"%PKG%" --cflags --libs libffi`) do set "FFIFLAGS=%%I"\n'
Yapýlmasý gerekenler
Windows'ta FOR /F komutu usebackq ile backtick kullanarak komut çalýþtýrýyor, evet doðru. Þimdi, libffi baðýmlýlýklarýný doðru þekilde kopyalamam gerekiyor. Belirtilen deps klasörüne ve ayrýca bin klasörüne kopyalayabilirim. Baðýmlýlýklar için bu kopyalama iþlemi derleme sürecinin bir parçasý olmalý.

Fazla dosya betiði incelemesi
bash -lc for f in /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/compiler/scripts/build_libraries.bat /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/compiler/scripts/build_libraries.ps1 /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/compiler/scripts/build_all_runtime_ext_dlls.bat /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.bat; do echo ====; cat "$f"; done
====
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0\..\..\.."
python uxb\tools\library_tools\uxb_library_build.py %*
exit /b %errorlevel%
====
param([ValidateSet("core","all")][string]$Tier="core",[string[]]$Library=@(),[switch]$StrictOptional,[switch]$NoBuild)
$ErrorActionPreference="Stop"
$repo=(Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\..\..")).Path
$args=@("uxb\tools\library_tools\uxb_library_build.py","--tier",$Tier)
foreach($n in $Library){$args += @("--library",$n)}
if($StrictOptional){$args += "--strict-optional"}
if($NoBuild){$args += "--no-build"}
Push-Location $repo
try { & python @args; exit $LASTEXITCODE } finally { Pop-Location }
====
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0\..\..\.."
echo === Compatibility entry: unified uXBasic library build ===
python uxb\tools\library_tools\uxb_library_build.py --tier all
exit /b %errorlevel%
====
@echo off
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0APPLY_UXB_LIBRARY_PATCH.ps1" %*
exit /b %errorlevel%
Gereken adýmlar
Belki py -3 komutunu bir yedek olarak eklemeliyim. Windows kullanýcýlarýnýn Python’a sahip olduðunu varsayýyorum. Ayrýca, docs dizini oluþturulmalý ve FBS derlenmeli. Ancak fbc yok, o yüzden sözdizimini incelemem gerekecek. Belki FreeBasic derleyicisini içeren test araçlarýný kullanabilirim.

Dosya arama ve derleyici incelemesi
bash -lc find /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base -iname 'fbc.exe' -o -iname 'fbc' | head -40
find /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base -iname 'gcc.exe' -o -iname 'clang.exe' | head -20
find /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base -maxdepth 3 -type f -iname 'uxb*.exe' | head -30
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/bin/uxb.exe
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/compiler/wrappers/uxb.exe
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/compiler/wrappers/uxb_main_wrapper_64.exe
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/compiler/wrappers/uxb_main_wrapper_64.tmp.exe
Test etme planý
Test etmek için temel uxb.exe kullanmayý düþünebilirim, test kopyasýnda üst katman (overlay) ekleyerek. Ancak, bu Windows üzerinde çalýþmayan bir program olabilir. Muhtemelen Wine kullanýlamaz, o yüzden bunu kontrol etmem gerekecek.

Kontrol edilen dosya türü ve Wine durumu
bash -lc which wine || true; file /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/bin/uxb.exe
/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/bin/uxb.exe: PE32 executable for MS Windows 4.00 (console), Intel i386, 17 sections
Patch geliþtirme ve denetim
Yamanýn güvenilirliðini artýrmaya odaklanmalýyým. Þu anki uxb_library_abi_audit.py dosyasýnýn yerine kullanýcý dostu, daha saðlam bir denetim scripti oluþturulmalý. Bu script baþlýklarý ve C kaynaklarýný inceleyip hatalarý raporlayacak, ama oto-düzeltme yapmayacak. Bunu eden kapsamlý bir ABI denetimi sunmalý.

Parsing build scripts for validation
bash -lc for f in /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/runtime_ext/*/build_*_dll.bat; do echo "==== ${f#*/base/}"; cat "$f" | sed -n '1,100p'; done > /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/all_build_scripts.txt
sed -n '1,1400p' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/all_build_scripts.txt
==== runtime_ext/bigfp/build_bigfp_dll.bat
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0..\..\.."

set "GCC=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\gcc.exe"
set "OUTDIR=uxb\dist\runtime_ext"
set "OUTDLL=%OUTDIR%\uxb_bigfp.dll"
set "IMPLIB=%OUTDIR%\libuxb_bigfp.dll.a"

if not exist "%GCC%" (
  echo ERROR: MSYS2 UCRT64 gcc not found: %GCC%
  echo Install MSYS2 UCRT64 and run:
  echo   pacman -S mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-gcc mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-mpfr mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-gmp
  exit /b 2
)

if not exist "%OUTDIR%" mkdir "%OUTDIR%"

set "PATH=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin;%PATH%"

"%GCC%" -O2 -shared -o "%OUTDLL%" "uxb\runtime_ext\bigfp\uxb_bigfp_mpfr.c" -Wl,--out-implib,"%IMPLIB%" -lmpfr -lgmp
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo ERROR: BIGF/BIGD/BALL runtime DLL build failed.
  echo Install dependencies in MSYS2 UCRT64:
  echo   pacman -S mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-mpfr mingw-w64-ucrt-x86_64-gmp
  exit /b 1
)

echo OK: %OUTDLL%
endlocal

==== runtime_ext/uxaimath/build_uxaimath_dll.bat
@echo off
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0\..\..\.."
set "GCC=C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin\gcc.exe"
set "OUTDIR=uxb\dist\runtime_ext"
if not exist "%OUTDIR%" mkdir "%OUTDIR%"
if not exist "%GCC%" (
  echo ERROR: MSYS2 UCRT64 gcc not found: %GCC%
  echo Run: uxb\runtime_ext\uxaimath\fetch_uxaimath_deps_msys2.bat
  exit /b 2
)
"%GCC%" -O2 -shared -DUXAIMATH_USE_CBLAS -IC:\msys64\ucrt64\include -o "%OUTDIR%\uxaimath.dll" "uxb\runtime_ext\uxaimath\uxaimath.c" -LC:\msys64\ucrt64\lib -lopenblas
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo ERROR: OpenBLAS build failed. Try fallback:
  echo   uxb\runtime_ext\uxaimath\build_uxaimath_fallback_dll.bat
  exit /b 1
)
call uxb\runtime_ext\uxaimath\copy_uxaimath_runtime_deps.bat
exit /b %ERRORLEVEL%
