Private Function ExecTryInvokeResolvedX64Mixed(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByRef es As ExecState, ByVal callNodeIdx As Integer, ByVal argStartPos As Integer, ByRef dllName As String, ByRef symbolName As String, ByRef retTypeText As String, ByRef callingConventionText As String, argTypes() As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByRef valueOut As Integer, ByRef invokedOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    valueOut = 0
    If ExecGetActiveFfiAbiName() <> "WIN64-MSABI" Then Return 1
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > 10 Then Return 1

    Dim retType As String
    If ExecFfiNormalizeTypeToken(retTypeText, retType) = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 9) As ULongInt
    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 9) As Double
    Dim argStrings(0 To 9) As String
    Dim argMask As Integer
    argMask = 0
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = ExecChildAt(ps, callNodeIdx, argStartPos + i)
        If argNode = -1 Then
            errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) mixed argument missing"
            Return 0
        End If
        Dim isF64 As Integer
        If ExecEvalDllArgAsMixedSlot(ps, es, argNode, argTypes(i), argStrings(i), argValuesU64(i), argValuesF64(i), isF64, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        If isF64 <> 0 Then argMask = argMask Or (1 Shl i)
    Next i

    ' Prefer the optional libffi bridge for five-to-ten arguments.  For
    ' zero-to-four arguments it may also be used when present; otherwise the
    ' established direct dispatcher remains the compatibility fallback.
    Dim bridgeInvoked As Integer
    Dim bridgeStatus As Integer
    bridgeStatus = ExecFfiBridge10Invoke(dllName, symbolName, retType, argTypes(), argCount, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argMask, valueOut, bridgeInvoked, errText)
    If bridgeStatus = 0 Then Return 0
    If bridgeInvoked <> 0 Then
        invokedOut = 1
        Return 1
    End If

    If argCount > 4 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) 5..10 mixed arguments require optional uxffi.dll"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim hModule As Any Ptr
    hModule = LoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If hModule = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 LoadLibrary failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_DLL_LOAD_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = GetProcAddress(hModule, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        errText = "exec: CALL(DLL) x64 GetProcAddress failed (" & LTrim(Str(UXB_FFI_ERR_SYMBOL_RESOLVE_FAILED)) & ")"
        Return 0
    End If

    If ExecX64DispatchMixedInvoke(procAddr, retType, argCount, argMask, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), valueOut, errText) = 0 Then
        Return 0
    End If

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub ExecNormalizeError(ByRef errText As String)
    If errText = "" Then Exit Sub

    Dim t As String
    t = LCase(LTrim(errText))
    If Left(t, Len(EXEC_ERR_PREFIX)) <> EXEC_ERR_PREFIX Then
        errText = EXEC_ERR_PREFIX & errText
    End If
End Sub

Private Function ExecConsumeLoopExit(ByRef es As ExecState, ByRef loopKind As String) As Integer
    If es.runtime.exitRequest = "" Then Return 0

    Dim req As String
    req = UCase(es.runtime.exitRequest)

    If req = "ANY" Or req = UCase(loopKind) Then
        es.runtime.exitRequest = ""
        Return 1
    End If

    Return 2
End Function

Private Function ExecIsStmtNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer) As Integer
    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then Return 0

    Dim kindUpper As String
    kindUpper = UCase(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind)

    If Len(kindUpper) < 5 Then Return 0
    If Right(kindUpper, 5) = "_STMT" Then Return 1
    Return 0
End Function

Private Function ExecClampI32(ByVal valueIn As Double) As Integer
    If valueIn > 2147483647# Then Return 2147483647
    If valueIn < -2147483648# Then Return -2147483648

    If pipeInputReady <> 0 Then
        MIRSetVarValue state, "INPUT", pipeInput
    End If

    If pipeOutputWanted <> 0 Then
        Dim zeroOut As MIRValue
        zeroOut.valueType = "INTEGER"
        zeroOut.intValue = 0
        zeroOut.floatValue = 0
        zeroOut.stringValue = ""
        zeroOut.arrayId = -1
        zeroOut.arrayLower = 0
        zeroOut.arraySize = 0

    End If

    If MIRInvokeRoutineNoArg(state, gMirSlotEntries(entryIdx).routineName, ret, errMsg) = 0 Then Return 0

    If pipeOutputWanted <> 0 Then
        If MIRGetVarValue(state, "OUTPUT", ret) = 0 Then
            ret.valueType = "INTEGER"
            ret.intValue = 0
            ret.floatValue = 0
            ret.stringValue = ""
            ret.arrayId = -1
            ret.arrayLower = 0
            ret.arraySize = 0
        End If
    End If

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function MIRTryInvokeSlotByCallName(ByRef state As MIREvaluatorState, ByRef fnName As String, ByRef arg1 As MIRValue, ByVal hasArg1 As Integer, ByRef ret As MIRValue, ByRef handledOut As Integer, ByRef errMsg As String) As Integer
    handledOut = 0

    Dim callName As String
    callName = UCase(Trim(fnName))
    If callName = "" Then Return 1

    Dim laneKinds(0 To 3) As String
    laneKinds(0) = "PIPE"
    laneKinds(1) = "EVENT"
    laneKinds(2) = "THREAD"
    laneKinds(3) = "PARALEL"

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        Dim idx As Integer
        idx = MIRSlotFindEntry(laneKinds(i), callName, -1, 0)
        If idx = -1 Then Continue For

        handledOut = 1
        If laneKinds(i) = "PIPE" Then
            If MIRSlotTriggerEntry(state, idx, hasArg1, arg1, 1, ret, errMsg) = 0 Then Return 0
        Else
            If MIRSlotTriggerEntry(state, idx, 0, arg1, 0, ret, errMsg) = 0 Then Return 0
            ret.valueType = "INTEGER"
            ret.intValue = 0
        End If
        Return 1
    Next i

    Return 1
End Function

Private Function MIRHandleSlotBuiltinCall(ByRef state As MIREvaluatorState, ByRef fnName As String, ByRef ret As MIRValue, ByRef handledOut As Integer, ByRef errMsg As String) As Integer
    handledOut = 1

    Select Case fnName
    Case "SLOT_DEF"
        If gMirInstr.operandCount < 5 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_DEF kind,name,id,routine ister"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim kindText As String
        Dim slotName As String
        Dim routineName As String
        If MIRCallArgText(state, 0, kindText) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_DEF kind cozumlene medi": Return 0
        If MIRCallArgText(state, 1, slotName) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_DEF name cozumlene medi": Return 0
        If MIRCallArgText(state, 3, routineName) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_DEF routine cozumlene medi": Return 0

        Dim idValue As MIRValue
        Dim slotId As LongInt
        If MIRTryGetCallArg(state, 2, idValue) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_DEF id cozumlene medi": Return 0
        If MIRAsInteger(idValue, slotId) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_DEF id sayisal olmali": Return 0

        If MIRSlotEnsureEntry(kindText, slotName, CInt(slotId), routineName, errMsg) = 0 Then Return 0
        ret.valueType = "INTEGER"
        ret.intValue = 0
        Return 1

    Case "SLOT_CAP"
        If gMirInstr.operandCount < 3 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_CAP kind,count ister"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim capKind As String
        If MIRCallArgText(state, 0, capKind) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_CAP kind cozumlene medi": Return 0
        Dim capKindNorm As String
        If MIRSlotNormalizeKind(capKind, capKindNorm) = 0 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_CAP invalid kind " & capKind
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim capValueRaw As MIRValue
        Dim capValue As LongInt
        If MIRTryGetCallArg(state, 1, capValueRaw) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_CAP count cozumlene medi": Return 0
        If MIRAsInteger(capValueRaw, capValue) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_CAP count sayisal olmali": Return 0
        If capValue < 0 Or capValue > 256 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: SLOT_CAP count out of range"
            Return 0
        End If

        MIRSlotSetCapacity capKindNorm, CInt(capValue)
        ret.valueType = "INTEGER"
        ret.intValue = CInt(capValue)
        Return 1

    Case "SLOT_ON", "SLOT_OFF", "SLOT_TRIGGER"
        If gMirInstr.operandCount < 3 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: " & fnName & " kind,target ister"
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim laneKind As String
        If MIRCallArgText(state, 0, laneKind) = 0 Then errMsg = "MIR: slot kind cozumlene medi": Return 0
        If MIRSlotNormalizeKind(laneKind, laneKind) = 0 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: invalid slot kind " & laneKind
            Return 0
        End If

        Dim targetName As String
        Dim targetId As Integer
        Dim preferId As Integer
        If MIRSlotParseTarget(state, 1, targetName, targetId, preferId, errMsg) = 0 Then Return 0

        Dim targetIdx As Integer
        targetIdx = MIRSlotFindEntry(laneKind, targetName, targetId, preferId)
        If targetIdx = -1 Then
            errMsg = "MIR: slot target not found"
            Return 0
        End If

        If fnName = "SLOT_ON" Then
            gMirSlotEntries(targetIdx).active = 1
            ret.valueType = "INTEGER"
            ret.intValue = 1
            Return 1
        End If

        If fnName = "SLOT_OFF" Then
            gMirSlotEntries(targetIdx).active = 0
            ret.valueType = "INTEGER"
            ret.intValue = 0
            Return 1
        End If

        Dim pipeInput As MIRValue
        Dim hasPipeInput As Integer
        hasPipeInput = 0
        If laneKind = "PIPE" And gMirInstr.operandCount >= 4 Then
            If MIRTryGetCallArg(state, 2, pipeInput) <> 0 Then hasPipeInput = 1
        End If

        If MIRSlotTriggerEntry(state, targetIdx, hasPipeInput, pipeInput, IIf(laneKind = "PIPE", 1, 0), ret, errMsg) = 0 Then Return 0
        Return 1
    End Select

    handledOut = 0
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function MIRIsDllConvention(ByRef textIn As String) As Integer
    Dim t As String
    t = UCase(Trim(textIn))
    Return IIf(t = "CDECL" Or t = "STDCALL", 1, 0)
End Function

' MIR Win64 mixed FFI type aliases (U=integer/pointer class, F=double/XMM class)

Private Function MIRDispatchX64MixedCall(ByVal procAddr As Any Ptr, ByRef retType As String, ByVal argCount As Integer, ByVal argMask As Integer, argValuesU64() As ULongInt, argValuesF64() As Double, ByRef rvU64 As ULongInt, ByRef rvF64 As Double, ByRef usesF64Return As Integer, ByRef errMsg As String) As Integer
    Dim retClass As String
    retClass = UCase(Trim(retType))
    If retClass = "F64" Then
        usesF64Return = 1
    Else
        usesF64Return = 0
    End If

    If usesF64Return <> 0 Then
        Select Case argCount
        Case 0
            Dim fn0F As MIRFnNoArgF64
            fn0F = procAddr
            rvF64 = fn0F()
        Case 1
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_F64_1_0 As MIRFnArg1F64_U
                fn_F64_1_0 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_1_0(argValuesU64(0))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_F64_1_1 As MIRFnArg1F64_F
                fn_F64_1_1 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_1_1(argValuesF64(0))
            Case Else
                errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL x64 mixed arg mask unsupported"
                Return 0
            End Select
        Case 2
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_F64_2_0 As MIRFnArg2F64_UU
                fn_F64_2_0 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_2_0(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_F64_2_1 As MIRFnArg2F64_FU
                fn_F64_2_1 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_2_1(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1))
            Case 2
                Dim fn_F64_2_2 As MIRFnArg2F64_UF
                fn_F64_2_2 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_2_2(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1))
            Case 3
                Dim fn_F64_2_3 As MIRFnArg2F64_FF
                fn_F64_2_3 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_2_3(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1))
            Case Else
                errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL x64 mixed arg mask unsupported"
                Return 0
            End Select
        Case 3
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_F64_3_0 As MIRFnArg3F64_UUU
                fn_F64_3_0 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_0(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_F64_3_1 As MIRFnArg3F64_FUU
                fn_F64_3_1 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_1(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 2
                Dim fn_F64_3_2 As MIRFnArg3F64_UFU
                fn_F64_3_2 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_2(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 3
                Dim fn_F64_3_3 As MIRFnArg3F64_FFU
                fn_F64_3_3 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_3(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesU64(2))
            Case 4
                Dim fn_F64_3_4 As MIRFnArg3F64_UUF
                fn_F64_3_4 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_4(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case 5
                Dim fn_F64_3_5 As MIRFnArg3F64_FUF
                fn_F64_3_5 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_5(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case 6
                Dim fn_F64_3_6 As MIRFnArg3F64_UFF
                fn_F64_3_6 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_6(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case 7
                Dim fn_F64_3_7 As MIRFnArg3F64_FFF
                fn_F64_3_7 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_3_7(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesF64(2))
            Case Else
                errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL x64 mixed arg mask unsupported"
                Return 0
            End Select
        Case 4
            Select Case argMask
            Case 0
                Dim fn_F64_4_0 As MIRFnArg4F64_UUUU
                fn_F64_4_0 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_0(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 1
                Dim fn_F64_4_1 As MIRFnArg4F64_FUUU
                fn_F64_4_1 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_1(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 2
                Dim fn_F64_4_2 As MIRFnArg4F64_UFUU
                fn_F64_4_2 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_2(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 3
                Dim fn_F64_4_3 As MIRFnArg4F64_FFUU
                fn_F64_4_3 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_3(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 4
                Dim fn_F64_4_4 As MIRFnArg4F64_UUFU
                fn_F64_4_4 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_4(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesF64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 5
                Dim fn_F64_4_5 As MIRFnArg4F64_FUFU
                fn_F64_4_5 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_5(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesF64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 6
                Dim fn_F64_4_6 As MIRFnArg4F64_UFFU
                fn_F64_4_6 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_6(argValuesU64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesF64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 7
                Dim fn_F64_4_7 As MIRFnArg4F64_FFFU
                fn_F64_4_7 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_7(argValuesF64(0), argValuesF64(1), argValuesF64(2), argValuesU64(3))
            Case 8
                Dim fn_F64_4_8 As MIRFnArg4F64_UUUF
                fn_F64_4_8 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_8(argValuesU64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesF64(3))
            Case 9
                Dim fn_F64_4_9 As MIRFnArg4F64_FUUF
                fn_F64_4_9 = procAddr
                rvF64 = fn_F64_4_9(argValuesF64(0), argValuesU64(1), argValuesU64(2), argValuesF64(3))

Private Sub MIREvaluatorStateInit(ByRef state As MIREvaluatorState)
    state.currentFunction = -1
    state.currentBlock = -1
    state.currentInstruction = -1
    state.varCount = 0
    state.arrayCount = 0
    state.callStackDepth = 0
    state.exitRequested = 0

    gMirMemHighCount = 0
    Erase gMirMemHigh
    gMirVarPtrCount = 0
    Erase gMirVarPtrEntries
    gMirVarPtrNextAddr = &h21000000UL
    gMirObjectClassCount = 0
    Erase gMirObjectClassEntries
    gMirCollectionCount = 0
    Erase gMirCollections
    gMirSlotEntryCount = 0
    Erase gMirSlotEntries
    gMirSlotCapEvent = 0
    gMirSlotCapThread = 0
    gMirSlotCapParalel = 0
    gMirSlotCapPipe = 0
    gMirPrintColumnPos = 0
    UxbConsoleReset
End Sub

#include once "mir_evaluator_memory_helpers.fbs"

Private Function MIRArrayBoundsToCount(ByVal lowerBound As Integer, ByVal upperBound As Integer) As Integer
    Return (upperBound - lowerBound) + 1
End Function

Private Function MIRGetArrayStore(ByRef state As MIREvaluatorState, ByVal arrayId As Integer) As MIRArrayStorage Ptr
    If arrayId < 0 Or arrayId >= state.arrayCount Then Return 0
    Return @state.arrays(arrayId)
End Function

Private Function MIREnsureArrayStore(ByRef state As MIREvaluatorState, ByRef arrayValue As MIRValue) As MIRArrayStorage Ptr
    If arrayValue.arrayId >= 0 And arrayValue.arrayId < state.arrayCount Then
        Return @state.arrays(arrayValue.arrayId)
    End If

    If state.arrayCount = 0 Then
        ReDim state.arrays(0)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve state.arrays(state.arrayCount)
    End If

    arrayValue.arrayId = state.arrayCount
    state.arrayCount += 1
    state.arrays(arrayValue.arrayId).lowerBound = 0
    state.arrays(arrayValue.arrayId).elementCount = 0
    Erase state.arrays(arrayValue.arrayId).elements
    Return @state.arrays(arrayValue.arrayId)
End Function

Private Function MIRResizeArrayValue(ByRef state As MIREvaluatorState, ByRef arrayValue As MIRValue, ByVal lowerBound As Integer, ByVal upperBound As Integer, ByVal preserveMode As Integer) As Integer
    Dim newCount As Integer
    newCount = MIRArrayBoundsToCount(lowerBound, upperBound)
    If newCount < 0 Then Return 0

    Dim arrStore As MIRArrayStorage Ptr
    arrStore = MIREnsureArrayStore(state, arrayValue)
    If arrStore = 0 Then Return 0

' uXBasic MIR (Mid-Level Intermediate Representation)
' Basic block tabanli, backend bagimsiz ara format
' Runtime evaluator icin MIR instruction set

#include once "mir_model.fbs"

Declare Function MIRChildAt(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer, ByVal childPos As Integer) As Integer

Private Sub MIRSetCurrentSourceFromAstNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer)
    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then
        MIRSetCurrentSourceFromParts "", 0, 0, 0, "", 0
        gMIRCurrentAstNodeId = -1
        Exit Sub
    End If
    MIRSetCurrentSourceFromParts ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).sourceFile, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).lineNo, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).colNo, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).sourceLength, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).lexemeSnippet, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).sourceOffset
    gMIRCurrentAstNodeId = nodeIdx
End Sub

Private Sub MIREnsureMain(ByRef module As MIRModule)
    Dim fnMain As MIRFunction Ptr

    If module.functionCount <> 0 Then Exit Sub

    ReDim module.functions(0)
    module.functionCount = 1
    module.functions(0).name = "MAIN"
    module.functions(0).entryBlock = 0
    fnMain = @module.functions(0)
    ReDim fnMain->blocks(0)
    module.functions(0).blockCount = 1
    module.functions(0).blocks(0).id = 0
    module.functions(0).blocks(0).instructionCount = 0
    module.functions(0).blocks(0).isEntry = 1
    module.functions(0).blocks(0).isExit = 1
End Sub

Private Sub MIRAppendInstruction(ByRef module As MIRModule, ByVal fnIdx As Integer, ByVal blockIdx As Integer, ByRef opcode As String, ByRef resultName As String, ByVal operandCount As Integer, ByRef op0 As String = "", ByRef op1 As String = "", ByRef op2 As String = "", ByRef op3 As String = "", ByRef op4 As String = "", ByRef op5 As String = "", ByRef op6 As String = "", ByRef op7 As String = "", ByRef op8 As String = "", ByRef op9 As String = "", ByRef op10 As String = "", ByRef op11 As String = "", ByRef op12 As String = "", ByRef op13 As String = "", ByRef op14 As String = "", ByRef op15 As String = "")
    Dim inst As MIRIRInstruction
    Dim blk As MIRBasicBlock Ptr
    inst.opcode = opcode
    inst.result = resultName
    inst.blockId = blockIdx
    inst.srcLoc = gMIRCurrentSource
    inst.flags = gMIRCurrentAstNodeId
    inst.operandCount = operandCount
    If operandCount > 0 Then inst.operands(0) = op0
    If operandCount > 1 Then inst.operands(1) = op1
    If operandCount > 2 Then inst.operands(2) = op2
    If operandCount > 3 Then inst.operands(3) = op3
    If operandCount > 4 Then inst.operands(4) = op4
    If operandCount > 5 Then inst.operands(5) = op5
    If operandCount > 6 Then inst.operands(6) = op6
    If operandCount > 7 Then inst.operands(7) = op7
    If operandCount > 8 Then inst.operands(8) = op8
    If operandCount > 9 Then inst.operands(9) = op9
    If operandCount > 10 Then inst.operands(10) = op10
    If operandCount > 11 Then inst.operands(11) = op11
    If operandCount > 12 Then inst.operands(12) = op12
    If operandCount > 13 Then inst.operands(13) = op13
    If operandCount > 14 Then inst.operands(14) = op14
    If operandCount > 15 Then inst.operands(15) = op15

    blk = @module.functions(fnIdx).blocks(blockIdx)
    Dim cnt As Integer
    cnt = blk->instructionCount
    If cnt = 0 Then
        ReDim blk->instructions(0)
    Else
        ReDim Preserve blk->instructions(cnt)
    End If
    blk->instructions(cnt) = inst
    blk->instructionCount = cnt + 1
End Sub

Private Function MIRNextTemp(ByRef seed As Integer) As String
    seed += 1
    Return "__t" & LTrim(Str(seed))
End Function

Private Function MIRCanonicalTypeName(ByRef rawType As String) As String
    Dim t As String
    t = UCase(Trim(rawType))

    Select Case t
    Case "SINGLE", "SNG", "SNGLE"
        Return "F32"
    Case "DOUBLE", "DBL", "DBLE"
        Return "F64"
    Case "EXTENDED", "EXT"
        Return "F80"
    End Select

    Return t
End Function

Private Function MIRIsFloatTypeName(ByRef rawType As String) As Integer
    Dim t As String
    t = MIRCanonicalTypeName(rawType)
    Return IIf(t = "F32" Or t = "F64" Or t = "F80", 1, 0)
End Function

Private Function MIRFindNearestDeclaredType(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal fromNodeIdx As Integer, ByRef varNameRaw As String, ByRef typeOut As String) As Integer
    typeOut = ""

    Dim target As String
    target = UCase(Trim(varNameRaw))
    If target = "" Then Return 0

    Dim startIdx As Integer
    startIdx = fromNodeIdx
    If startIdx < 0 Or startIdx >= ps.ast.count Then startIdx = ps.ast.count - 1

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = startIdx To 0 Step -1
        Dim k As String
        k = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(i).kind))
        If k <> "DIM_DECL" And k <> "REDIM_DECL" And k <> "PARAM_DECL" Then Continue For
        If UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(i).value)) <> target Then Continue For

        Dim typeNode As Integer

' Extracted from code_generator.fbs for wave-14 modular split
' Scope: dll/ffi call planning and emitters

Private Sub X64CollectDllCallPlanNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer, ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext)
    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then Exit Sub

    If X64IsDllCallNode(ps, nodeIdx) <> 0 Then
        Dim argc As Integer
        argc = X64ChildCount(ps, nodeIdx)
        Dim argStartPos As Integer
        argStartPos = X64DllCallArgStartPos(ps, nodeIdx)

        If cg.ffiCallTotal >= 0 And cg.ffiCallTotal <= UBound(gX64FfiArgCounts) Then
            gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = argc - argStartPos
            If gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) < 0 Then gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = 0
            cg.ffiCallTotal += 1
        End If
    Else
        Dim nodeKind As String
        nodeKind = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind)
        Dim nodeValue As String
        nodeValue = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).value)
        If (nodeKind = "CALL_EXPR" Or nodeKind = "CALL_STMT" Or nodeKind = "CALL") And nodeValue = "CALL" Then
            Dim firstArg As Integer
            firstArg = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 0)
            If firstArg <> -1 Then
                Dim firstKind As String
                firstKind = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(firstArg).kind)
                Dim markerValue2 As String
                markerValue2 = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(firstArg).value)
                If (firstKind = "IDENT" Or firstKind = "KEYWORD_REF") And (markerValue2 = "DLL" Or markerValue2 = "API") Then
                    Dim argc2 As Integer
                    argc2 = X64ChildCount(ps, nodeIdx)
                    Dim argStartPos2 As Integer
                    argStartPos2 = X64DllCallArgStartPos(ps, nodeIdx)
                    If cg.ffiCallTotal >= 0 And cg.ffiCallTotal <= UBound(gX64FfiArgCounts) Then
                        gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = argc2 - argStartPos2
                        If gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) < 0 Then gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = 0
                        cg.ffiCallTotal += 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If

    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left <> -1 Then X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left, cg
    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right <> -1 Then X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right, cg

    Dim ch As Integer
    ch = ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).firstChild
    Do While ch <> -1
        X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, ch, cg
        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
    Loop
End Sub

Private Function X64CollectDllCallPlan(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer, ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext) As Integer
    cg.ffiCallTotal = 0
    cg.ffiCallEmitIndex = 0
    X64ResetFfiArgCounts
    X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, nodeIdx, cg
    Return 1
End Function

Private Sub X64EmitDllExterns(ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext)
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To cg.ffiCallTotal - 1
        Dim stubId As String
        stubId = X64ToStr(i + 1)
        X64EmitText cg, "extern __uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId

        Dim argIdx As Integer
        For argIdx = 1 To X64FfiArgCountAt(i)
            X64EmitText cg, "extern __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_" & X64ToStr(argIdx)
        Next argIdx
    Next i

    If cg.ffiCallTotal > 0 Then X64EmitText cg, ""
End Sub

Private Sub X64EmitFfiSupport(ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext)
    If cg.ffiCallTotal <= 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To cg.ffiCallTotal - 1
        Dim stubId As String
        stubId = X64ToStr(i + 1)

        X64EmitText cg, "global __uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId
        X64EmitText cg, "global __uxb_ffi_symptr_" & stubId
        Dim globalArgIdx As Integer
        For globalArgIdx = 1 To X64FfiArgCountAt(i)
            X64EmitText cg, "global __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_" & X64ToStr(globalArgIdx)
        Next globalArgIdx
        X64EmitText cg, "__uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId & ":"
        X64EmitText cg, "    sub rsp, 40"

        If X64FfiArgCountAt(i) >= 1 Then
            X64EmitText cg, "    mov rcx, qword [rel __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_1]"
            X64EmitText cg, "    movq xmm0, rcx"
        End If
        If X64FfiArgCountAt(i) >= 2 Then
            X64EmitText cg, "    mov rdx, qword [rel __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_2]"
            X64EmitText cg, "    movq xmm1, rdx"
        End If
        If X64FfiArgCountAt(i) >= 3 Then
            X64EmitText cg, "    mov r8, qword [rel __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_3]"
            X64EmitText cg, "    movq xmm2, r8"
        End If
        If X64FfiArgCountAt(i) >= 4 Then
            X64EmitText cg, "    mov r9, qword [rel __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_4]"
            X64EmitText cg, "    movq xmm3, r9"
        End If

        Dim argIdx As Integer
        For argIdx = 5 To X64FfiArgCountAt(i)
            Dim slotOffset As Integer
            slotOffset = 32 + ((argIdx - 5) * 8)
            X64EmitText cg, "    mov rax, qword [rel __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_" & X64ToStr(argIdx) & "]"
            X64EmitText cg, "    mov qword [rsp + " & X64ToStr(slotOffset) & "], rax"
        Next argIdx

        X64EmitText cg, "    mov r11, qword [rel __uxb_ffi_symptr_" & stubId & "]"
        X64EmitText cg, "    test r11, r11"
        X64EmitText cg, "    jnz __uxb_ffi_call_" & stubId
        X64EmitText cg, "    xor rax, rax"
        X64EmitText cg, "    add rsp, 40"
        X64EmitText cg, "    ret"
        X64EmitText cg, "__uxb_ffi_call_" & stubId & ":"
        X64EmitText cg, "    call r11"
        X64EmitText cg, "    add rsp, 40"
        X64EmitText cg, "    ret"
        X64EmitText cg, ""

        X64EmitData cg, "__uxb_ffi_symptr_" & stubId & ": dq 0"
        For argIdx = 1 To X64FfiArgCountAt(i)
            X64EmitData cg, "__uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_" & X64ToStr(argIdx) & ": dq 0"
        Next argIdx
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function X64EmitDllCallNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer, ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    If cg.ffiCallEmitIndex >= cg.ffiCallTotal Then
        errText = "x64-codegen: ffi call emit index overflow"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim stubIdNum As Integer
    stubIdNum = cg.ffiCallEmitIndex + 1
    Dim stubId As String
    stubId = X64ToStr(stubIdNum)

    Dim argc As Integer
    argc = X64ChildCount(ps, nodeIdx)

    Dim argStartPos As Integer
    argStartPos = X64DllCallArgStartPos(ps, nodeIdx)

    Dim argCount As Integer
    argCount = argc - argStartPos
    If argCount < 0 Then argCount = 0

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        Dim argNode As Integer
        argNode = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, argStartPos + i)
        If argNode = -1 Then Continue For

        Dim argFloatKind As Integer
        argFloatKind = X64InferFloatKindOfExpr(ps, argNode, cg)

        If argFloatKind = UXF_F32 Or argFloatKind = UXF_F64 Then
            ' C/varargs uyumu: float arglar qword-double bit pattern olarak saklanir.
            If X64EmitExprToXmm0As(ps, argNode, cg, UXF_F64, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
            X64EmitText cg, "    movq rax, xmm0"
        Else
            If X64EmitExprToRax(ps, argNode, cg, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        End If

        X64EmitText cg, "    mov [rel __uxb_ffi_arg_" & stubId & "_" & X64ToStr(i + 1) & "], rax"
    Next i

    X64EmitText cg, "    call __uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId
    cg.ffiCallEmitIndex += 1
    Return 1
End Function


    MIRX64Emit cg, "extern " & runtimeSymbol
    If Trim(argReg) <> "" Then
        MIRX64Emit cg, "    mov rcx, " & argReg
    End If
    MIRX64Emit cg, "    sub rsp, 32"
    MIRX64Emit cg, "    call " & runtimeSymbol
    MIRX64Emit cg, "    add rsp, 32"
    Return 1
End Function

Function MIRX64EmitWin64Call0(ByRef cg As MIRX64Context, ByRef targetSymbol As String, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    errText = ""
    If Trim(targetSymbol) = "" Then
        errText = "UXB_FFI_TARGET_SYMBOL_MISSING"
        MIRX64AddError cg, errText
        Return 0
    End If
    MIRX64Emit cg, "extern " & targetSymbol
    MIRX64Emit cg, "    sub rsp, 32"
    MIRX64Emit cg, "    call " & targetSymbol
    MIRX64Emit cg, "    add rsp, 32"
    Return 1
End Function

Private Function X64IsDllCallNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer) As Integer
    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then Return 0

    Dim nodeKind As String
    nodeKind = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind)
    If nodeKind <> "CALL_STMT" And nodeKind <> "CALL_EXPR" And nodeKind <> "CALL" Then Return 0

    Dim calleeName As String
    calleeName = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).value)
    If calleeName = "DLL" Then Return 1
    If calleeName = "API" Then Return 1

    If calleeName = "CALL" Then
        Dim firstArg As Integer
        firstArg = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 0)
        If firstArg <> -1 Then
            Dim firstKind As String
            firstKind = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(firstArg).kind)
            If firstKind = "IDENT" Or firstKind = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
                Dim markerValue As String
                markerValue = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(firstArg).value)
                Return IIf(markerValue = "DLL" Or markerValue = "API", 1, 0)
            End If
        End If
    End If

    Return 0
End Function

Private Function X64DllCallArgStartPos(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer) As Integer
    Dim argc As Integer
    argc = X64ChildCount(ps, nodeIdx)

    Dim callName As String
    callName = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).value)

    Dim argStartPos As Integer
    If callName = "CALL" Then
        argStartPos = 4
        If argc >= 6 Then
            Dim convNode As Integer
            convNode = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 4)
            If convNode <> -1 And X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).kind) = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
                Dim convCandidate As String
                convCandidate = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).value)
                If convCandidate = "CDECL" Or convCandidate = "STDCALL" Then argStartPos = 5
            End If
        End If
    Else
        argStartPos = 3
        If argc >= 5 Then
            Dim convNode As Integer
            convNode = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 3)
            If convNode <> -1 And X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).kind) = "KEYWORD_REF" Then
                Dim convCandidate As String
                convCandidate = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(convNode).value)
                If convCandidate = "CDECL" Or convCandidate = "STDCALL" Then argStartPos = 4
            End If
        End If
    End If

    Dim typeListNode As Integer
    typeListNode = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, argStartPos)
    If typeListNode <> -1 And X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).kind) = "STRING" Then
        If Instr(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).value, ",") > 0 Then argStartPos += 1
    End If

    Return argStartPos
End Function

Type MIRIRInstruction

    blockId As Integer  ' Which block this belongs to
    srcLoc As SourceLocation
    comment As String
    flags As Integer
End Type

    successorCount As Integer

    isExit As Integer   ' 1 if this is exit block
End Type

Private Function MIRLowerTopLevelStmtErrorFlow(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal stmtIdx As Integer, ByRef module As MIRModule, ByVal fnIdx As Integer, ByRef blkIdx As Integer, ByRef tempSeed As Integer, ByRef handledOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    MIRSetCurrentSourceFromAstNode ps, stmtIdx
    handledOut = 1
    If stmtIdx < 0 Or stmtIdx >= ps.ast.count Then handledOut = 0: Return 1

    Dim st As ASTNode
    st = ps.ast.nodes(stmtIdx)
    Dim k As String
    k = UCase(st.kind)

    Select Case k
    Case "THROW_STMT"
        Dim throwExprIdx As Integer
        throwExprIdx = MIRChildAt(ps, stmtIdx, 0)
        If throwExprIdx = -1 Then errText = "mir: THROW expression missing": Return 0

        Dim throwName As String
        If MIRLowerExprNode(ps, throwExprIdx, module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed, throwName, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_STORE_VAR, "__mir_exc_value", 1, throwName
        MIRMarkExceptionActive module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed

        Dim throwTarget As Integer
        If MIRCurrentTryTarget(throwTarget) <> 0 Then
            MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_JMP, "", 1, LTrim(Str(throwTarget))
            Return 1
        End If
        MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_RET, "", 1, "__result__"
        Return 1

    End Select
End Function

Private Sub MIREmitTryEscalationCheck(ByRef module As MIRModule, ByVal fnIdx As Integer, ByRef blkIdx As Integer, ByRef tempSeed As Integer)
    Dim tryTarget As Integer
    If MIRCurrentTryTarget(tryTarget) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim noExcBlock As Integer
    noExcBlock = MIRAllocBlock(module, fnIdx)

    Dim excActiveName As String
    excActiveName = MIRNextTemp(tempSeed)
    MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_LOAD_VAR, excActiveName, 1, "__mir_exc_active"
    MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_JZ, "", 2, excActiveName, LTrim(Str(noExcBlock))
    MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_JMP, "", 1, LTrim(Str(tryTarget))
    blkIdx = noExcBlock
End Sub

Private Function MIRLowerTopLevelStmtCallDispatch(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal stmtIdx As Integer, ByRef module As MIRModule, ByVal fnIdx As Integer, ByRef blkIdx As Integer, ByRef tempSeed As Integer, ByRef handledOut As Integer, ByRef errText As String) As Integer
    MIRSetCurrentSourceFromAstNode ps, stmtIdx
    handledOut = 1
    If stmtIdx < 0 Or stmtIdx >= ps.ast.count Then handledOut = 0: Return 1

    Dim st As ASTNode
    st = ps.ast.nodes(stmtIdx)
    Dim k As String
    k = UCase(st.kind)
    If k <> "CALL_STMT" Then handledOut = 0: Return 1

    Dim stmtCallName As String
    Dim skipFirstStmtCallChild As Integer
    Dim injectStmtReceiver As Integer
    Dim stmtReceiverVar As String
    stmtCallName = UCase(Trim(st.value))
    skipFirstStmtCallChild = 0
    injectStmtReceiver = 0
    stmtReceiverVar = ""

    If st.left <> -1 And InStr(stmtCallName, ".") > 0 Then
        Dim stmtMethodTemp As String
        If MIRAppendMethodCallInstruction(ps, stmtIdx, module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed, stmtMethodTemp, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
        MIREmitTryEscalationCheck module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed
        Return 1
    End If

    If InStr(stmtCallName, ".") > 0 Then
        Dim loweredStmtCallName As String
        If MIRResolveMethodCallRoute(ps, stmtIdx, stmtCallName, loweredStmtCallName, injectStmtReceiver, stmtReceiverVar) <> 0 Then
            stmtCallName = loweredStmtCallName
        End If
    End If

    If stmtCallName = "CALL" And st.firstChild <> -1 Then
        Dim stmtMarkerKind As String
        Dim stmtMarkerValue As String
        stmtMarkerKind = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(st.firstChild).kind))
        stmtMarkerValue = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(st.firstChild).value))
        If (stmtMarkerKind = "IDENT" Or stmtMarkerKind = "KEYWORD_REF") And stmtMarkerValue = "DLL" Then
            stmtCallName = "DLL"
            skipFirstStmtCallChild = 1
        ElseIf (stmtMarkerKind = "IDENT" Or stmtMarkerKind = "KEYWORD_REF") And stmtMarkerValue = "API" Then
            stmtCallName = "API"
            skipFirstStmtCallChild = 1
        End If
    End If

    Dim argCount As Integer
    argCount = MIRChildCount(ps, stmtIdx)
    If skipFirstStmtCallChild <> 0 And argCount > 0 Then argCount -= 1
    If injectStmtReceiver <> 0 Then argCount += 1

    Dim c1 As String, c2 As String, c3 As String, c4 As String, c5 As String, c6 As String, c7 As String, c8 As String
    Dim c9 As String, c10 As String, c11 As String, c12 As String, c13 As String, c14 As String, c15 As String, c16 As String
    c1 = stmtCallName
    Dim cPos As Integer
    Dim cSlot As Integer
    cSlot = 2

    If injectStmtReceiver <> 0 Then
        c2 = stmtReceiverVar
        cSlot = 3
    End If

    Dim rawArgCount As Integer
    rawArgCount = MIRChildCount(ps, stmtIdx)
    For cPos = 0 To rawArgCount - 1
        If skipFirstStmtCallChild <> 0 And cPos = 0 Then Continue For
        Dim cArgNode As Integer
        cArgNode = MIRChildAt(ps, stmtIdx, cPos)
        If cArgNode = -1 Then errText = "mir: CALL argument missing": Return 0

        Dim cArgName As String
        If MIRLowerExprNode(ps, cArgNode, module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed, cArgName, errText) = 0 Then Return 0

        Select Case cSlot

        MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_JMP, "", 1, LTrim(Str(endBlock))

        blkIdx = endBlock
        valueNameOut = resultTemp
        Return 1
    End If

    If k = "CALL_EXPR" Then
        Dim callName As String
        Dim skipFirstCallChild As Integer
        Dim injectReceiver As Integer
        Dim receiverVar As String
        callName = UCase(Trim(n.value))
        skipFirstCallChild = 0
        injectReceiver = 0
        receiverVar = ""

        If callName = "OFFSETOF" Then
            If MIRChildCount(ps, nodeIdx) <> 2 Then
                errText = "mir: OFFSETOF expects two arguments"
                Return 0
            End If

            Dim typeNode As Integer
            Dim pathNode As Integer
            typeNode = MIRChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 0)
            pathNode = MIRChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 1)
            If typeNode = -1 Or pathNode = -1 Then
                errText = "mir: OFFSETOF arguments missing"
                Return 0
            End If

            Dim typeKind As String
            Dim pathKind As String
            typeKind = UCase(ps.ast.nodes(typeNode).kind)
            pathKind = UCase(ps.ast.nodes(pathNode).kind)
            If typeKind <> "IDENT" And typeKind <> "KEYWORD_REF" Then
                errText = "mir: OFFSETOF first argument must be type name"
                Return 0
            End If
            If pathKind <> "STRING" Then
                errText = "mir: OFFSETOF second argument must be string path"
                Return 0
            End If

            Dim offValue As Integer
            Dim offErr As String
            If TypeLayoutOffsetOf(ps, ps.ast.nodes(typeNode).value, ps.ast.nodes(pathNode).value, offValue, offErr) = 0 Then
                errText = "mir: OFFSETOF resolve failed " & offErr
                Return 0
            End If

            Dim offTemp As String
            offTemp = MIRNextTemp(tempSeed)
            MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_LOAD_CONST, offTemp, 1, LTrim(Str(offValue))
            valueNameOut = offTemp
            Return 1
        End If

        If InStr(callName, ".") = 0 And MIRIsKnownArray(module, callName) <> 0 And MIRChildCount(ps, nodeIdx) = 1 Then
            Dim indexName As String
            Dim indexNode As Integer
            Dim loadTemp As String
            indexNode = MIRChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 0)
            If indexNode = -1 Then
                errText = "mir: indexed load index missing"
                Return 0
            End If
            If MIRLowerExprNode(ps, indexNode, module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed, indexName, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
            loadTemp = MIRNextTemp(tempSeed)
            MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_LOAD_INDEXED, loadTemp, 2, callName, indexName
            valueNameOut = loadTemp
            Return 1
        End If

        If n.left <> -1 And InStr(callName, ".") > 0 Then
            Dim methodCallTemp As String
            If MIRAppendMethodCallInstruction(ps, nodeIdx, module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed, methodCallTemp, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
            valueNameOut = methodCallTemp
            Return 1
        End If

        If InStr(callName, ".") > 0 Then
            Dim loweredCallName As String
            If MIRResolveMethodCallRoute(ps, nodeIdx, callName, loweredCallName, injectReceiver, receiverVar) <> 0 Then
                callName = loweredCallName
            End If
        End If

        If callName = "CALL" And n.firstChild <> -1 Then
            Dim markerKind As String
            Dim markerValue As String
            markerKind = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(n.firstChild).kind))
            markerValue = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(n.firstChild).value))
            If (markerKind = "IDENT" Or markerKind = "KEYWORD_REF") And markerValue = "DLL" Then
                callName = "DLL"
                skipFirstCallChild = 1
            ElseIf (markerKind = "IDENT" Or markerKind = "KEYWORD_REF") And markerValue = "API" Then
                callName = "API"
                skipFirstCallChild = 1
            End If
        End If

        Dim childCount As Integer
        childCount = 0
        Dim ch As Integer
        ch = n.firstChild
        Do While ch <> -1
            If skipFirstCallChild = 0 Or ch <> n.firstChild Then childCount += 1
            ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
        Loop

        If injectReceiver <> 0 Then childCount += 1

        Dim t As String
        t = MIRNextTemp(tempSeed)

        If childCount = 0 Then
            MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_CALL, t, 1, callName
        Else
            Dim a1 As String, a2 As String, a3 As String, a4 As String, a5 As String, a6 As String, a7 As String, a8 As String
            Dim a9 As String, a10 As String, a11 As String, a12 As String, a13 As String, a14 As String, a15 As String, a16 As String
            a1 = callName
            Dim idx As Integer
            idx = 2

            If injectReceiver <> 0 Then
                a2 = receiverVar
                idx = 3
            End If

            ch = n.firstChild
            Do While ch <> -1
                If skipFirstCallChild <> 0 And ch = n.firstChild Then
                    ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
                    Continue Do
                End If
                Dim argName As String
                Dim argKind As String
                argKind = UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(ch).kind))
                If (callName = "LEN" Or callName = "ASC" Or callName = "VAL") And argKind = "STRING" Then
                    argName = Chr(34) & ps.ast.nodes(ch).value & Chr(34)
                Else
                    If MIRLowerExprNode(ps, ch, module, fnIdx, blkIdx, tempSeed, argName, errText) = 0 Then Return 0
                End If
                Select Case idx
                Case 2: a2 = argName
                Case 3: a3 = argName
                Case 4: a4 = argName
                Case 5: a5 = argName
                Case 6: a6 = argName
                Case 7: a7 = argName
                Case 8: a8 = argName
                Case 9: a9 = argName
                Case 10: a10 = argName
                Case 11: a11 = argName
                Case 12: a12 = argName
                Case 13: a13 = argName
                Case 14: a14 = argName
                Case 15: a15 = argName
                Case 16: a16 = argName
                Case Else
                    errText = "mir: CALL arguman sayisi limiti asildi"
                    Return 0
                End Select
                idx += 1
                ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
            Loop

            MIRAppendInstruction module, fnIdx, blkIdx, MIR_OP_CALL, t, childCount + 1, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9, a10, a11, a12, a13, a14, a15, a16
        End If

        valueNameOut = t
        Return 1
    End If

    If k = "FIELD_EXPR" Then
        Dim fieldPath As String
        If MIRBuildFieldPath(ps, nodeIdx, fieldPath, errText) = 0 Then Return 0

        Dim dotPos As Integer
        dotPos = InStr(fieldPath, ".")
        If dotPos > 1 Then
            Dim rootName As String
            Dim memberPath As String
Patching x64 helpers and stub
bash -lc set -e
P=/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb

mkdir -p "$P/src/codegen/x64/helpers" "$P/src/codegen/x64/stmt_emitters" "$P/src/semantic"

cp "$B/src/codegen/x64/stmt_emitters/x64_codegen_stmt_dll_ffi.fbs" "$P/src/codegen/x64/stmt_emitters/x64_codegen_stmt_dll_ffi.fbs"

python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/codegen/x64/helpers/x64_codegen_runtime_emit_helpers.fbs')
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
old='''    Dim typeListNode As Integer
    typeListNode = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, argStartPos)
    If typeListNode <> -1 And X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).kind) = "STRING" Then
        If Instr(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).value, ",") > 0 Then argStartPos += 1
    End If
'''
new='''    Dim typeListNode As Integer
    typeListNode = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, argStartPos)
    If typeListNode <> -1 And X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).kind) = "STRING" Then
        Dim typeListText As String
        Dim typeListUpper As String
        typeListText = Trim(ps.ast.nodes(typeListNode).value)
        typeListUpper = X64Upper(typeListText)
        If Instr(typeListText, ",") > 0 Then
            argStartPos += 1
        Else
            ' Tek parametreli DLL imzalarý da tür listesi taþýr: "PTR", "F64" vb.
            Select Case typeListUpper
            Case "I32", "U32", "I64", "U64", "F32", "F64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF"
                argStartPos += 1
            End Select
        End If
    End If
'''
if old not in s: raise SystemExit('helper pattern missing')
p.write_text(s.replace(old,new),encoding='utf-8')

p=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/codegen/x64/stmt_emitters/x64_codegen_stmt_dll_ffi.fbs')
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
old='''        X64EmitText cg, "__uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId & ":"
        X64EmitText cg, "    sub rsp, 40"
'''
new='''        X64EmitText cg, "__uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId & ":"

        ' Win64 ABI: 32 bayt shadow space + 5. ve sonraki argümanlar için
        ' stack slotlarý. CALL öncesinde RSP 16-bayt hizalý tutulur.
        Dim stackArgCount As Integer
        Dim ffiStackBytes As Integer
        stackArgCount = X64FfiArgCountAt(i) - 4
        If stackArgCount < 0 Then stackArgCount = 0
        ffiStackBytes = 32 + (stackArgCount * 8)
        If (ffiStackBytes Mod 16) = 0 Then ffiStackBytes += 8
        X64EmitText cg, "    sub rsp, " & X64ToStr(ffiStackBytes)
'''
if old not in s: raise SystemExit('stub start pattern missing')
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''        X64EmitText cg, "    add rsp, 40"
        X64EmitText cg, "    ret"
        X64EmitText cg, "__uxb_ffi_call_" & stubId & ":"
        X64EmitText cg, "    call r11"
        X64EmitText cg, "    add rsp, 40"
''','''        X64EmitText cg, "    add rsp, " & X64ToStr(ffiStackBytes)
        X64EmitText cg, "    ret"
        X64EmitText cg, "__uxb_ffi_call_" & stubId & ":"
        X64EmitText cg, "    call r11"
        X64EmitText cg, "    add rsp, " & X64ToStr(ffiStackBytes)
''')
p.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')
PY
Patching MIR evaluator with FFI bridge
bash -lc cat > /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/semantic/mir_ffi_bridge10.fbs <<'EOF'
#ifndef UXB_MIR_FFI_BRIDGE10_FBS
#define UXB_MIR_FFI_BRIDGE10_FBS

' MIR interpreter için isteðe baðlý libffi köprüsü.
' uxffi.dll yoksa mevcut doðrudan 0..8 tamsayý/pointer ve 0..4 karma
' çaðrý yollarý kullanýlmaya devam eder. Köprü bulunduðunda 0..10 karma
' argüman ve tür bilgili dönüþ sonucu MIRValue içine taþýnýr.

Const UXB_MIR_FFI_BRIDGE10_ABI_VERSION As UInteger = 1
Const UXB_MIR_FFI_BRIDGE10_MAX_ARGS As Integer = 10

Type UXBMIRFfiBridge10Request
    abiVersion As UInteger
    structSize As UInteger
    argCount As UInteger
    flags As UInteger
    dllName As ZString Ptr
    symbolName As ZString Ptr
    returnType As ZString Ptr
    argTypesCsv As ZString Ptr
    u64Args As ULongInt Ptr
    f64Args As Double Ptr
    f64Mask As ULongInt
    resultU64 As ULongInt
    resultF64 As Double
    resultKind As UInteger
    statusCode As Integer
    errorText As ZString * 512
End Type

Type UXBMIRFfiBridge10InvokeFn As Function Cdecl(ByVal requestPtr As UXBMIRFfiBridge10Request Ptr) As Integer

Dim Shared gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Tried As Integer
Dim Shared gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Module As Any Ptr
Dim Shared gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Invoke As UXBMIRFfiBridge10InvokeFn

Private Sub MIRFfiBridge10LoadIfNeeded()
    If gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Tried <> 0 Then Exit Sub
    gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Tried = 1
    gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Module = MIRLoadLibraryA(StrPtr("uxffi.dll"))
    If gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Module = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim procAddr As Any Ptr
    procAddr = MIRGetProcAddress(gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Module, StrPtr("uxffi_invoke_v1"))
    If procAddr = 0 Then
        gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Module = 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Invoke = procAddr
End Sub

Private Function MIRFfiBridge10Invoke( _
    ByRef dllName As String, _
    ByRef symbolName As String, _
    ByRef returnType As String, _
    argTypes() As String, _
    ByVal argCount As Integer, _
    u64Args() As ULongInt, _
    f64Args() As Double, _
    ByVal f64Mask As Integer, _
    ByRef valueOut As MIRValue, _
    ByRef invokedOut As Integer, _
    ByRef errMsg As String _
) As Integer
    invokedOut = 0
    If argCount < 0 Or argCount > UXB_MIR_FFI_BRIDGE10_MAX_ARGS Then
        errMsg = "MIR: uxffi bridge en fazla 10 arguman destekler"
        Return 0
    End If

    MIRFfiBridge10LoadIfNeeded
    If gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Module = 0 Or gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Invoke = 0 Then Return 1

    Dim csv As String
    csv = ""
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
        If i > 0 Then csv &= ","
        csv &= UCase(Trim(argTypes(i)))
    Next i

    Dim req As UXBMIRFfiBridge10Request
    req.abiVersion = UXB_MIR_FFI_BRIDGE10_ABI_VERSION
    req.structSize = SizeOf(UXBMIRFfiBridge10Request)
    req.argCount = argCount
    req.flags = 0
    req.dllName = StrPtr(dllName)
    req.symbolName = StrPtr(symbolName)
    req.returnType = StrPtr(returnType)
    req.argTypesCsv = StrPtr(csv)
    If argCount > 0 Then
        req.u64Args = @u64Args(0)
        req.f64Args = @f64Args(0)
    Else
        req.u64Args = 0
        req.f64Args = 0
    End If
    req.f64Mask = CULngInt(f64Mask)

    Dim rc As Integer
    rc = gUXBMIRFfiBridge10Invoke(@req)
    If rc = 0 Or req.statusCode <> 0 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: uxffi bridge failed"
        If req.errorText <> "" Then errMsg &= ": " & req.errorText
        Return 0
    End If

    valueOut.valueType = "INTEGER"
    valueOut.intValue = 0
    valueOut.floatValue = 0
    valueOut.stringValue = ""
    valueOut.arrayId = -1
    valueOut.arrayLower = 0
    valueOut.arraySize = 0

    Dim retUpper As String
    retUpper = UCase(Trim(returnType))
    Select Case retUpper
    Case "F32", "F64"
        valueOut.valueType = "FLOAT"
        valueOut.floatValue = req.resultF64
    Case "I32"
        valueOut.intValue = CLngInt(CInt(req.resultU64))
    Case "U32"
        valueOut.intValue = CLngInt(CUInt(req.resultU64))
    Case "I64", "U64", "PTR", "STRPTR", "BYREF"
        valueOut.intValue = CLngInt(req.resultU64)
    Case "VOID"
        valueOut.intValue = 0
    Case Else
        errMsg = "MIR: uxffi bridge invalid return type " & returnType
        Return 0
    End Select

    invokedOut = 1
    Return 1
End Function

#endif
EOF
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/semantic/mir_evaluator.fbs')
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
needle='#include once "mir_evaluator_memory_helpers.fbs"\n'
if needle not in s: raise SystemExit('include needle missing')
s=s.replace(needle, needle+'#include once "mir_ffi_bridge10.fbs"\n',1)
s=s.replace('''    If runtimeArgCount > 8 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL en fazla 8 arguman destekler"
        Return 0
    End If
''','''    If runtimeArgCount > 10 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL en fazla 10 arguman destekler"
        Return 0
    End If
''',1)
old_load='''    Dim h As Any Ptr
    h = MIRLoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If h = 0 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL LoadLibrary failed " & dllName
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim p As Any Ptr
    p = MIRGetProcAddress(h, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If p = 0 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL GetProcAddress failed " & symbolName
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 7) As ULongInt
    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 7) As Double
    Dim keepAlive(0 To 7) As String
'''
new_arrays='''    Dim argValuesU64(0 To 9) As ULongInt
    Dim argValuesF64(0 To 9) As Double
    Dim keepAlive(0 To 9) As String
'''
if old_load not in s: raise SystemExit('old load block missing')
s=s.replace(old_load,new_arrays,1)
needle2='''    Next i

    Dim rv As ULongInt
    If SizeOf(Any Ptr) = 4 Then
'''
insert='''    Next i

    ' uxffi.dll mevcutsa MIR interpreter 0..10 karma tür argümanýný ve
    ' F64/I64/PTR dönüþünü kayýpsýz MIRValue biçiminde alýr.
    Dim bridgeInvoked As Integer
    Dim bridgeStatus As Integer
    bridgeStatus = MIRFfiBridge10Invoke(dllName, symbolName, retType, argTypes(), runtimeArgCount, argValuesU64(), argValuesF64(), argF64Mask, ret, bridgeInvoked, errMsg)
    If bridgeStatus = 0 Then Return 0
    If bridgeInvoked <> 0 Then Return 1

    ' Köprü kurulmamýþsa eski doðrudan yollar korunur.
    If runtimeArgCount > 8 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL 9..10 arguman optional uxffi.dll gerektirir"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim h As Any Ptr
    h = MIRLoadLibraryA(StrPtr(dllName))
    If h = 0 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL LoadLibrary failed " & dllName
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim p As Any Ptr
    p = MIRGetProcAddress(h, StrPtr(symbolName))
    If p = 0 Then
        errMsg = "MIR: CALL DLL GetProcAddress failed " & symbolName
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim rv As ULongInt
    If SizeOf(Any Ptr) = 4 Then
'''
if needle2 not in s: raise SystemExit('insertion needle missing')
s=s.replace(needle2,insert,1)
p.write_text(s,encoding='utf-8')
PY

Ýnceleme için FFI makrolarý ve türleri
bash -lc cat /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/runtime_ext/uxffi/uxffi_smoke_target.c
#include <stdint.h>
#if defined(_WIN32)
#define UXFFI_SMOKE_API __declspec(dllexport)
#else
#define UXFFI_SMOKE_API
#endif

UXFFI_SMOKE_API int32_t uxffi_sum8_i32(
    int32_t a1, int32_t a2, int32_t a3, int32_t a4,
    int32_t a5, int32_t a6, int32_t a7, int32_t a8) {
    return a1+a2+a3+a4+a5+a6+a7+a8;
}

UXFFI_SMOKE_API int32_t uxffi_sum10_i32(
    int32_t a1, int32_t a2, int32_t a3, int32_t a4, int32_t a5,
    int32_t a6, int32_t a7, int32_t a8, int32_t a9, int32_t a10) {
    return a1+a2+a3+a4+a5+a6+a7+a8+a9+a10;
}

UXFFI_SMOKE_API int32_t uxffi_mixed10(
    int32_t a1, double a2, int64_t a3, double a4, uint32_t a5,
    int32_t a6, double a7, int64_t a8, double a9, uint32_t a10) {
    return a1 + (int32_t)a2 + (int32_t)a3 + (int32_t)a4 + (int32_t)a5 +
           a6 + (int32_t)a7 + (int32_t)a8 + (int32_t)a9 + (int32_t)a10;
}
Building C stubs for Windows and FFI
bash -lc set -e
STUB=/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/c_stubs
rm -rf "$STUB" && mkdir -p "$STUB"
cat > "$STUB/windows.h" <<'EOF'
#ifndef WINDOWS_H_STUB
#define WINDOWS_H_STUB
#include <stdint.h>
#define MAX_PATH 260
typedef void *HMODULE;
typedef void *FARPROC;
HMODULE LoadLibraryA(const char *name);
FARPROC GetProcAddress(HMODULE module, const char *name);
#endif
EOF
cat > "$STUB/ffi.h" <<'EOF'
#ifndef FFI_H_STUB
#define FFI_H_STUB
#include <stddef.h>
typedef struct { int dummy; } ffi_type;
typedef struct { int dummy; } ffi_cif;
typedef enum { FFI_OK = 0 } ffi_status;
extern ffi_type ffi_type_sint32, ffi_type_uint32, ffi_type_sint64, ffi_type_uint64, ffi_type_double, ffi_type_pointer, ffi_type_void;
#define FFI_DEFAULT_ABI 0
#define FFI_FN(x) ((void (*)(void))(x))
ffi_status ffi_prep_cif(ffi_cif *cif, int abi, unsigned int nargs, ffi_type *rtype, ffi_type **atypes);
void ffi_call(ffi_cif *cif, void (*fn)(void), void *rvalue, void **avalue);
#endif
EOF

--- PS
param(
    [string]$ProjectRoot = "",
    [switch]$BuildCore,
    [switch]$BuildAll,
    [switch]$AuditOnly
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$patchRoot = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ProjectRoot)) {
    $ProjectRoot = (Get-Location).Path
}

# Accept either the repository parent (...\UXBc) or the uxb directory itself.
if ((Test-Path (Join-Path $ProjectRoot "src")) -and
    (Test-Path (Join-Path $ProjectRoot "runtime_ext"))) {
    $uxbRoot = (Resolve-Path $ProjectRoot).Path
} else {
    $candidate = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "uxb"
    if (-not (Test-Path $candidate)) {
        throw "uxb klasoru bulunamadi: $candidate"
    }
    $uxbRoot = (Resolve-Path $candidate).Path
}

$payload = Join-Path $patchRoot "uxb"
if (-not (Test-Path $payload)) {
    throw "Yama payload klasoru bulunamadi: $payload"
}

$payloadFull = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($payload).TrimEnd('\')
$targetFull = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($uxbRoot).TrimEnd('\')
$alreadyOverlaid = $payloadFull -ieq $targetFull
$backup = $null

if (-not $alreadyOverlaid) {
    $stamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"
    $backup = Join-Path $uxbRoot "patch_backups\library_abi10_$stamp"
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $backup | Out-Null

    Get-ChildItem $payload -Recurse -File | ForEach-Object {
        $relative = $_.FullName.Substring($payload.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $destination = Join-Path $uxbRoot $relative
        if (Test-Path $destination) {
            $backupFile = Join-Path $backup $relative
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $backupFile) | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $destination $backupFile -Force
        }
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $destination) | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $_.FullName $destination -Force
    }
    Write-Host "PATCH FILES OK. Backup: $backup"
} else {
    Write-Host "PATCH FILES: ZIP doðrudan proje üzerine açýlmýþ; dosya kopyalama atlandý."
}

# Physical source locations remain unchanged to avoid breaking current includes.
# Canonical library paths are directory junctions.
$librariesRoot = Join-Path $uxbRoot "libraries"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $librariesRoot | Out-Null
$links = [ordered]@{
    native   = "runtime_ext"
    wrappers = "libsx"
    include  = "include\modules"
}
foreach ($name in $links.Keys) {
    $link = Join-Path $librariesRoot $name
    $target = Join-Path $uxbRoot $links[$name]
    if (-not (Test-Path $link)) {
        try {
            New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path $link -Target $target | Out-Null
        } catch {
            Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 (Join-Path $librariesRoot ($name + ".path")) $target
            Write-Warning "Junction kurulamadý; yol iþaretçisi yazýldý: $name.path"
        }
    }
}

$auditScript = Join-Path $uxbRoot "tools\library_tools\uxb_library_abi_audit.py"
if ($AuditOnly) {
    & python $auditScript
    exit $LASTEXITCODE
}
if ($BuildAll) {
    & (Join-Path $uxbRoot "compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1") -Tier all
    exit $LASTEXITCODE
}
if ($BuildCore) {
    & (Join-Path $uxbRoot "compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1") -Tier core
    exit $LASTEXITCODE
}

Write-Host "PATCH OK. ABI denetimi: python `"$auditScript`""
Write-Host "Çekirdek DLL üretimi: .\uxb\compiler\scripts\build_libraries.ps1 -Tier core"
--- build ps
param([ValidateSet("core","all")][string]$Tier="core",[string[]]$Library=@(),[switch]$StrictOptional,[switch]$NoBuild)
$ErrorActionPreference="Stop"
$repo=(Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\..\..")).Path
$args=@("uxb\tools\library_tools\uxb_library_build.py","--tier",$Tier)
foreach($n in $Library){$args += @("--library",$n)}
if($StrictOptional){$args += "--strict-optional"}
if($NoBuild){$args += "--no-build"}
Push-Location $repo
try { & python @args; exit $LASTEXITCODE } finally { Pop-Location }

 Private Function ValidateCallDllSemanticsForNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal callNode As Integer) As Integer
     If UCase(Trim(ps.ast.nodes(callNode).value)) <> "DLL" Then Return 1
 
+    ' CALL(DLL) has three/four metadata fields plus up to ten runtime arguments.
+    ' Keep validation independent from a fixed six-element scratch array.
+    Const UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS As Integer = 10
+    Const UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN As Integer = UXB_FFI_MAX_RUNTIME_ARGS + 5
+
     Dim argCount As Integer
-    Dim argIdx(5) As Integer
-    argIdx(0) = -1
-    argIdx(1) = -1
-    argIdx(2) = -1
-    argIdx(3) = -1
-    argIdx(4) = -1
-    argIdx(5) = -1
+    Dim argIdx(0 To UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN - 1) As Integer
+    Dim initIdx As Integer
+    For initIdx = 0 To UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN - 1
+        argIdx(initIdx) = -1
+    Next initIdx
 
     Dim ch As Integer
     ch = ps.ast.nodes(callNode).firstChild
     Do While ch <> -1
-        If argCount <= 5 Then argIdx(argCount) = ch
+        If argCount < UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then argIdx(argCount) = ch
         argCount += 1
         ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
     Loop
 
+    If argCount > UXB_FFI_MAX_CALL_CHILDREN Then
+        ps.lastError = "CALL(DLL): call metadata plus arguments exceed the ABI10 limit"
+        Return 0
+    End If
+
     If argCount < 4 Then

+' Extracted from code_generator.fbs for wave-14 modular split
+' Scope: dll/ffi call planning and emitters
+
+Private Sub X64CollectDllCallPlanNode(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer, ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext)
+    If nodeIdx < 0 Or nodeIdx >= ps.ast.count Then Exit Sub
+
+    If X64IsDllCallNode(ps, nodeIdx) <> 0 Then
+        Dim argc As Integer
+        argc = X64ChildCount(ps, nodeIdx)
+        Dim argStartPos As Integer
+        argStartPos = X64DllCallArgStartPos(ps, nodeIdx)
+
+        If cg.ffiCallTotal >= 0 And cg.ffiCallTotal <= UBound(gX64FfiArgCounts) Then
+            gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = argc - argStartPos
+            If gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) < 0 Then gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = 0
+            cg.ffiCallTotal += 1
+        End If
+    Else
+        Dim nodeKind As String
+        nodeKind = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).kind)
+        Dim nodeValue As String
+        nodeValue = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).value)
+        If (nodeKind = "CALL_EXPR" Or nodeKind = "CALL_STMT" Or nodeKind = "CALL") And nodeValue = "CALL" Then
+            Dim firstArg As Integer
+            firstArg = X64ChildAt(ps, nodeIdx, 0)
+            If firstArg <> -1 Then
+                Dim firstKind As String
+                firstKind = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(firstArg).kind)
+                Dim markerValue2 As String
+                markerValue2 = X64Upper(ps.ast.nodes(firstArg).value)
+                If (firstKind = "IDENT" Or firstKind = "KEYWORD_REF") And (markerValue2 = "DLL" Or markerValue2 = "API") Then
+                    Dim argc2 As Integer
+                    argc2 = X64ChildCount(ps, nodeIdx)
+                    Dim argStartPos2 As Integer
+                    argStartPos2 = X64DllCallArgStartPos(ps, nodeIdx)
+                    If cg.ffiCallTotal >= 0 And cg.ffiCallTotal <= UBound(gX64FfiArgCounts) Then
+                        gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = argc2 - argStartPos2
+                        If gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) < 0 Then gX64FfiArgCounts(cg.ffiCallTotal) = 0
+                        cg.ffiCallTotal += 1
+                    End If
+                End If
+            End If
+        End If
+    End If
+
+    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left <> -1 Then X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).left, cg
+    If ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right <> -1 Then X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).right, cg
+
+    Dim ch As Integer
+    ch = ps.ast.nodes(nodeIdx).firstChild
+    Do While ch <> -1
+        X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, ch, cg
+        ch = ps.ast.nodes(ch).nextSibling
+    Loop
+End Sub
+
+Private Function X64CollectDllCallPlan(ByRef ps As ParseState, ByVal nodeIdx As Integer, ByRef cg As X64CodegenContext) As Integer
+    cg.ffiCallTotal = 0
+    cg.ffiCallEmitIndex = 0
+    X64ResetFfiArgCounts
+    X64CollectDllCallPlanNode ps, nodeIdx, cg
+    Return 1
+End Function

+    For i = 0 To cg.ffiCallTotal - 1
+        Dim stubId As String
+        stubId = X64ToStr(i + 1)
+        X64EmitText cg, "extern __uxb_ffi_stub_" & stubId

Dahil dosyalarýnda INCLUDE anahtar kelimesi arama
bash -lc for f in /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/include/modules/uxmath.uxmh /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/include/modules/uxstats.uxmh; do echo ---; cat "$f"; done; grep -R -n '^INCLUDE "libsx' /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/base/include | head

Fixing x64 max check in code
bash -lc python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('/mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/codegen/x64/stmt_emitters/x64_codegen_stmt_dll_ffi.fbs')
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
old='''    Dim argCount As Integer
    argCount = argc - argStartPos
    If argCount < 0 Then argCount = 0

    Dim i As Integer
'''
new='''    Dim argCount As Integer
    argCount = argc - argStartPos
    If argCount < 0 Then argCount = 0
    If argCount > 10 Then
        errText = "x64-codegen: CALL(DLL) en fazla 10 runtime argumani destekler"
        Return 0
    End If

    Dim i As Integer
'''
if old not in s: raise SystemExit('max check needle missing')
p.write_text(s.replace(old,new,1),encoding='utf-8')
PY
grep -n -C 5 "en fazla 10" /mnt/data/uxb_patch_work/patchroot/uxb/src/codegen/x64/stmt_emitters/x64_codegen_stmt_dll_ffi.fbs
167-
168-    Dim argCount As Integer
169-    argCount = argc - argStartPos
170-    If argCount < 0 Then argCount = 0
171-    If argCount > 10 Then
172:        errText = "x64-codegen: CALL(DLL) en fazla 10 runtime argumani destekler"
173-        Return 0
174-    End If
175-
176-    Dim i As Integer
177-    For i = 0 To argCount - 1
